```js
// ---- Passport photos (kept on their own tab so student reads stay small and fast) ----
// Column C is filled in when the student changes their own photo: each student gets ONE change.
let photoIndex = { at: 0, regs: [], changed: [] };
async function photoRegs(ttlMs) {
  if (Date.now() - photoIndex.at > ttlMs) {
    const data = await call(`/values:batchGet?ranges=${range("A2:A", PHOTO_TAB)}&ranges=${range("C2:C", PHOTO_TAB)}`);
    const [a, c] = data.valueRanges || [];
    const regs = ((a && a.values) || []).map((r) => r[0] || "");
    const cv = (c && c.values) || [];
    photoIndex = { at: Date.now(), regs, changed: regs.map((_, i) => !!(cv[i] && cv[i][0])) };
  }
  return photoIndex.regs;
}

// Has this student already used their one photo change?
async function photoChanged(reg) {
  const regs = await photoRegs(30000);
  const i = regs.lastIndexOf(reg);
  return i >= 0 && photoIndex.changed[i];
}

const photoCache = new Map();

async function appendPhoto(reg, base64) {
  await call(
    `/values/${range("A:B", PHOTO_TAB)}:append?valueInputOption=RAW&insertDataOption=INSERT_ROWS`,
    { method: "POST", data: { values: [[reg, base64]] } }
  );
  photoIndex.at = 0;
  photoCache.delete(reg);
}

// Replace a student's photo (or add one if they never had one). The row is overwritten in place, and any
// extra rows for the same reg are left alone: getPhoto reads the LAST match, so replacing that one is enough.
// Returns "ok", or "already" when the student has used their one change. A student with no photo row yet
// gets one added, and that does not use up the change.
async function setPhoto(reg, base64, force = false) {
  const regs = await photoRegs(0); // always fresh: a stale index could overwrite the wrong row
  const i = regs.lastIndexOf(reg);
  if (i < 0) { await appendPhoto(reg, base64); return "ok"; }
  if (photoIndex.changed[i] && !force) return "already"; // force: a mentor asked this student to send a new photo
  const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  await call("/values:batchUpdate", {
    method: "POST",
    data: {
      valueInputOption: "RAW",
      data: [
        { range: `${quoteTab(PHOTO_TAB)}!B${i + 2}`, values: [[base64]] },
        { range: `${quoteTab(PHOTO_TAB)}!C${i + 2}`, values: [[today]] },
      ],
    },
  });
  photoIndex.changed[i] = true;
  photoCache.delete(reg);
  return "ok";
}

async function getPhoto(reg) {
  const hit = photoCache.get(reg);
  if (hit && Date.now() - hit.at < 10 * 60 * 1000) return hit.b64;

  let regs = await photoRegs(30000);
  let i = regs.lastIndexOf(reg);
  if (i < 0) {
    regs = await photoRegs(5000); // maybe a brand-new registration
    i = regs.lastIndexOf(reg);
  }
  if (i < 0) return null;

  const data = await call(`/values/${range(`B${i + 2}`, PHOTO_TAB)}`);
  const b64 = (data.values && data.values[0] && data.values[0][0]) || null;
  if (b64) {
    photoCache.set(reg, { b64, at: Date.now() });
    if (photoCache.size > 300) photoCache.delete(photoCache.keys().next().value);
  }
  return b64;
}

// ---- Signatures (their own tab, like photos): one row per student, the newest save replaces the old one ----
// The tab is made at start-up, but it is checked again here (once per run) so a start-up hiccup, or a tab
// someone deleted, can never stop a student saving their signature.
let sigReady = null;
function ensureSigTab() {
  if (!sigReady) {
    sigReady = (async () => {
      await ensureTab(SIG_TAB);
      await ensureHeaders(SIG_TAB, SIG_HEADERS, "B");
    })().catch((e) => { sigReady = null; throw e; });
  }
  return sigReady;
}

// Run a signature step; if Google refuses it, make sure the tab is there and try once more.
async function withSigTab(fn) {
  await ensureSigTab();
  try {
    return await fn();
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("Signature step failed, retrying once:", e && e.message);
    sigReady = null;
    await ensureSigTab();
    return fn();
  }
}

async function sigRow(reg) {
  const data = await call(`/values/${range("A2:A", SIG_TAB)}`);
  const regs = ((data && data.values) || []).map((r) => (r && r[0]) || "");
  const i = regs.lastIndexOf(reg);
  return i < 0 ? 0 : i + 2; // sheet row number, or 0 when there is none
}

function getSignature(reg) {
  return withSigTab(async () => {
    const row = await sigRow(reg);
    if (!row) return null;
    const data = await call(`/values/${range(`B${row}`, SIG_TAB)}`);
    return (data.values && data.values[0] && data.values[0][0]) || null;
  });
}

function setSignature(reg, base64) {
  return withSigTab(async () => {
    const row = await sigRow(reg);
    if (!row) {
      await call(
        `/values/${range("A:B", SIG_TAB)}:append?valueInputOption=RAW&insertDataOption=INSERT_ROWS`,
        { method: "POST", data: { values: [[reg, base64]] } }
      );
      return;
    }
    await call(`/values/${range(`B${row}`, SIG_TAB)}?valueInputOption=RAW`, { method: "PUT", data: { values: [[base64]] } });
  });
}

// ---- Generic tab access for the editor dashboard's "Sheets" view (the rules live in admin-sheets.js) ----
// The Photos tab is deliberately left out: it holds long image text that nobody should edit by hand.
const EDITOR_TABS = {
  students: { title: TAB, headers: HEADERS, lastCol: LAST_COL },
  codes: { title: CODE_TAB, headers: CODE_HEADERS, lastCol: CODE_LAST_COL },
  quiz: { title: QUIZ_TAB, headers: QUIZ_HEADERS, lastCol: QUIZ_LAST_COL },
  progress: { title: PROG_TAB, headers: PROG_HEADERS, lastCol: PROG_LAST_COL },
  links: { title: LINKS_TAB, headers: LINKS_HEADERS, lastCol: LINKS_LAST_COL },
  videos: { title: VIDEO_TAB, headers: VIDEO_HEADERS, lastCol: VIDEO_LAST_COL },
  // Internal only: never listed in the dashboard. Used to remove a deleted student's photo.
  photos: { title: PHOTO_TAB, headers: PHOTO_HEADERS, lastCol: "B" },
  // Internal only, like photos: signature pictures. Used to remove a deleted student's signature.
  signatures: { title: SIG_TAB, headers: SIG_HEADERS, lastCol: "B" },
  // Internal only: the one-time date of birth corrections students make themselves (old and new value, and when).
  dobchanges: { title: DOB_TAB, headers: DOB_HEADERS, lastCol: "D" },
  // Internal only: quiz codes. Managed through quizcodes.js and the "Quiz codes" dashboard tab, never the Database view.
  quizcodes: { title: QUIZCODE_TAB, headers: QUIZCODE_HEADERS, lastCol: QUIZCODE_LAST_COL },
  // Internal only, like photos: the dashboard team (password hashes). Managed through team.js, never the Database view.
  team: { title: TEAM_TAB, headers: TEAM_HEADERS, lastCol: TEAM_LAST_COL },
  // Internal only: custom roles. Managed through team.js, never the Database view.
  roles: { title: ROLES_TAB, headers: ROLES_HEADERS, lastCol: ROLES_LAST_COL },
};

function editorTab(key) {
  if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(EDITOR_TABS, key)) throw new Error("Unknown sheet: " + key);
  return EDITOR_TABS[key];
}

const cellText = (v) => (v == null ? "" : String(v));
// Google trims empty cells at the end of a row; give every row its full width again.
function fitRow(row, width) {
  const r = (row || []).slice(0, width).map(cellText);
  while (r.length < width) r.push("");
  return r;
}
const colLetter = (i) => String.fromCharCode(65 + i);
const quoteTab = (title) => "'" + title.replace(/'/g, "''") + "'";

// Forget whatever the website has remembered about a tab, so the next read sees the change.
function invalidate(key) {
  if (key === "students") cache.at = 0;
  else if (key === "quiz") quizCache.at = 0;
  else if (key === "progress") progCache.at = 0;
  else if (key === "links") linksCache = { at: 0, values: null };
  else if (key === "videos") videoCache = { at: 0, values: null };
  else if (key === "photos") { photoIndex.at = 0; photoCache.clear(); }
}

function sheetUrl() {
  return SHEET_ID ? `https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/${encodeURIComponent(SHEET_ID)}/edit` : "";
}

// Every data row of a tab, always read fresh. `n` is the row number in the sheet (the header is row 1).
async function tabRows(key) {
  const t = editorTab(key);
  const data = await call(`/values/${range(`A2:${t.lastCol}`, t.title)}`);
  return (data.values || []).map((r, i) => ({ n: i + 2, cells: fitRow(r, t.headers.length) }));
}

async function tabRow(key, n) {
  const t = editorTab(key);
  const data = await call(`/values/${range(`A${n}:${t.lastCol}${n}`, t.title)}`);
  return fitRow((data.values || [])[0], t.headers.length);
}

// Change only the cells that changed ({ columnIndex: text }), so the rest of the row is left exactly as it was.
// RAW means the text is stored as typed: a value starting with "=" can never run as a formula.
async function writeTabCells(key, n, changes) {
  const t = editorTab(key);
  const data = Object.keys(changes).map((i) => ({
    range: `${quoteTab(t.title)}!${colLetter(Number(i))}${n}`,
    values: [[cellText(changes[i])]],
  }));
  if (!data.length) return;
  await call("/values:batchUpdate", { method: "POST", data: { valueInputOption: "RAW", data } });
  invalidate(key);
}

// Change a registration number inside one column of a tab, in every row that holds it (a photo, a signature,
// quiz results, progress...). All the changes go in one request. Returns how many rows were changed.
async function renameRegCells(key, col, oldReg, newReg) {
  const t = editorTab(key);
  const tab = await findTab(t.title);
  if (!tab) return 0;
  const data = await call(`/values/${range(`A2:${t.lastCol}`, t.title)}`);
  const writes = [];
  (data.values || []).forEach((r, i) => {
    if (String((r && r[col]) || "").trim().toUpperCase() === oldReg) {
      writes.push({ range: `${quoteTab(t.title)}!${colLetter(col)}${i + 2}`, values: [[newReg]] });
    }
  });
  if (!writes.length) return 0;
  await call("/values:batchUpdate", { method: "POST", data: { valueInputOption: "RAW", data: writes } });
  invalidate(key);
  return writes.length;
}

async function appendTabRows(key, rows) {
  const t = editorTab(key);
  if (!rows.length) return;
  await call(
    `/values/${range(`A:${t.lastCol}`, t.title)}:append?valueInputOption=RAW&insertDataOption=INSERT_ROWS`,
    { method: "POST", data: { values: rows.map((r) => fitRow(r, t.headers.length)) } }
  );
  invalidate(key);
}

async function deleteTabRow(key, n) {
  const t = editorTab(key);
  const tab = await findTab(t.title);
  if (!tab) throw new Error("The sheet tab was not found: " + t.title);
  await call(":batchUpdate", {
    method: "POST",
    data: {
      requests: [{ deleteDimension: { range: { sheetId: tab.sheetId, dimension: "ROWS", startIndex: n - 1, endIndex: n } } }],
    },
  });
  invalidate(key);
}

// Delete every row of a tab whose column A equals `reg` (a deleted student's photo, results, progress).
// Rows are removed from the bottom up in ONE request, so the row numbers cannot shift under it.
async function deleteTabRowsByReg(key, reg, col = 0) {
  const t = editorTab(key);
  const tab = await findTab(t.title);
  if (!tab) return 0;
  const data = await call(`/values/${range(`A2:${t.lastCol}`, t.title)}`);
  const hits = [];
  (data.values || []).forEach((r, i) => { if (String((r && r[col]) || "").trim().toUpperCase() === reg) hits.push(i + 2); });
  if (!hits.length) return 0;
  await call(":batchUpdate", {
    method: "POST",
    data: {
      requests: hits.reverse().map((n) => ({
        deleteDimension: { range: { sheetId: tab.sheetId, dimension: "ROWS", startIndex: n - 1, endIndex: n } },
      })),
    },
  });
  invalidate(key);
  return hits.length;
}

module.exports = {
  init, readAll, readCached, appendRow, appendPhoto, setPhoto, photoChanged, getPhoto, getSignature, setSignature, readQuiz, appendQuiz, findFreeCode, markCodeUsed,
  getProgressCell, saveProgress, readLinks, saveLinks, readVideos, saveVideos, HEADERS, STATUSES,
  EDITOR_TABS, tabRows, tabRow, writeTabCells, renameRegCells, appendTabRows, deleteTabRow, deleteTabRowsByReg, invalidate, sheetUrl,
};
```

#### `team.js`

```js
// Dashboard team: named accounts with roles, and the roles themselves.
//
// Access (what a role can open) is a list of permissions:
//   view      Statistics, student lists and quiz results. Every role has it: it is what signing in needs.
//   links     Site links (social icons, Deriv link, About, Contact).
//   community Student community: the WhatsApp and Telegram groups the portal's Community page opens.
//   videos    Chapter videos.
//   quizcodes Quiz codes: make and cancel the one-time codes students need to unlock the quiz.
//   certificates Certificates: choose who signs them (name, title, signature), and open and download any certificate.
//   database  The Database tab: add, edit and delete records.
//   team      Team accounts and roles.
//
// Built-in roles (fixed, cannot be changed or deleted)
//   owner      Everything. The person who holds ADMIN_PASSWORD on Render is the main owner: always works, is not
//              stored in the sheet and cannot be removed or locked out by anyone. Signs in with the username "owner".
//              An owner can also give the Owner role to a team account (a co-owner). Only an owner can do that, and
//              only an owner can change, disable or remove a co-owner. A co-owner has a normal account on the "Team"
//              tab with their own username and password.
//   admin      Everything, including the team and roles.
//   editor     Statistics, site links, the student community links, chapter videos and the Database. (Not the team, and not quiz codes: give
//              someone "quizcodes" through a custom role, e.g. a "Quiz supervisor" role with only that access.)
//   moderator  Read only: statistics, student lists and quiz results.
// Custom roles are created by anyone with the "team" permission, who picks the access for each one. They live
// on the "Roles" sheet tab (a name and a list of permissions, no secrets).
//
// Accounts live on the "Team" sheet tab. Only a salted scrypt hash of each password is stored, never the
// password itself. A role change, a change to a role's access, a disable or a removal takes effect on the very
// next request, because the sign-in token only says WHO you are; your access is looked up each time.
const crypto = require("crypto");
const { promisify } = require("util");
const scrypt = promisify(crypto.scrypt);

const OWNER = "owner";

const PERMS = [
  { key: "view", label: "Statistics and quiz results", help: "See statistics, student lists and quiz results. Every role has this; it is needed to sign in." },
  { key: "links", label: "Site links", help: "Change the social icons, Deriv link, About and Contact." },
  { key: "community", label: "Student community", help: "Set the WhatsApp and Telegram groups students open from the Community page of their portal." },
  { key: "videos", label: "Chapter videos", help: "Add or change the YouTube video of each handbook chapter." },
  { key: "quizcodes", label: "Quiz codes", help: "Make and cancel the one-time codes students need to unlock the quiz." },
  { key: "certificates", label: "Certificates", help: "Set who signs the certificates (name, title and signature) and open or download any student's certificate." },
  { key: "database", label: "Database", help: "Add, edit and delete students, codes, quiz results and other records." },
  { key: "team", label: "Team and roles", help: "Add team members, create roles and choose their access." },
];
const PERM_KEYS = PERMS.map((p) => p.key);
const ALL = PERM_KEYS.slice();

const ROLES = {
  owner: { label: "Owner", perms: ALL },
  admin: { label: "Admin", perms: ALL },
  editor: { label: "Editor", perms: ["view", "links", "community", "videos", "database"] },
  moderator: { label: "Moderator", perms: ["view"] },
};
// Built-in roles a person may be given. "owner" is in the list but only an owner may hand it out.
const ASSIGNABLE = ["owner", "admin", "editor", "moderator"];
const RESERVED_ROLE_KEYS = Object.keys(ROLES);

// Sheet columns (also listed in sheets.js, and a test checks they match).
const HEADERS = ["Username", "Full Name", "Role", "Password Hash (do not edit)", "Active", "Created At (Lagos)", "Created By"];
const COL = { username: 0, name: 1, role: 2, hash: 3, active: 4, createdAt: 5, createdBy: 6 };

// The "Roles" tab: custom roles only (the built-in ones live in this file).
const ROLE_HEADERS = ["Role Key (do not edit)", "Role Name", "Access (do not edit)", "Created At (Lagos)", "Created By"];
const RCOL = { key: 0, name: 1, perms: 2, createdAt: 3, createdBy: 4 };

class TeamError extends Error {
  constructor(status, message, fields) {
    super(message);
    this.status = status;
    if (fields) this.fields = fields;
  }
}

// Built-in roles only. Custom roles are looked up through the service (permsOf).
const can = (role, perm) => Boolean(ROLES[role] && ROLES[role].perms.includes(perm));

// ---------- Input checks ----------
function normUsername(v) {
  return String(v == null ? "" : v).trim().toLowerCase();
}
function usernameError(u) {
  if (!/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]{2,29}$/.test(u)) return "Use 3 to 30 letters, numbers, dots, dashes or underscores.";
  if (u === OWNER) return "That username is reserved.";
  return "";
}
function passwordError(p) {
  if (typeof p !== "string" || p.length < 8) return "Use at least 8 characters.";
  if (p.length > 100) return "Use 100 characters or fewer.";
  return "";
}
function roleKeyFrom(name) {
  return oneLine(name, 40).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "").slice(0, 30).replace(/-+$/g, "");
}
// Keep only known permissions, always include "view", and put them in the standard order.
function cleanPerms(list) {
  const set = new Set(Array.isArray(list) ? list.map(String) : []);
  return PERM_KEYS.filter((k) => k === "view" || set.has(k));
}
function oneLine(v, max) {
  return String(v == null ? "" : v).replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().slice(0, max);
}

// ---------- Passwords ----------
async function hashPassword(pw) {
  const salt = crypto.randomBytes(16);
  const key = await scrypt(pw, salt, 64);
  return "s1$" + salt.toString("base64url") + "$" + key.toString("base64url");
}
async function verifyPassword(pw, stored) {
  const parts = String(stored || "").split("$");
  if (parts.length !== 3 || parts[0] !== "s1" || typeof pw !== "string") return false;
  try {
    const salt = Buffer.from(parts[1], "base64url");
    const want = Buffer.from(parts[2], "base64url");
    const got = await scrypt(pw, salt, want.length);
    return want.length > 0 && crypto.timingSafeEqual(got, want);
  } catch (e) {
    return false;
  }
}
// Spent on unknown usernames, so "no such user" and "wrong password" take about as long.
const DUMMY_HASH_PROMISE = hashPassword("not-a-real-password");

// ---------- Sign-in tokens ----------
const TOKEN_TTL_MS = 12 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
const TOKEN_PREFIX = "t1.";

function mac(payload, secret) {
  return crypto.createHmac("sha256", secret).update("admin." + payload).digest("base64url");
}
function signToken(username, secret, now = Date.now()) {
  const payload = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ u: username, e: now + TOKEN_TTL_MS })).toString("base64url");
  return TOKEN_PREFIX + payload + "." + mac(payload, secret);
}
// Returns the username, or null when the token is wrong, tampered with or expired.
function verifyToken(token, secret, now = Date.now()) {
  if (typeof token !== "string" || !token.startsWith(TOKEN_PREFIX) || token.length > 600 || !secret) return null;
  const [payload, sig, extra] = token.slice(TOKEN_PREFIX.length).split(".");
  if (!payload || !sig || extra !== undefined) return null;
  const a = Buffer.from(mac(payload, secret));
  const b = Buffer.from(sig);
  if (a.length !== b.length || !crypto.timingSafeEqual(a, b)) return null;
  try {
    const d = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(payload, "base64url").toString("utf8"));
    if (typeof d.u !== "string" || !(d.e > now)) return null;
    return d.u;
  } catch (e) {
    return null;
  }
}

// ---------- Rows <-> people ----------
function fromRows(rows) {
  return rows
    .map(({ n, cells }) => ({
      n,
      username: normUsername(cells[COL.username]),
      name: cells[COL.name] || "",
      role: String(cells[COL.role] || "").trim().toLowerCase(),
      hash: cells[COL.hash] || "",
      active: String(cells[COL.active] || "").trim().toLowerCase() !== "no",
      createdAt: cells[COL.createdAt] || "",
      createdBy: cells[COL.createdBy] || "",
    }))
    .filter((u) => u.username);
}
// What the dashboard may see: everything except the hash.
function publicUser(u) {
  return { username: u.username, name: u.name, role: u.role, active: u.active, createdAt: u.createdAt, createdBy: u.createdBy };
}

function roleFromRows(rows) {
  return rows
    .map(({ n, cells }) => ({
      n,
      key: String(cells[RCOL.key] || "").trim().toLowerCase(),
      label: cells[RCOL.name] || "",
      // only permissions that exist; "view" is always there
      perms: cleanPerms(String(cells[RCOL.perms] || "").split(",").map((x) => x.trim())),
      createdAt: cells[RCOL.createdAt] || "",
      createdBy: cells[RCOL.createdBy] || "",
    }))
    .filter((r) => r.key && !ROLES[r.key]);
}

// ---------- The service ----------
// `store` needs tabRows / appendTabRows / writeTabCells / deleteTabRow for the "team" and "roles" tabs (sheets.js,
// or the in-memory stand-in the tests use). `now` gives the "Created At" text.
function create(store, opts = {}) {
  const stamp = opts.now || (() => new Date().toISOString());
  const cacheMs = opts.cacheMs == null ? 10000 : opts.cacheMs;
  let cache = { at: 0, users: [] };
  let roleCache = { at: 0, roles: [] };
  let chain = Promise.resolve();
  // Changes go one at a time, so two admins adding the same username (or role) cannot both succeed.
  const queued = (task) => {
    const run = chain.then(task, task);
    chain = run.catch(() => {});
    return run;
  };
  const forget = () => { cache = { at: 0, users: [] }; roleCache = { at: 0, roles: [] }; };

  async function listFresh() {
    const users = fromRows(await store.tabRows("team"));
    cache = { at: Date.now(), users };
    return users;
  }
  async function list() {
    if (cache.at && Date.now() - cache.at < cacheMs) return cache.users;
    return listFresh();
  }

  // ----- roles -----
  async function customFresh() {
    const roles = roleFromRows(await store.tabRows("roles"));
    roleCache = { at: Date.now(), roles };
    return roles;
  }
  async function customList() {
    if (roleCache.at && Date.now() - roleCache.at < cacheMs) return roleCache.roles;
    return customFresh();
  }
  // { key, label, perms, builtin } for a role key, or null when there is no such role.
  async function roleInfo(key) {
    if (ROLES[key]) return { key, label: ROLES[key].label, perms: ROLES[key].perms.slice(), builtin: true };
    const r = (await customList()).find((x) => x.key === key);
    return r ? { key: r.key, label: r.label, perms: r.perms.slice(), builtin: false } : null;
  }
  async function permsOf(key) {
    // The owner never depends on the sheet.
    if (key === "owner") return ALL.slice();
    const r = await roleInfo(key);
    return r ? r.perms : [];
  }
  async function allow(role, perm) {
    return (await permsOf(role)).includes(perm);
  }
  const subsetOf = (perms, have) => perms.every((p) => have.includes(p));
  async function permsOfActor(actor) {
    return permsOf(actor.role);
  }

  // Roles a person can be given, with what each allows and whether the signed-in person may hand it out.
  // `actor` is optional; without it every role is marked as grantable.
  async function roles(actor) {
    const users = await listFresh();
    const have = actor ? await permsOfActor(actor) : ALL;
    const custom = await customFresh();
    const count = (key) => users.filter((u) => u.role === key).length;
    const out = ASSIGNABLE.map((k) => ({ key: k, label: ROLES[k].label, perms: ROLES[k].perms.slice(), builtin: true }));
    custom.forEach((r) => out.push({ key: r.key, label: r.label, perms: r.perms.slice(), builtin: false, createdAt: r.createdAt, createdBy: r.createdBy }));
    return out.map((r) => ({ ...r, members: count(r.key), canGrant: subsetOf(r.perms, have) && (r.key !== "owner" || !actor || actor.role === "owner") }));
  }

  // Who is this username right now? { username, name, role } or null (unknown, disabled or an unknown role).
  async function lookup(username) {
    if (username === OWNER) return { username: OWNER, name: "Owner", role: "owner" };
    const u = (await list()).find((x) => x.username === username);
    if (!u || !u.active || !(await roleInfo(u.role))) return null;
    return { username: u.username, name: u.name, role: u.role };
  }

  async function login(username, password, ownerPassword, safeEqual) {
    const name = normUsername(username);
    if (name === OWNER) {
      if (!ownerPassword) throw new TeamError(503, "The dashboard is not set up yet. Set ADMIN_PASSWORD on Render.");
      if (!safeEqual(String(password || ""), ownerPassword)) throw new TeamError(401, "Wrong username or password.");
      return { username: OWNER, name: "Owner", role: "owner" };
    }
    const u = (await list()).find((x) => x.username === name);
    const ok = await verifyPassword(String(password || ""), u ? u.hash : await DUMMY_HASH_PROMISE);
    if (!u || !ok) throw new TeamError(401, "Wrong username or password.");
    if (!u.active) throw new TeamError(403, "This account has been disabled. Ask an admin.");
    if (!(await roleInfo(u.role))) throw new TeamError(403, "This account has no valid role. Ask an admin.");
    return { username: u.username, name: u.name, role: u.role };
  }

  // For the sign-in drop-down: the roles somebody can sign in as, and how many accounts each has.
  // Usernames are never listed.
  async function loginChoices() {
    // The main owner (ADMIN_PASSWORD) always counts as one, plus any co-owners on the team.
    const out = [{ key: "owner", label: ROLES.owner.label, count: 1 }];
    const byRole = new Map();
    for (const u of await list()) {
      if (!u.active) continue;
      byRole.set(u.role, (byRole.get(u.role) || 0) + 1);
    }
    for (const [key, count] of byRole) {
      if (key === "owner") { out[0].count += count; continue; }
      const info = await roleInfo(key);
      if (info) out.push({ key, label: info.label, count });
    }
    const order = ["owner", "admin", "editor", "moderator"];
    out.sort((a, b) => (order.indexOf(a.key) + 1 || 99) - (order.indexOf(b.key) + 1 || 99) || a.label.localeCompare(b.label));
    return out;
  }

  // The sign-in page picks a role, and the role name is the username. Return the accounts that hold that role;
  // the password then decides which of them is signing in.
  async function loginNames(roleKey) {
    const role = String(roleKey || "").toLowerCase();
    const names = (await list()).filter((u) => u.active && u.role === role).map((u) => u.username);
    return role === "owner" ? [OWNER, ...names] : names;
  }

  async function members() {
    return (await listFresh()).map(publicUser);
  }

  // A role the actor may give to someone: it must exist, must not be the owner, and may not allow more than the actor has.
  async function checkAssignable(role, actor, errors) {
    if (!role) { errors.role = "Choose a role."; return false; }
    const info = await roleInfo(role);
    if (!info) { errors.role = "That role does not exist."; return false; }
    if (role === "owner" && !(actor && actor.role === "owner")) {
      errors.role = "Only an owner can make someone an owner.";
      return false;
    }
    if (actor && !subsetOf(info.perms, await permsOfActor(actor))) {
      errors.role = "You cannot give a role that allows more than you can do yourself.";
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  function add(input, actor) {
    return queued(async () => {
      const b = input || {};
      const username = normUsername(b.username);
      const name = oneLine(b.fullName, 80);
      const errors = {};
      const ue = usernameError(username);
      if (ue) errors.username = ue;
      if (!name) errors.fullName = "Enter their full name.";
      await checkAssignable(b.role, actor, errors);
      const pe = passwordError(b.password);
      if (pe) errors.password = pe;
      if (Object.keys(errors).length) throw new TeamError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", errors);
      const users = await listFresh();
      if (users.some((u) => u.username === username)) {
        throw new TeamError(409, "That username is already taken.", { username: "That username is already taken." });
      }
      const hash = await hashPassword(b.password);
      const row = [];
      row[COL.username] = username; row[COL.name] = name; row[COL.role] = b.role; row[COL.hash] = hash;
      row[COL.active] = "Yes"; row[COL.createdAt] = stamp(); row[COL.createdBy] = actor.username;
      await store.appendTabRows("team", [row]);
      forget();
      return publicUser({ username, name, role: b.role, active: true, createdAt: row[COL.createdAt], createdBy: actor.username });
    });
  }

  // changes: any of { fullName, role, active, password }. Nobody can demote, disable or remove themselves.
  function update(username, changes, actor) {
    return queued(async () => {
      const target = normUsername(username);
      const c = changes || {};
      const u = (await listFresh()).find((x) => x.username === target);
      if (!u) throw new TeamError(404, "That account no longer exists.");
      const self = target === actor.username;
      const errors = {};
      const write = {};
      if (c.fullName !== undefined) {
        const name = oneLine(c.fullName, 80);
        if (!name) errors.fullName = "Enter their full name."; else write[COL.name] = name;
      }
      if (c.role !== undefined && c.role !== u.role) {
        if (self) throw new TeamError(400, "You cannot change your own role.");
        if (await checkAssignable(c.role, actor, errors)) write[COL.role] = c.role;
      }
      // A co-owner is out of reach of everyone who is not an owner (an admin has the same access, so the access
      // comparison below would not stop them).
      if (!self && u.role === "owner" && actor.role !== "owner") {
        throw new TeamError(403, "Only an owner can change an owner's account.");
      }
      // Someone whose current role allows more than the actor can do is out of the actor's reach.
      if (!self && (write[COL.role] !== undefined || c.active !== undefined || (c.password !== undefined && c.password !== ""))) {
        const theirs = await permsOf(u.role);
        if (!subsetOf(theirs, await permsOfActor(actor))) {
          throw new TeamError(403, "This person can do more than you can, so you cannot change their account.");
        }
      }
      if (c.active !== undefined && Boolean(c.active) !== u.active) {
        if (self) throw new TeamError(400, "You cannot disable your own account.");
        write[COL.active] = c.active ? "Yes" : "No";
      }
      if (c.password !== undefined && c.password !== "") {
        const pe = passwordError(c.password);
        if (pe) errors.password = pe; else write[COL.hash] = await hashPassword(c.password);
      }
      if (Object.keys(errors).length) throw new TeamError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", errors);
      if (Object.keys(write).length) {
        await store.writeTabCells("team", u.n, write);
        forget();
      }
      return publicUser((await listFresh()).find((x) => x.username === target));
    });
  }

  function remove(username, actor) {
    return queued(async () => {
      const target = normUsername(username);
      if (target === actor.username) throw new TeamError(400, "You cannot remove your own account.");
      const u = (await listFresh()).find((x) => x.username === target);
      if (!u) throw new TeamError(404, "That account no longer exists.");
      if (u.role === "owner" && actor.role !== "owner") throw new TeamError(403, "Only an owner can remove an owner.");
      if (!subsetOf(await permsOf(u.role), await permsOfActor(actor))) {
        throw new TeamError(403, "This person can do more than you can, so you cannot remove them.");
      }
      await store.deleteTabRow("team", u.n);
      forget();
      return { removed: target };
    });
  }

  // A team member changes their own password (the owner's lives on Render, not here).
  function changeOwnPassword(username, current, next) {
    return queued(async () => {
      const target = normUsername(username);
      const u = (await listFresh()).find((x) => x.username === target);
      if (!u) throw new TeamError(404, "Account not found.");
      if (!(await verifyPassword(String(current || ""), u.hash))) {
        throw new TeamError(400, "Your current password is not right.", { current: "Your current password is not right." });
      }
      const pe = passwordError(next);
      if (pe) throw new TeamError(400, pe, { next: pe });
      await store.writeTabCells("team", u.n, { [COL.hash]: await hashPassword(next) });
      forget();
      return { ok: true };
    });
  }

  // ----- creating and changing roles -----
  // input: { name, perms: [permission keys] }. "view" is always included.
  // Nobody can give a role access they do not have themselves, and nobody changes the role they hold.
  function checkRoleInput(input, actorPerms, errors) {
    const b = input || {};
    const label = oneLine(b.name, 40);
    if (!label) errors.name = "Enter a name for the role.";
    const asked = Array.isArray(b.perms) ? b.perms.map(String) : [];
    const unknown = asked.filter((k) => !PERM_KEYS.includes(k));
    if (unknown.length) errors.perms = "Unknown access: " + unknown.join(", ") + ".";
    const perms = cleanPerms(asked);
    if (!errors.perms && !subsetOf(perms, actorPerms)) errors.perms = "You can only give access that you have yourself.";
    return { label, perms };
  }

  function addRole(input, actor) {
    return queued(async () => {
      const errors = {};
      const { label, perms } = checkRoleInput(input, await permsOfActor(actor), errors);
      const key = roleKeyFrom(label);
      if (label && key.length < 3) errors.name = "Use at least 3 letters or numbers in the name.";
      else if (label && RESERVED_ROLE_KEYS.includes(key)) errors.name = "That name is taken by a built-in role. Choose another.";
      if (Object.keys(errors).length) throw new TeamError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", errors);
      if ((await customFresh()).some((r) => r.key === key)) {
        throw new TeamError(409, "A role with that name already exists.", { name: "A role with that name already exists." });
      }
      const row = [];
      row[RCOL.key] = key; row[RCOL.name] = label; row[RCOL.perms] = perms.join(",");
      row[RCOL.createdAt] = stamp(); row[RCOL.createdBy] = actor.username;
      await store.appendTabRows("roles", [row]);
      forget();
      return { key, label, perms, builtin: false, createdAt: row[RCOL.createdAt], createdBy: actor.username };
    });
  }

  function updateRole(key, input, actor) {
    return queued(async () => {
      if (ROLES[key]) throw new TeamError(400, "Built-in roles cannot be changed.");
      const r = (await customFresh()).find((x) => x.key === key);
      if (!r) throw new TeamError(404, "That role no longer exists.");
      if (actor.role === key) throw new TeamError(400, "You cannot change the role you hold. Ask someone else with access to the team.");
      const have = await permsOfActor(actor);
      // A role that allows more than the actor cannot be reduced or renamed by the actor either.
      if (!subsetOf(r.perms, have)) throw new TeamError(403, "This role allows more than you can do, so you cannot change it.");
      const errors = {};
      const { label, perms } = checkRoleInput(input, have, errors);
      if (Object.keys(errors).length) throw new TeamError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", errors);
      const write = {};
      if (label !== r.label) write[RCOL.name] = label;
      if (perms.join(",") !== r.perms.join(",")) write[RCOL.perms] = perms.join(",");
      if (Object.keys(write).length) {
        await store.writeTabCells("roles", r.n, write);
        forget();
      }
      return { key, label, perms, builtin: false, createdAt: r.createdAt, createdBy: r.createdBy };
    });
  }

  function removeRole(key, actor) {
    return queued(async () => {
      if (ROLES[key]) throw new TeamError(400, "Built-in roles cannot be removed.");
      const r = (await customFresh()).find((x) => x.key === key);
      if (!r) throw new TeamError(404, "That role no longer exists.");
      if (actor.role === key) throw new TeamError(400, "You cannot remove the role you hold.");
      if (!subsetOf(r.perms, await permsOfActor(actor))) throw new TeamError(403, "This role allows more than you can do, so you cannot remove it.");
      const using = (await listFresh()).filter((u) => u.role === key).length;
      if (using) {
        throw new TeamError(409, using === 1
          ? "One team member still has this role. Give them another role first."
          : using + " team members still have this role. Give them another role first.");
      }
      await store.deleteTabRow("roles", r.n);
      forget();
      return { removed: key };
    });
  }

  return {
    lookup, login, loginChoices, loginNames, members, add, update, remove, changeOwnPassword,
    roles, roleInfo, permsOf, allow, addRole, updateRole, removeRole,
  };
}

module.exports = {
  OWNER, ROLES, ASSIGNABLE, PERMS, PERM_KEYS, RESERVED_ROLE_KEYS, HEADERS, COL, ROLE_HEADERS, RCOL, TeamError, can, create,
  hashPassword, verifyPassword, signToken, verifyToken, normUsername, usernameError, passwordError, fromRows, publicUser,
  roleKeyFrom, cleanPerms, roleFromRows,
};
```

#### `quizcodes.js`

```js
// One-time quiz codes: the quiz stays locked until a student enters a code, and a code works for ONE quiz.
//
// Life of a code (the "Status" column of the "Quiz Codes" sheet tab):
//   Unused     made by an admin (or any role given the "quizcodes" access). Nobody has used it yet.
//   Unlocked   a student entered it. THEIR quiz is open and stays open until they finish it
//              (on any device, even after signing out), because this is kept here and not in the browser.
//   Completed  the student's quiz result was saved. The code is spent: the quiz is locked again and a
//              new code is needed to take it another time.
//   Revoked    cancelled by an admin before it was finished. If the student was mid-quiz, the quiz locks.
//
// Rules this file enforces (the website, not the browser, is the judge):
//   - A code can be entered once. The same student entering it again while it is open just gets "already open".
//   - A code made "for" one registration number cannot be used by anyone else.
//   - A student can have only ONE open quiz at a time, so codes cannot be stockpiled or burned by accident.
//   - Entering a code and finishing a quiz are changed one at a time (a queue), so two people cannot use the
//     same code at once and a finish cannot race an entry.
//
// Codes look like QZ-7K4M-9PXR (no 0/O/1/I/L, so they are easy to read out loud). When a code is checked,
// capitals, spaces and dashes do not matter: "qz 7k4m 9pxr" is the same code.
const crypto = require("crypto");

const HEADERS = [
  "Quiz Code",
  "Created At (Lagos)",
  "Created By",
  "For (Reg Number, optional)",
  "Status",
  "Used By (Reg Number)",
  "Unlocked At (Lagos)",
  "Completed At (Lagos)",
  "Revoked (By, At)",
];
const COL = { code: 0, createdAt: 1, createdBy: 2, forReg: 3, status: 4, usedBy: 5, unlockedAt: 6, completedAt: 7, revoked: 8 };

const STATUS = { UNUSED: "Unused", UNLOCKED: "Unlocked", COMPLETED: "Completed", REVOKED: "Revoked" };
const STATUS_LIST = Object.values(STATUS);

const ALPHABET = "ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789"; // 31 characters: no 0, O, 1, I or L
const MAX_AT_ONCE = 50;
const REG_RE = /^ABECFX\/\d{2}\/\d{4}$/;

// What a student sees when a code does not work. One message for "no such code", "already used", "cancelled"
// and "meant for someone else", so nobody can use the form to find out which codes exist.
const BAD_CODE = "This quiz code is not valid or has already been used. Ask your mentor for a new one.";

class QuizCodeError extends Error {
  constructor(status, message, fields) {
    super(message);
    this.status = status;
    if (fields) this.fields = fields;
  }
}

// Capitals only, letters and numbers only: the form a code is compared in.
function normCode(v) {
  return String(v == null ? "" : v).slice(0, 60).toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/g, "");
}
const normReg = (v) => String(v == null ? "" : v).trim().toUpperCase().replace(/\s+/g, "");

// QZ-XXXX-XXXX from 31^8 (about 850 billion) possibilities. `random(n)` returns a whole number 0..n-1.
function makeCode(random = (n) => crypto.randomInt(n)) {
  const pick = (len) => Array.from({ length: len }, () => ALPHABET[random(ALPHABET.length)]).join("");
  return "QZ-" + pick(4) + "-" + pick(4);
}

function statusOf(cells) {
  const s = String(cells[COL.status] || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  const found = STATUS_LIST.find((x) => x.toLowerCase() === s);
  if (found) return found;
  // A row typed in by hand with no status is a fresh code, unless it already names who used it.
  return String(cells[COL.usedBy] || "").trim() ? STATUS.COMPLETED : STATUS.UNUSED;
}

function fromRows(rows) {
  return rows
    .map(({ n, cells }) => ({
      n,
      code: String(cells[COL.code] || "").trim(),
      createdAt: cells[COL.createdAt] || "",
      createdBy: cells[COL.createdBy] || "",
      forReg: normReg(cells[COL.forReg]),
      status: statusOf(cells),
      usedBy: normReg(cells[COL.usedBy]),
      unlockedAt: cells[COL.unlockedAt] || "",
      completedAt: cells[COL.completedAt] || "",
      revoked: cells[COL.revoked] || "",
    }))
    .filter((r) => r.code);
}

// What the dashboard may see of a code.
function publicCode(r) {
  return {
    code: r.code,
    status: r.status,
    forReg: r.forReg,
    createdAt: r.createdAt,
    createdBy: r.createdBy,
    usedBy: r.usedBy,
    unlockedAt: r.unlockedAt,
    completedAt: r.completedAt,
    revoked: r.revoked,
  };
}

// `store` needs tabRows / appendTabRows / writeTabCells for the "quizcodes" tab (sheets.js, or the in-memory
// stand-in the tests use). Options:
//   now            gives the time text written to the sheet (the website passes Lagos time)
//   studentExists  async (reg) => boolean, so a code cannot be made "for" a number nobody has
//   cacheMs        how long a read may be re-used for the "is the quiz open?" display. Anything that
//                  decides (entering a code, opening the quiz, saving a result) always reads fresh.
//   random         a random whole number below n (the tests pass their own)
function create(store, opts = {}) {
  const stamp = opts.now || (() => new Date().toISOString());
  const studentExists = opts.studentExists || null;
  const cacheMs = opts.cacheMs == null ? 3000 : opts.cacheMs;
  const random = opts.random || ((n) => crypto.randomInt(n));
  let cache = { at: 0, rows: [] };
  let chain = Promise.resolve();

  // Changes go one at a time, so two people cannot spend the same code and a finish cannot race an entry.
  const queued = (task) => {
    const run = chain.then(task, task);
    chain = run.catch(() => {});
    return run;
  };
  const forget = () => { cache = { at: 0, rows: [] }; };

  async function fresh() {
    const rows = fromRows(await store.tabRows("quizcodes"));
    cache = { at: Date.now(), rows };
    return rows;
  }
  async function recent() {
    if (cache.at && Date.now() - cache.at < cacheMs) return cache.rows;
    return fresh();
  }

  const openFor = (rows, reg) => rows.find((r) => r.status === STATUS.UNLOCKED && r.usedBy === reg) || null;

  // ---------- Students ----------

  // Is this student's quiz open right now? { unlocked, since }.
  // Pass { fresh: true } whenever the answer decides something (opening the quiz, saving a result).
  async function accessFor(reg, o = {}) {
    const rows = o.fresh ? await fresh() : await recent();
    const open = openFor(rows, normReg(reg));
    return { unlocked: Boolean(open), since: open ? open.unlockedAt : "" };
  }

  // A student enters a code. Opens their quiz, or throws a QuizCodeError that is safe to show them.
  function redeem(codeInput, regInput) {
    return queued(async () => {
      const reg = normReg(regInput);
      const want = normCode(codeInput);
      if (!want) throw new QuizCodeError(400, "Enter your quiz code.", { code: "Enter your quiz code." });
      const rows = await fresh();
      const row = rows.find((r) => normCode(r.code) === want) || null;
      const open = openFor(rows, reg);

      if (open) {
        // Typing the same code again (a refresh, another device) is fine: the quiz is already open.
        if (row && row.n === open.n) return { unlocked: true, already: true };
        throw new QuizCodeError(409, "Your quiz is already unlocked. Finish it first. You will need a new code to take the quiz again.");
      }
      if (!row || row.status !== STATUS.UNUSED || (row.forReg && row.forReg !== reg)) {
        throw new QuizCodeError(400, BAD_CODE, { code: BAD_CODE });
      }
      await store.writeTabCells("quizcodes", row.n, {
        [COL.status]: STATUS.UNLOCKED,
        [COL.usedBy]: reg,
        [COL.unlockedAt]: stamp(),
      });
      forget();
      return { unlocked: true, already: false };
    });
  }

  // The student's quiz result has been saved: the open code is spent and the quiz locks again.
  // Returns the code that was spent, or null when the student had none open.
  function complete(regInput) {
    return queued(async () => {
      const reg = normReg(regInput);
      const open = openFor(await fresh(), reg);
      if (!open) return null;
      await store.writeTabCells("quizcodes", open.n, { [COL.status]: STATUS.COMPLETED, [COL.completedAt]: stamp() });
      forget();
      return open.code;
    });
  }

  // ---------- The dashboard (admin, or a role given the "quizcodes" access) ----------

  // Make `count` new codes, optionally all for one registration number. Returns them with the time they were made.
  function generate(input, actor) {
    return queued(async () => {
      const b = input || {};
      const count = Number(b.count);
      const forReg = normReg(b.forReg);
      const errors = {};
      if (!Number.isInteger(count) || count < 1 || count > MAX_AT_ONCE) errors.count = "Choose how many codes to make (1 to " + MAX_AT_ONCE + ").";
      if (forReg) {
        if (!REG_RE.test(forReg)) errors.forReg = "Enter a registration number like ABECFX/26/0001, or leave it empty.";
        else if (studentExists && !(await studentExists(forReg))) errors.forReg = "No student has this registration number.";
      }
      if (Object.keys(errors).length) throw new QuizCodeError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", errors);

      const taken = new Set((await fresh()).map((r) => normCode(r.code)));
      const at = stamp();
      const by = (actor && actor.username) || "";
      const made = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        let code;
        let guard = 0;
        do { code = makeCode(random); } while (taken.has(normCode(code)) && ++guard < 100);
        if (taken.has(normCode(code))) throw new QuizCodeError(500, "Could not make a new code. Please try again.");
        taken.add(normCode(code));
        made.push([code, at, by, forReg, STATUS.UNUSED, "", "", "", ""]);
      }
      await store.appendTabRows("quizcodes", made);
      forget();
      return { codes: made.map((r) => ({ code: r[COL.code], createdAt: at, createdBy: by, forReg })) };
    });
  }

  // Every code, newest first.
  async function list() {
    return (await fresh()).map(publicCode).reverse();
  }

  // Cancel a code that has not been finished. If its student is in the middle of the quiz, their quiz locks.
  function revoke(codeInput, actor) {
    return queued(async () => {
      const want = normCode(codeInput);
      const row = want ? (await fresh()).find((r) => normCode(r.code) === want) : null;
      if (!row) throw new QuizCodeError(404, "That code could not be found.");
      if (row.status === STATUS.COMPLETED) throw new QuizCodeError(409, "This code was already used to finish a quiz, so there is nothing to cancel.");
      if (row.status === STATUS.REVOKED) return { code: row.code, status: STATUS.REVOKED, wasOpen: false };
      const by = (actor && actor.username) || "";
      await store.writeTabCells("quizcodes", row.n, { [COL.status]: STATUS.REVOKED, [COL.revoked]: by + ", " + stamp() });
      forget();
      return { code: row.code, status: STATUS.REVOKED, wasOpen: row.status === STATUS.UNLOCKED };
    });
  }

  return { accessFor, redeem, complete, generate, list, revoke };
}

module.exports = { create, makeCode, normCode, HEADERS, COL, STATUS, STATUS_LIST, MAX_AT_ONCE, BAD_CODE, QuizCodeError };
```

#### `admin-database.js`

```js
// The editor dashboard's "Database" view: browse, search, add, edit and delete the records behind the website
// (students, mentorship codes, quiz results, handbook progress, site links, chapter videos) without opening
// Google Sheets. The records are stored in the Google Sheet; this file decides what may be done to each table.
//
// Every change is checked HERE, never trusted from the browser, with the same rules as the rest of the site
// (registration form, status list, grading, handbook chapters, link and video checks).
//
//   Students          create (number, sign-up time and ID date are filled in for you), edit, delete.
//                     Locked: registration number, sign-up time, mentorship code used.
//                     Deleting a student also deletes their photo, quiz results and handbook progress, and their
//                     number is never given to anyone else (new numbers go past every number ever recorded).
//   Mentorship codes  create (many at once), edit, delete. A code that a student used cannot be renamed.
//   Quiz results      create (for a registered student), edit score and total (percent and grade follow), delete.
//   Handbook progress create (one row per registered student), edit the chapters read (count and percent follow), delete.
//   Site links, Chapter videos   fixed rows (the website looks them up by name): edit the Value only.
// The Photos tab is never listed.
//
// A row is only changed when it still matches what the editor was looking at (`original`). If someone else
// changed or removed it in the meantime, the change is refused and the editor reloads, so a stale screen can
// never write over the wrong row.

const countries = require("./countries");
const states = require("./states.json");
const idrules = require("./idrules");
const grading = require("./grading");
const progress = require("./handbook-progress");
const handbook = require("./handbook-content");
const links = require("./links");
const videos = require("./videos");

const GENDERS = ["Male", "Female"];
const REG_RE = /^ABECFX\/\d{2}\/\d{4}$/;
const CODE_RE = /^[A-Z0-9_-]{4,40}$/;
const MAX_CODES_AT_ONCE = 200;
const MAX_CELL = 5000;

class AdminError extends Error {
  constructor(status, message, fields) {
    super(message);
    this.status = status;
    this.fields = fields || null; // { columnIndex: message }
  }
}

// One line, no control characters, trimmed and capped.
function clean(v, max = 120) {
  return String(v == null ? "" : v)
    .replace(/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]+/g, " ")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim()
    .slice(0, max);
}

function lagosTimestamp() {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-GB", { timeZone: "Africa/Lagos", dateStyle: "medium", timeStyle: "medium" }).format(new Date());
}

function realDate(v) {
  const m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(v);
  if (!m) return false;
  const y = +m[1], mo = +m[2], d = +m[3];
  const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, mo - 1, d));
  return dt.getUTCFullYear() === y && dt.getUTCMonth() === mo - 1 && dt.getUTCDate() === d;
}

const normReg = (v) => clean(v, 20).toUpperCase().replace(/\s+/g, "");
const same = (a, b) => String(a == null ? "" : a).trim() === String(b == null ? "" : b).trim();

function create(store) {
  const rowsOf = async (key) => (await store.tabRows(key)).map((r) => r.cells);
  const findStudent = async (reg) => (await rowsOf("students")).find((r) => (r[0] || "").toUpperCase() === reg);

  const TABS = {
    students: {
      label: "Students",
      help: "One row per student. The sign-up time and mentorship code are locked. If you change a registration number, their photo, signature, quiz results, handbook progress and code records are updated to the new number automatically. A student's photo can only be added when the student registers.",
      editable: [0, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 9, 14, 10, 11, 12, 13, 15],
      selects: { 2: countries, 7: GENDERS, 9: store.STATUSES, 15: ["", "Yes"] },
      placeholders: { 0: "ABECFX/26/0001 (changing it updates their photo, signature, quiz results and progress too)", 14: "e.g. Mentor, CEO, Support Staff", 10: "e.g. 15 Oct 2026", 11: "YYYY-MM-DD (a new date renews an expired ID)", 13: "YYYY-MM-DD" },
      newestFirst: true,
      needsRows: true,
      create: {
        label: "Add student",
        help: "The registration number, sign-up time and ID issue date are filled in for you. A student added here has no photo, so the ID card shows their initials.",
        fields: [2, 12, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 13, 9, 14, 10],
        defaults: { 3: "Forex Course", 9: "Uncertified" },
      },
      deleteWarning: "This also deletes the student's photo, quiz results and handbook progress, and cannot be undone. Their registration number will never be given to anyone else.",
      check(p, cur, rows) {
        const errors = {};
        const changed = (i) => p[i] !== cur[i];
        if (changed(0) && cur[0] != null) {
          p[0] = normReg(p[0]);
          if (!REG_RE.test(p[0])) errors[0] = "Use the format ABECFX/26/0001 (ABECFX, a slash, the 2-digit year, a slash, a 4-digit number).";
          else if (rows.some((r) => (r[0] || "").trim().toUpperCase() === p[0])) errors[0] = "Another student already has this registration number.";
        }
        if (changed(2)) {
          p[2] = clean(p[2]);
          if (!countries.includes(p[2])) errors[2] = "Choose a country from the list.";
        }
        if (changed(2) || changed(12)) {
          p[12] = clean(p[12], 60);
          if (!errors[2]) {
            const list = states[p[2]];
            if (list) {
              if (!list.includes(p[12])) errors[12] = "Choose a state or region that belongs to " + p[2] + ".";
            } else if (p[12].length < 2) {
              errors[12] = "Enter the state, province or region.";
            }
          }
        }
        if (changed(3)) {
          p[3] = clean(p[3]);
          if (!p[3]) errors[3] = "Enter the programme.";
        }
        if (changed(4)) {
          p[4] = clean(p[4]);
          if (p[4].length < 3 || !/\s/.test(p[4])) errors[4] = "Enter the full name (first and last name).";
        }
        if (changed(5)) {
          p[5] = clean(p[5], 254).toLowerCase();
          if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]{2,}$/.test(p[5])) errors[5] = "Enter a valid email address.";
          else if (rows.some((r) => (r[5] || "").toLowerCase() === p[5])) errors[5] = "Another student already uses this email address.";
        }
        if (changed(6)) {
          p[6] = clean(p[6], 25);
          const digits = p[6].replace(/[^\d]/g, "");
          if (!/^\+?[\d\s\-()]+$/.test(p[6]) || digits.length < 7 || digits.length > 15) {
            errors[6] = "Enter a WhatsApp number with country code, e.g. +234 801 234 5678.";
          }
        }
        if (changed(7)) {
          p[7] = clean(p[7]);
          if (!GENDERS.includes(p[7])) errors[7] = "Choose Male or Female.";
        }
        if (changed(9)) {
          p[9] = clean(p[9]);
          if (!store.STATUSES.includes(p[9])) errors[9] = "Choose a status from the list.";
        }
        // The role only exists for a Team Member: it is required for them and cleared for everyone else.
        if (!errors[9]) {
          if (p[9] === "Team Member") {
            p[14] = clean(p[14], 40);
            if (p[14].length < 2) errors[14] = "Type this team member's role, e.g. Mentor, CEO or Support Staff.";
          } else {
            p[14] = "";
          }
        }
        if (changed(15)) {
          p[15] = clean(p[15], 10);
          if (p[15] && p[15].toLowerCase() !== "yes") errors[15] = "Choose Yes, or leave it empty.";
          else if (p[15]) p[15] = "Yes";
        }
        if (changed(10)) p[10] = clean(p[10], 40);
        if (changed(11)) {
          p[11] = clean(p[11], 10);
          if (p[11] && !(realDate(p[11]) && p[11] >= "2000-01-01" && p[11] <= "2100-12-31")) errors[11] = "Enter a real date as YYYY-MM-DD, or leave it empty.";
        }
        if (changed(13)) {
          p[13] = clean(p[13], 10);
          if (p[13] && !(realDate(p[13]) && p[13] >= "1920-01-01" && p[13] <= idrules.lagosToday())) {
            errors[13] = "Enter a real date of birth as YYYY-MM-DD (not in the future), or leave it empty.";
          }
        }
        return errors;
      },
      // Fill in what the website normally fills in, then return the new row.
      async build(p) {
        const [students, codes] = await Promise.all([rowsOf("students"), rowsOf("codes")]);
        const yy = idrules.lagosToday().slice(2, 4);
        const prefix = `ABECFX/${yy}/`;
        let max = 0;
        // Past every number ever recorded (students AND the codes' "used by"), so a deleted student's number is never reused.
        for (const reg of students.map((r) => r[0]).concat(codes.map((r) => r[2]))) {
          const r = String(reg || "").trim().toUpperCase();
          if (r.startsWith(prefix)) { const n = parseInt(r.slice(prefix.length), 10); if (n > max) max = n; }
        }
        if (max >= 9999) throw new AdminError(400, "This year's registration numbers are used up.");
        p[0] = prefix + String(max + 1).padStart(4, "0");
        p[1] = lagosTimestamp();
        p[8] = "";
        p[11] = p[11] || idrules.lagosToday();
        if (p[9] !== "Uncertified" && !p[10]) p[10] = idrules.formatISO(idrules.lagosToday());
        return p;
      },
      async cascade(row) {
        const reg = (row[0] || "").trim().toUpperCase();
        if (!reg) return;
        await store.deleteTabRowsByReg("photos", reg, 0);
        await store.deleteTabRowsByReg("signatures", reg, 0);
        await store.deleteTabRowsByReg("dobchanges", reg, 0);
        await store.deleteTabRowsByReg("quiz", reg, 1);
        await store.deleteTabRowsByReg("progress", reg, 0);
      },
    },

    codes: {
      label: "Mentorship codes",
      help: "A code works once. A code that a student has used cannot be renamed.",
      editable: [0, 2, 3],
      selects: {},
      placeholders: { 2: "ABECFX/26/0001 (empty = not used)", 3: "e.g. 15 Oct 2026, 10:30:00" },
      newestFirst: true,
      needsRows: true,
      create: { label: "Add codes", mode: "text", help: "Type or paste new codes: one per line, or separated by commas. Letters, numbers, - and _ only, 4 to 40 characters. Letters are made capital." },
      deleteWarning: "If a student used this code, the record of who used it is lost. The student keeps their account.",
      check(p, cur, rows) {
        const errors = {};
        if (p[0] !== cur[0]) {
          p[0] = clean(p[0], 40).toUpperCase();
          if ((cur[2] || "").trim()) errors[0] = "This code has already been used, so its name cannot be changed.";
          else if (!CODE_RE.test(p[0])) errors[0] = "Use 4 to 40 letters, numbers, - or _.";
          else if (rows.some((r) => (r[0] || "").trim().toUpperCase() === p[0])) errors[0] = "This code is already in the list.";
        }
        if (p[2] !== cur[2]) {
          p[2] = clean(p[2], 20).toUpperCase();
          if (p[2] && !REG_RE.test(p[2])) errors[2] = "Enter a registration number like ABECFX/26/0001, or leave it empty.";
        }
        if (p[3] !== cur[3]) p[3] = clean(p[3], 40);
        if (!p[2]) p[3] = ""; // an unused code has no "used at" time
        return errors;
      },
      // text -> rows to append
      prepareText(text, rows) {
        const wanted = String(text == null ? "" : text).split(/[\s,;]+/).map((c) => c.trim().toUpperCase()).filter(Boolean);
        if (!wanted.length) throw new AdminError(400, "Type at least one code.");
        if (wanted.length > MAX_CODES_AT_ONCE) throw new AdminError(400, "Add at most " + MAX_CODES_AT_ONCE + " codes at a time.");
        const bad = wanted.filter((c) => !CODE_RE.test(c));
        if (bad.length) throw new AdminError(400, "These are not valid codes (use 4 to 40 letters, numbers, - or _): " + bad.slice(0, 5).join(", ") + (bad.length > 5 ? "..." : ""));
        const have = new Set(rows.map((r) => (r[0] || "").trim().toUpperCase()));
        const seen = new Set();
        const dup = [];
        for (const c of wanted) {
          if (have.has(c) || seen.has(c)) dup.push(c);
          seen.add(c);
        }
        if (dup.length) throw new AdminError(400, "Already in the list or typed twice: " + [...new Set(dup)].slice(0, 5).join(", ") + (dup.length > 5 ? "..." : ""));
        const at = lagosTimestamp();
        return [...seen].map((c) => [c, at, "", ""]);
      },
    },

    quiz: {
      label: "Quiz results",
      help: "Every finished quiz attempt. Edit the score or total and the percent and grade follow. Adding a result needs a registered student.",
      editable: [3, 4],
      selects: {},
      placeholders: { 3: "Questions answered correctly", 4: "Number of questions in the quiz" },
      newestFirst: true,
      canDelete: true,
      create: { label: "Add result", help: "Enter the student's registration number, the score and the total. The name, percent, grade and attempt number are worked out for you.", fields: [1, 3, 4] },
      deleteWarning: "This quiz result is removed for good.",
      check(p) {
        const errors = {};
        const score = Number(p[3]), total = Number(p[4]);
        if (!/^\d{1,3}$/.test(clean(p[3], 4)) || !Number.isInteger(score)) errors[3] = "Enter the score as a whole number.";
        if (!/^\d{1,3}$/.test(clean(p[4], 4)) || !Number.isInteger(total) || total < 1 || total > 500) errors[4] = "Enter the total number of questions (1 to 500).";
        if (!errors[3] && !errors[4] && score > total) errors[3] = "The score cannot be more than the total.";
        if (Object.keys(errors).length) return errors;
        const pct = grading.percent(score, total);
        p[3] = String(score); p[4] = String(total); p[5] = String(pct); p[6] = grading.gradeFor(pct).grade;
        return {};
      },
      async prepare(p) {
        const reg = normReg(p[1]);
        if (!REG_RE.test(reg)) throw new AdminError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", { 1: "Enter a registration number like ABECFX/26/0001." });
        const stu = await findStudent(reg);
        if (!stu) throw new AdminError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", { 1: "No student has this registration number." });
        const errors = this.check(p, []);
        if (Object.keys(errors).length) throw new AdminError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", errors);
        const mine = (await rowsOf("quiz")).filter((q) => (q[1] || "").toUpperCase() === reg);
        return [lagosTimestamp(), reg, stu[4] || "", p[3], p[4], p[5], p[6], String(mine.length + 1), "admin", "{}", new Date().toISOString()];
      },
    },

    progress: {
      label: "Handbook progress",
      help: "Which handbook chapters each student has marked as read (00 is the introduction, 01 to 12 the chapters). Count and percent follow the chapters you list.",
      editable: [2],
      selects: {},
      placeholders: { 2: "e.g. 00, 01, 02" },
      newestFirst: false,
      canDelete: true,
      create: { label: "Add progress", help: "Choose a registered student who has no progress row yet, and list the chapters read, e.g. 00, 01, 02. Leave it empty for none.", fields: [0, 2] },
      deleteWarning: "This resets the student's handbook progress to nothing.",
      check(p) {
        const tokens = String(p[2] == null ? "" : p[2]).split(/[\s,;]+/).filter(Boolean);
        const bad = tokens.filter((t) => !handbook.IDS.includes(/^\d{1,2}$/.test(t) ? t.padStart(2, "0") : t));
        if (bad.length) return { 2: "Not a chapter: " + bad.slice(0, 5).join(", ") + ". Use " + handbook.IDS[0] + " to " + handbook.IDS[handbook.IDS.length - 1] + "." };
        const sum = progress.summary(progress.parseRead(tokens.join(",")));
        p[2] = sum.read.join(",");
        p[3] = String(sum.count); p[4] = String(sum.total); p[5] = String(sum.percent); p[6] = lagosTimestamp();
        return {};
      },
      async prepare(p) {
        const reg = normReg(p[0]);
        if (!REG_RE.test(reg)) throw new AdminError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", { 0: "Enter a registration number like ABECFX/26/0001." });
        const stu = await findStudent(reg);
        if (!stu) throw new AdminError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", { 0: "No student has this registration number." });
        if ((await rowsOf("progress")).some((r) => (r[0] || "").toUpperCase() === reg)) {
          throw new AdminError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", { 0: "This student already has a progress row. Edit that row instead." });
        }
        const row = [reg, stu[4] || "", p[2] || "", "", "", "", ""];
        const e = this.check(row); // fills in count, total, percent and time
        if (Object.keys(e).length) throw new AdminError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", { 2: e[2] });
        return row;
      },
    },

    links: {
      label: "Site links",
      help: "The icons and text shown on the website. The rows are fixed (the website looks them up by name), so only the Value can be changed, with the same checks as the Site links tab.",
      editable: [2],
      selects: {},
      multiline: [2],
      newestFirst: false,
      check(p, cur) {
        const field = links.fieldFor(cur[0]);
        const key = field ? field.key : "";
        if (!field) return { 2: "This row is not one of the website's links, so it cannot be changed here." };
        const out = links.sanitize({ [key]: p[2] });
        if (out.errors[key]) return { 2: out.errors[key] };
        p[2] = out.values[key];
        return {};
      },
    },

    videos: {
      label: "Chapter videos",
      help: "The YouTube video for each handbook chapter. The rows are fixed (one per chapter), so only the address can be changed, with the same checks as the Chapter videos tab.",
      editable: [2],
      selects: {},
      placeholders: { 2: "https://youtu.be/..." },
      newestFirst: false,
      check(p, cur) {
        const key = (cur[0] || "").trim().padStart(2, "0");
        if (!videos.VIDEO_CHAPTERS.some((c) => c.id === key)) return { 2: "This row is not one of the handbook chapters, so it cannot be changed here." };
        const out = videos.sanitize({ [key]: p[2] });
        if (out.errors[key]) return { 2: out.errors[key] };
        p[2] = out.values[key];
        return {};
      },
    },
  };

  // Students first: they are what most editing is about.
  const ORDER = ["students", "codes", "quiz", "progress", "links", "videos"];

  function tabOf(key) {
    if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(TABS, key)) throw new AdminError(404, "That table could not be found.");
    return TABS[key];
  }

  function rowNumber(n) {
    const v = Number(n);
    if (!Number.isInteger(v) || v < 2 || v > 1000000) throw new AdminError(400, "That row could not be found.");
    return v;
  }

  function describe(key) {
    const t = TABS[key];
    const c = t.create;
    return {
      key,
      label: t.label,
      help: t.help,
      headers: store.EDITOR_TABS[key].headers,
      editable: t.editable,
      selects: t.selects || {},
      multiline: t.multiline || [],
      placeholders: t.placeholders || {},
      add: c ? { label: c.label, help: c.help, mode: c.mode || "form", fields: c.fields || [], defaults: c.defaults || {} } : null,
      canDelete: Boolean(t.canDelete || t.cascade || t.deleteWarning),
      deleteWarning: t.deleteWarning || "",
      newestFirst: Boolean(t.newestFirst),
    };
  }

  function assertUnchanged(current, original, width) {
    const ok = Array.isArray(original) && current.length === width && current.every((c, i) => same(c, original[i]));
    if (!ok) {
      throw new AdminError(409, "This row was changed or removed since you opened it. The latest version has been loaded.");
    }
  }

  async function otherRows(key, skipN) {
    return (await store.tabRows(key)).filter((r) => r.n !== skipN).map((r) => r.cells);
  }

  // Every place a registration number is stored besides the Students tab: [tab, column index].
  const REG_REFS = [["photos", 0], ["signatures", 0], ["dobchanges", 0], ["quiz", 1], ["progress", 0], ["codes", 2], ["quizcodes", 3], ["quizcodes", 5]];

  // A new number must not already appear anywhere as someone else's (including a deleted student's records).
  async function regConflict(newReg) {
    for (const [key, col] of REG_REFS) {
      const rows = await rowsOf(key);
      if (rows.some((r) => String((r && r[col]) || "").trim().toUpperCase() === newReg)) return store.EDITOR_TABS[key].title || key;
    }
    return "";
  }

  // Move everything that belongs to a registration number over to the new one. If a step fails, the steps
  // already done are put back, so a student is never left half-renamed.
  async function renameReg(oldReg, newReg) {
    const done = [];
    try {
      for (const [key, col] of REG_REFS) {
        await store.renameRegCells(key, col, oldReg, newReg);
        done.push([key, col]);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error("Registration number change failed, undoing:", e.message);
      for (const [key, col] of done.reverse()) {
        try { await store.renameRegCells(key, col, newReg, oldReg); } catch (e2) { console.error("Could not undo", key, e2.message); }
      }
      throw new AdminError(500, "The registration number could not be changed everywhere, so nothing was changed. Please try again.");
    }
  }

  return {
    TABS,
    ORDER,

    // What the dashboard needs to draw the table picker and the forms.
    meta() {
      return { sheets: ORDER.map(describe), sheetUrl: store.sheetUrl ? store.sheetUrl() : "" };
    },

    async read(key) {
      tabOf(key);
      return { ...describe(key), rows: await store.tabRows(key) };
    },

    async update(key, n, original, values) {
      const t = tabOf(key);
      if (!t.editable.length) throw new AdminError(403, "This table cannot be edited here.");
      n = rowNumber(n);
      const width = store.EDITOR_TABS[key].headers.length;
      const current = await store.tabRow(key, n);
      assertUnchanged(current, original, width);

      const p = current.slice();
      const errors = {};
      for (const [k, raw] of Object.entries(values && typeof values === "object" ? values : {})) {
        const i = Number(k);
        if (!Number.isInteger(i) || i < 0 || i >= width) continue;
        const v = String(raw == null ? "" : raw).slice(0, MAX_CELL);
        if (t.editable.includes(i)) p[i] = v;
        else if (!same(v, current[i])) errors[i] = "This column is locked and cannot be changed here.";
      }
      if (!Object.keys(errors).length) {
        Object.assign(errors, t.check(p, current, t.needsRows ? await otherRows(key, n) : []));
      }
      if (Object.keys(errors).length) throw new AdminError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", errors);

      const changes = {};
      p.forEach((v, i) => { if (v !== current[i]) changes[i] = v; });
      if (!Object.keys(changes).length) return { row: current, changed: false };
      const oldReg = String(current[0] || "").trim().toUpperCase();
      const renaming = key === "students" && changes[0] !== undefined && oldReg;
      if (renaming) {
        const where = await regConflict(p[0]);
        if (where) throw new AdminError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", { 0: "This number is already recorded for someone else (in " + where + "), so it cannot be used." });
        await renameReg(oldReg, p[0]);
      }
      try {
        await store.writeTabCells(key, n, changes);
      } catch (e) {
        if (renaming) await renameReg(p[0], oldReg).catch(() => {});
        throw e;
      }
      return { row: p, changed: true };
    },

    // body: { text } for the codes table, { values: { columnIndex: text } } for everything else.
    async create(key, body) {
      const t = tabOf(key);
      const c = t.create;
      if (!c) throw new AdminError(403, "Rows cannot be added to this table here.");
      const b = body && typeof body === "object" ? body : {};
      if (c.mode === "text") {
        const rows = t.prepareText(b.text, await otherRows(key, -1));
        await store.appendTabRows(key, rows);
        return { added: rows.length };
      }
      const width = store.EDITOR_TABS[key].headers.length;
      const p = Array(width).fill("");
      Object.entries(c.defaults || {}).forEach(([i, v]) => { p[Number(i)] = v; });
      for (const [k, raw] of Object.entries(b.values && typeof b.values === "object" ? b.values : {})) {
        const i = Number(k);
        if (!c.fields.includes(i)) continue; // anything the form does not offer is ignored
        const v = String(raw == null ? "" : raw).slice(0, MAX_CELL);
        if (v.trim() || !(c.defaults && c.defaults[i])) p[i] = v;
      }
      let row;
      if (t.prepare) {
        row = await t.prepare(p);
      } else {
        // every column counts as "changed" when creating, so all of them are checked
        const errors = t.check(p, Array(width).fill(null), t.needsRows ? await otherRows(key, -1) : []);
        if (Object.keys(errors).length) throw new AdminError(400, "Please fix the highlighted fields.", errors);
        row = await t.build(p);
      }
      await store.appendTabRows(key, [row]);
      return { added: 1, row, ...(key === "students" ? { reg: row[0] } : {}) };
    },

    async remove(key, n, original) {
      const t = tabOf(key);
      if (!(t.canDelete || t.cascade || t.deleteWarning)) throw new AdminError(403, "Rows cannot be deleted from this table here.");
      n = rowNumber(n);
      const width = store.EDITOR_TABS[key].headers.length;
      const current = await store.tabRow(key, n);
      assertUnchanged(current, original, width);
      if (t.cascade) await t.cascade(current);
      await store.deleteTabRow(key, n);
      return { ok: true };
    },
  };
}

module.exports = { create, AdminError };
```

#### `idrules.js`

```js
// ID card rules: who gets an expiry date, and the date maths behind it.
// An ID card is one of four kinds: Uncertified, Certified, Team Member or Alumni Member.
// Certified students are graduates (their ID card says "Certified"). Only Uncertified students have IDs
// that are valid for 2 months from the issue date. Certified, Team Member and Alumni Member have no expiry.
// "Graduate" is no longer a status of its own. Rows that still hold it in the sheet are read as Certified.
// Old Mentor, Staff Member and CEO rows are read as Team Member.
const LEGACY_STATUS = { graduate: "Certified", mentor: "Team Member", "staff member": "Team Member", ceo: "Team Member" };
function normalizeStatus(v) {
  const s = String(v == null ? "" : v).trim();
  return LEGACY_STATUS[s.toLowerCase()] || s;
}

const MONTHS = ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"];
const EXPIRING_STATUSES = ["Uncertified"];
const VALID_MONTHS = 2;
const ISO = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;

const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0");

// Today's date in Lagos as YYYY-MM-DD.
function lagosToday(now = new Date()) {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", { timeZone: "Africa/Lagos" }).format(now);
}

// Issue date as YYYY-MM-DD. Uses the "ID Issue Date" column when it holds one; otherwise reads the
// "13 Oct 2026, 14:20:11" text in "Registered At", so students registered before this column existed still work.
function parseIssued(row) {
  const explicit = String(row[11] || "").trim();
  if (ISO.test(explicit)) return explicit;
  return parseRegistered(row);
}

// Date the student joined, as YYYY-MM-DD, read from "Registered At" ("13 Oct 2026, 14:20:11").
// Unlike the ID issue date, this never changes when an editor renews an ID.
function parseRegistered(row) {
  const m = /^(\d{1,2})\s+([A-Za-z]{3,})\.?\s+(\d{4})/.exec(String(row[1] || "").trim());
  if (m) {
    const mi = MONTHS.findIndex((x) => x.toLowerCase() === m[2].slice(0, 3).toLowerCase());
    if (mi >= 0) return `${m[3]}-${pad(mi + 1)}-${pad(Number(m[1]))}`;
  }
  return "";
}

// Add whole months, keeping the day of month where possible (31 Dec + 2 months = 28/29 Feb).
function addMonthsISO(iso, n) {
  const [y, m, d] = iso.split("-").map(Number);
  const t = m - 1 + n;
  const ny = y + Math.floor(t / 12);
  const nm = ((t % 12) + 12) % 12;
  const lastDay = new Date(Date.UTC(ny, nm + 1, 0)).getUTCDate();
  return `${ny}-${pad(nm + 1)}-${pad(Math.min(d, lastDay))}`;
}

function formatISO(iso) {
  const [y, m, d] = iso.split("-").map(Number);
  return `${pad(d)} ${MONTHS[m - 1]} ${y}`;
}

function daysBetween(fromIso, toIso) {
  return Math.round((Date.parse(toIso + "T00:00:00Z") - Date.parse(fromIso + "T00:00:00Z")) / 86400000);
}

// expiryKind: "none" (never expires), "date" (has an expiry date), "unknown" (should expire but no issue date).
// An ID is valid through its expiry date and counts as expired from the day after.
function expiryInfo(status, issuedISO, todayISO) {
  if (!EXPIRING_STATUSES.includes(status)) {
    return { expiryKind: "none", expires: "", expired: false, daysLeft: null };
  }
  if (!issuedISO) return { expiryKind: "unknown", expires: "", expired: false, daysLeft: null };
  const exp = addMonthsISO(issuedISO, VALID_MONTHS);
  const daysLeft = daysBetween(todayISO, exp);
  return { expiryKind: "date", expires: formatISO(exp), expired: daysLeft < 0, daysLeft };
}

module.exports = {
  normalizeStatus, lagosToday, parseIssued, parseRegistered, addMonthsISO, formatISO, daysBetween, expiryInfo, EXPIRING_STATUSES };
```

#### `grading.js`

```js
// A-F grading scale for quiz results. Change the numbers here and the whole site follows.
// Scale used (common Nigerian grading): A 70-100, B 60-69, C 50-59, D 45-49, E 40-44, F 0-39.
const SCALE = [
  { grade: "A", min: 70, label: "Excellent" },
  { grade: "B", min: 60, label: "Very good" },
  { grade: "C", min: 50, label: "Good" },
  { grade: "D", min: 45, label: "Fair" },
  { grade: "E", min: 40, label: "Pass" },
  { grade: "F", min: 0, label: "Fail" },
];

// pct is the whole-number percentage that is shown to the student (0-100).
function gradeFor(pct) {
  const p = Number.isFinite(pct) ? pct : 0;
  return SCALE.find((s) => p >= s.min) || SCALE[SCALE.length - 1];
}

function percent(score, total) {
  return total > 0 ? Math.round((score / total) * 100) : 0;
}

module.exports = { SCALE, gradeFor, percent };
```

#### `stats.js`

```js
// Statistics for the editor dashboard. Pure functions: rows in, numbers out.
// Students who registered before the rename have "Forex Mentorship" stored; they are shown as "Forex Course".
const programmeName = (v) => (/^forex mentorship$/i.test(String(v || "").trim()) ? "Forex Course" : String(v || ""));
// Student row columns: 0 reg, 1 registered at, 2 country, 3 programme, 4 name, 7 gender, 9 status, 12 state.
// Quiz row columns: 0 submitted at, 1 reg, 2 name, 3 score, 4 total, 5 percent, 6 grade, 7 attempt.
```
