#### `public/app.js`

```js
(function () {
  var form = document.getElementById("regForm");
  var btn = document.getElementById("submitBtn");
  var formErr = document.getElementById("formErr");
  var countrySel = document.getElementById("country");

  document.getElementById("year").textContent = new Date().getFullYear();

  // A student who registered on this device is taken back to their portal, not the form.
  var KEY = "abecfx_reg";
  try {
    var saved = localStorage.getItem(KEY);
    if (saved) {
      document.getElementById("returningReg").textContent = saved;
      document.getElementById("returning").hidden = false;
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  fetch("/api/countries")
    .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })
    .then(function (list) {
      list.forEach(function (c) {
        var o = document.createElement("option");
        o.value = c;
        o.textContent = c;
        countrySel.appendChild(o);
      });
    })
    .catch(function () {
      formErr.textContent = "Could not load the country list. Refresh the page to try again.";
    });


  // ---- State / region: appears once a country is chosen. A dropdown when we have a list, else a text box ----
  var stateField = document.getElementById("stateField");
  var stateSel = document.getElementById("state");
  var stateText = document.getElementById("stateText");
  var stateLabel = document.getElementById("stateLabel");
  var STATE_WORD = {
    "Nigeria": "State", "United States": "State", "India": "State", "Brazil": "State", "Australia": "State",
    "Mexico": "State", "Malaysia": "State", "Germany": "State", "Austria": "State", "Venezuela": "State",
    "Canada": "Province", "South Africa": "Province", "Argentina": "Province", "China": "Province",
    "Ghana": "Region", "Cameroon": "Region", "Tanzania": "Region", "Senegal": "Region", "Namibia": "Region"
  };
  var stateReq = 0;

  function useStateControl(sel) {
    stateSel.hidden = !sel;
    stateText.hidden = !!sel;
    stateLabel.setAttribute("for", sel ? "state" : "stateText");
  }

  countrySel.addEventListener("change", function () {
    var country = countrySel.value;
    var mine = ++stateReq;
    stateSel.replaceChildren();
    stateText.value = "";
    if (!country) { stateField.hidden = true; return; }
    var word = STATE_WORD[country] || "State / Region";
    stateLabel.textContent = "State of residence";
    stateText.placeholder = "State of residence";
    stateField.hidden = false;
    useStateControl(false); // text box until the list arrives
    fetch("/api/states?country=" + encodeURIComponent(country))
      .then(function (r) { return r.json(); })
      .then(function (list) {
        if (mine !== stateReq || !list.length) return;
        var first = document.createElement("option");
        first.value = ""; first.textContent = "Select your state of residence";
        stateSel.appendChild(first);
        list.forEach(function (s) {
          var o = document.createElement("option");
          o.value = s; o.textContent = s;
          stateSel.appendChild(o);
        });
        useStateControl(true);
      })
      .catch(function () {});
  });

  // ---- Passport photo: crop to 4:5, shrink to 320x400 JPEG so it fits in a Google Sheet cell ----
  var photoInput = document.getElementById("photo");
  var photoPick = document.getElementById("photoPick");
  var photoPreview = document.getElementById("photoPreview");
  var photoIcon = document.getElementById("photoIcon");
  var photoBtn = document.getElementById("photoBtn");
  var regNumberText = "";
  var termsLink = document.getElementById("termsLink");
  if (termsLink) termsLink.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    var det = document.getElementById("termsText");
    det.open = true;
    det.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "nearest" });
  });
  var sig = window.abecSignature.create(document.getElementById("sigMount"));
  var sigDataUrl = "";
  var photoB64 = "";
  var photoDataUrl = "";

  function setPhotoError(msg) {
    var span = form.querySelector('.err[data-for="photo"]');
    span.textContent = msg || "";
    span.closest(".field").classList.toggle("invalid", !!msg);
  }

  // Open the picked file as something we can draw. createImageBitmap needs no blob: address (the site's
  // security policy blocks those); the FileReader fallback uses a data: address, which is allowed.
  function openImage(file) {
    if (window.createImageBitmap) {
      return createImageBitmap(file).then(
        function (bmp) { return { src: bmp, w: bmp.width, h: bmp.height }; },
        function () { return openViaReader(file); }
      );
    }
    return openViaReader(file);
  }
  function openViaReader(file) {
    return new Promise(function (resolve, reject) {
      var fr = new FileReader();
      fr.onerror = function () { reject(new Error("read")); };
      fr.onload = function () {
        var img = new Image();
        img.onload = function () { resolve({ src: img, w: img.naturalWidth, h: img.naturalHeight }); };
        img.onerror = function () { reject(new Error("decode")); };
        img.src = fr.result;
      };
      fr.readAsDataURL(file);
    });
  }

  function processPhoto(file) {
    if (file.type && !/^image\//.test(file.type)) return Promise.reject(new Error("Choose an image file (JPG or PNG)."));
    if (file.size > 15 * 1024 * 1024) return Promise.reject(new Error("That photo is too large. Choose one under 15 MB."));
    return openImage(file).then(
      function (im) {
        var sw = im.w, sh = im.h;
        if (sw < 200 || sh < 250) throw new Error("That photo is too small. Choose a larger, clearer one.");
        // largest 4:5 area, biased toward the top so heads are not cut off
        var cw = sw, ch = sw * 1.25;
        if (ch > sh) { ch = sh; cw = sh * 0.8; }
        var sx = (sw - cw) / 2, sy = (sh - ch) * 0.25;
        var sizes = [[320, 400], [256, 320], [200, 250]];
        for (var i = 0; i < sizes.length; i++) {
          var c = document.createElement("canvas");
          c.width = sizes[i][0]; c.height = sizes[i][1];
          var ctx = c.getContext("2d");
          ctx.fillStyle = "#fff";
          ctx.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
          ctx.drawImage(im.src, sx, sy, cw, ch, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
          for (var q = 0.86; q >= 0.4; q -= 0.06) {
            var data = c.toDataURL("image/jpeg", q);
            if (data.length <= 42000) { if (im.src.close) im.src.close(); return data; }
          }
        }
        if (im.src.close) im.src.close();
        throw new Error("We could not shrink that photo. Try a different one.");
      },
      function () {
        throw new Error("We could not read that photo. Use a JPG or PNG. On iPhone, pick the photo again from your library or take a new one.");
      }
    );
  }

  photoInput.addEventListener("change", function () {
    var file = photoInput.files && photoInput.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    setPhotoError("");
    processPhoto(file)
      .then(function (dataUrl) {
        photoDataUrl = dataUrl;
        photoB64 = dataUrl.slice(dataUrl.indexOf(",") + 1);
        photoPreview.src = dataUrl;
        photoPreview.hidden = false;
        photoIcon.style.display = "none";
        photoPick.classList.add("has-photo");
        photoBtn.textContent = "Change photo";
      })
      .catch(function (e) {
        photoB64 = ""; photoDataUrl = "";
        photoPreview.hidden = true;
        photoIcon.style.display = "";
        photoPick.classList.remove("has-photo");
        photoBtn.textContent = "Choose photo";
        photoInput.value = "";
        setPhotoError(e.message);
      });
  });

  // The date picker cannot go past today.
  (function () {
    var dob = document.getElementById("dob");
    if (!dob) return;
    var t = new Date();
    dob.max = t.getFullYear() + "-" + String(t.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0") + "-" + String(t.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
  })();

  function clearErrors() {
    formErr.textContent = "";
    form.querySelectorAll(".err").forEach(function (e) { e.textContent = ""; });
    form.querySelectorAll(".field").forEach(function (f) { f.classList.remove("invalid"); });
  }

  function showErrors(errors) {
    Object.keys(errors).forEach(function (key) {
      var span = form.querySelector('.err[data-for="' + key + '"]');
      if (span) {
        span.textContent = errors[key];
        span.closest(".field").classList.add("invalid");
      }
    });
    var first = form.querySelector(".field.invalid input, .field.invalid select");
    if (first) first.focus();
  }

  form.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    clearErrors();

    var data = {};
    new FormData(form).forEach(function (v, k) { if (k !== "photo") data[k] = v; });
    data.state = stateField.hidden ? "" : (stateSel.hidden ? stateText.value : stateSel.value);
    data.terms = !!document.getElementById("terms").checked;
    data.photo = photoB64; // the server reports a missing photo together with any other errors

    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.textContent = "Registering...";
    var sigReady = sig.value();

    // The signature is made first. If it is missing the server reports it together with any other errors.
    sigReady.then(function (sigUrl) {
      sigDataUrl = sigUrl;
      sig.setError(""); // the form shows one message for a missing signature, not two
      data.signature = sigUrl ? sigUrl.slice(sigUrl.indexOf(",") + 1) : "";
      return fetch("/api/register", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(data),
      });
    })
      .then(function (r) {
        return r.json().then(function (body) { return { ok: r.ok, body: body }; });
      })
      .then(function (res) {
        if (res.ok) {
          var first = (res.body.fullName || "").split(" ")[0];
          document.getElementById("welcome").textContent = first
            ? "You're registered, " + first
            : "You're registered";
          document.getElementById("cardMount").replaceChildren(
            window.renderStudentCard(res.body.card, { photoSrc: photoDataUrl, signatureSrc: sigDataUrl })
          );
          regNumberText = res.body.regNumber;
          try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, res.body.regNumber); if (res.body.token) localStorage.setItem("abecfx_token", res.body.token); } catch (e) {}
          document.getElementById("returning").hidden = true;
          document.getElementById("formCard").hidden = true;
          document.getElementById("successCard").hidden = false;
          window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
        } else if (res.body.errors) {
          showErrors(res.body.errors);
        } else {
          formErr.textContent = res.body.error || "Something went wrong. Please try again.";
        }
      })
      .catch(function () {
        formErr.textContent = "Network problem. Check your connection and try again.";
      })
      .then(function () {
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.textContent = "Register";
      });
  });


  document.getElementById("copyBtn").addEventListener("click", function () {
    var text = regNumberText;
    var b = this;
    function done() {
      b.textContent = "Copied";
      setTimeout(function () { b.textContent = "Copy number"; }, 1800);
    }
    if (navigator.clipboard) navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(done, done);
    else done();
  });
})();
```

#### `public/portal.html`

```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8" />
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
  <title>Student Portal | ABEC-FX</title>
  <meta name="description" content="Your ABEC-FX student portal: your ID card, status and expiry date in one place." />
  <meta name="theme-color" content="#5a1060" />
  <link rel="icon" type="image/png" href="/favicon.png" />
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="/styles.css" />
</head>
<body>
  <div class="stage">
    <aside class="panel">
      <a class="brandbar" href="/" aria-label="ABEC-FX home">
        <img src="/logo-white.png" alt="" />
        <span><span class="name">ABEC-FX</span><span class="tag">Adebass Base Edge Consult</span></span>
      </a>

      <div class="pitch">
        <h1>Your student portal.</h1>
        <p>Your ID card, quiz and Handbook in one place.</p>
      </div>

      <nav class="nav" aria-label="Main">
        <a href="/" aria-current="page"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="3" y="5" width="18" height="14" rx="2.5"/><circle cx="9" cy="11" r="2"/><path d="M6.5 16c.6-1.4 1.6-2 2.5-2s1.9.6 2.5 2M14 10h4M14 13h3"/></svg><span>My portal</span></a>
        <a href="/register"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="10" cy="8" r="3.5"/><path d="M3.5 20c0-3.6 2.9-6 6.5-6M17 11v6M14 14h6"/></svg><span>Register</span></a>
        <a href="/status"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="6.5"/><path d="M16 16l4.5 4.5"/></svg><span>Find a student</span></a>
      </nav>

      <div class="social" id="siteLinks" hidden></div>
    </aside>

    <main class="sheet">
      <div class="sheet-inner">
        <p class="live" id="loading" role="status">Opening your portal...</p>

        <!-- Not signed in on this device -->
        <section id="signin" hidden>
          <h2>Open your student portal</h2>
          <p class="lead">The student portal is for registered students. Sign in with the registration number you received and the email address you registered with.</p>
          <form id="signinForm" novalidate>
            <div class="field">
              <label for="reg">Registration number</label>
              <input id="reg" type="text" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="characters" spellcheck="false" placeholder="ABECFX/26/0001" required />
            </div>
            <div class="field">
              <label for="email">Registered email</label>
              <input id="email" type="email" autocomplete="email" spellcheck="false" placeholder="you@example.com" required />
            </div>
            <p class="formErr" id="signinErr" role="alert"></p>
            <button type="submit" id="signinBtn" class="primary">Sign in</button>
          </form>
          <p class="or">New to ABEC-FX?</p>
          <a class="btn ghost wide" href="/register">Register as a new student</a>
          <p class="or">Just want to check a student's ID?</p>
          <a class="btn ghost wide" href="/status">Find a student</a>
        </section>

        <!-- Signed in -->
        <section id="home" hidden>
          <!-- My portal submenu -->
          <nav class="subnav" id="subnav" aria-label="My portal">
            <a href="#/card" data-view="card" aria-current="page">ID card</a>
            <a href="#/community" data-view="community">Community</a>
            <a href="#/handbook" data-view="handbook">Handbook</a>
            <a href="#/workbook" data-view="workbook">Workbook</a>
            <a href="#/quiz" data-view="quiz">Quiz</a>
          </nav>

          <!-- View: ID card -->
          <div id="viewCard" class="view">
            <h2 id="welcome">Welcome back</h2>
            <p class="lead">This is your ID card.</p>
            <div class="cardwrap" id="cardMount"></div>
            <p class="live" id="liveLine" role="status"></p>
            <div class="pass-actions">
              <button type="button" id="downloadBtn" class="btn ghost">Download ID card</button>
            </div>

            <!-- Certificate: only for Certified students, shown straight under the ID card -->
            <section class="cert-block" id="certBlock" aria-labelledby="certHead" hidden>
              <h3 id="certHead">Your certificate</h3>
              <p class="cert-note">Congratulations on completing the course. This is your certificate of completion.</p>
              <div id="certMount"></div>
              <div class="cert-actions">
                <button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="certPng">Download certificate (PNG)</button>
                <button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="certPdf">Download certificate (PDF)</button>
              </div>
              <p class="formErr" id="certMsg" role="status"></p>
            </section>
            <div class="photo-edit" id="photoEdit">
              <input id="newPhoto" type="file" accept="image/*" class="sr-only" aria-label="Choose a new passport photo" />
              <label for="newPhoto" class="btn ghost wide" id="newPhotoBtn">Change my photo (once only)</label>
              <p class="formErr" id="photoMsg" role="status"></p>
            </div>
            <div class="sig-edit" id="sigEdit">
              <button type="button" class="btn ghost wide" id="sigToggle">Add my signature</button>
              <div id="sigPanel" hidden>
                <div id="sigPortalMount"></div>
                <button type="button" class="btn primary wide" id="sigSave">Save my signature</button>
              </div>
              <p class="formErr" id="sigMsg" role="status"></p>
            </div>

            <a class="journey" id="journeyCard" href="#/handbook" aria-label="Open the Handbook">
              <span class="journey-top"><strong>Your learning journey</strong><span class="journey-pct" id="journeyPct">&ndash;</span></span>
              <span class="pbar" aria-hidden="true"><span class="pbar-fill" id="journeyFill"></span></span>
              <span class="journey-sub" id="journeySub">Loading your progress...</span>
            </a>
          </div>

          <!-- View: Quiz -->
          <div id="viewQuiz" class="view" hidden>
            <h2>Quiz</h2>
            <p class="lead">Test what you have learned in the Handbook.</p>
            <!-- The quiz is locked until the student enters a one-time quiz code -->
            <section class="quizcard quizgate" id="quizGate" aria-labelledby="quizGateTitle" hidden>
              <h3 id="quizGateTitle">Unlock the quiz</h3>
              <p class="quizgate-note" id="quizGateNote"></p>
              <form id="quizUnlockForm" novalidate>
                <div class="field">
                  <label for="quizCode">Quiz code</label>
                  <input id="quizCode" type="text" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="characters" spellcheck="false" maxlength="24" placeholder="QZ-XXXX-XXXX" required />
                </div>
                <p class="formErr" id="quizCodeErr" role="alert"></p>
                <button type="submit" id="quizUnlockBtn" class="primary">Unlock quiz</button>
              </form>
              <p class="quizgate-foot">A code works for one quiz. It stays open until you finish the quiz, then you need a new code to take it again.</p>
            </section>

            <section class="quizcard" id="quizCard" aria-labelledby="quizTitle">
              <h3 id="quizTitle">Quiz results</h3>
              <div id="quizBody" class="quizbody"><p class="live" role="status">Loading your results...</p></div>
              <p class="quiz-open" id="quizOpenNote" hidden>Your quiz is unlocked. It stays open until you finish it.</p>
              <button type="button" id="quizStartBtn" class="btn primary wide" hidden>Take the quiz here</button>
              <a id="quizLink" class="btn ghost wide" href="#" target="_blank" rel="noopener" hidden>Take the quiz on Telegram</a>
            </section>

            <!-- The quiz runs here, inside the portal -->
            <section class="quizrun" id="quizRun" aria-labelledby="quizRunTitle" hidden>
              <div class="quizrun-bar">
                <strong id="quizRunTitle">Edge Handbook Quiz</strong>
                <button type="button" id="quizCloseBtn" class="btn ghost">Close quiz</button>
              </div>
              <p class="live" id="quizRunMsg" role="status">Opening the quiz. The first load can take up to a minute.</p>
              <iframe id="quizFrame" title="ABEC-FX Edge Handbook quiz" sandbox="allow-scripts allow-same-origin" referrerpolicy="no-referrer"></iframe>
              <p class="quizrun-foot"><a id="quizTab" href="#" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Quiz not loading? Open it in a new tab</a></p>
            </section>
          </div>

          <!-- View: Community (the WhatsApp and Telegram groups, set on the dashboard's Community tab) -->
          <div id="viewCommunity" class="view" hidden>
            <h2>Community</h2>
            <p class="lead">Open the ABEC-FX community to learn alongside other students and get updates from your mentors.</p>
            <section class="quizcard communitycard" aria-label="Community links">
              <p class="live" id="communityMsg" role="status">Loading...</p>
              <a id="communityWhatsapp" class="btn primary wide" href="#" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" hidden>Open WhatsApp community</a>
              <a id="communityTelegram" class="btn ghost wide" href="#" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" hidden>Open Telegram community</a>
            </section>
          </div>

          <!-- View: Handbook -->
          <div id="viewHandbook" class="view" hidden>
            <div id="hbMount"><p class="live" role="status">Opening the handbook...</p></div>
          </div>

          <!-- View: Workbook -->
          <div id="viewWorkbook" class="view" hidden>
            <div id="wbMount"><p class="live" role="status">Opening the workbooks...</p></div>
          </div>

          <div class="signout-row">
            <button type="button" id="switchBtn" class="btn ghost">Sign out</button>
          </div>
        </section>

        <!-- Age requirement notice: shown to a signed-in student whose saved date of birth is too recent -->
        <dialog id="dobDlg" class="dobdlg" aria-labelledby="dobTitle">
          <h2 id="dobTitle">Please update your date of birth</h2>
          <p id="dobText"></p>
          <div id="dobStart">
            <button type="button" class="btn primary wide" id="dobOpenForm">Update my date of birth</button>
            <button type="button" class="btn ghost wide" id="dobSignOut">Sign out</button>
          </div>
          <form id="dobForm" hidden novalidate>
            <p class="dob-warn" role="note"><strong>You can only update your date of birth once.</strong> Make sure it matches your documents before you save.</p>
            <label for="dobInput">New date of birth</label>
            <input id="dobInput" type="date" min="1920-01-01" autocomplete="bday" required />
            <p class="formErr" id="dobMsg" role="alert"></p>
            <button type="submit" class="btn primary wide" id="dobSave">Save my date of birth</button>
            <button type="button" class="btn ghost wide" id="dobBack">Back</button>
          </form>
        </dialog>

        <!-- Photo notice: shown to a signed-in student a mentor has asked to send a new passport photo -->
        <dialog id="photoDlg" class="dobdlg" aria-labelledby="photoDlgTitle">
          <h2 id="photoDlgTitle">Please send a passport photograph</h2>
          <p>The system has noticed that your photograph does not meet our requirements. Please upload a clear passport-size photograph of your face (facing the camera, plain background, no hat or sunglasses, no full-body or group pictures) to continue enjoying our services.</p>
          <div id="photoDlgStart">
            <input id="photoDlgFile" type="file" accept="image/*" class="sr-only" aria-label="Choose a passport photograph" />
            <label for="photoDlgFile" class="btn primary wide" id="photoDlgPick">Choose passport photo</label>
            <button type="button" class="btn ghost wide" id="photoDlgSignOut">Sign out</button>
          </div>
          <div id="photoDlgForm" hidden>
            <p class="dob-warn" role="note"><strong>You can only do this once.</strong> Check that the photo below is a clear passport photo before you save.</p>
            <img id="photoDlgPreview" class="photo-dlg-preview" alt="Preview of your new passport photograph" />
            <p class="formErr" id="photoDlgMsg" role="alert"></p>
            <button type="button" class="btn primary wide" id="photoDlgSave">Save this photo</button>
            <label for="photoDlgFile" class="btn ghost wide">Choose a different photo</label>
          </div>
        </dialog>

        <!-- Signature notice: shown to a signed-in student who has no signature on file -->
        <dialog id="sigDlg" class="dobdlg" aria-labelledby="sigDlgTitle">
          <h2 id="sigDlgTitle">Please add your signature</h2>
          <p id="sigDlgText">The system has noticed that you have not added your signature. Your signature appears on your ID card. Please add it now to continue enjoying our services.</p>
          <div id="sigDlgStart">
            <button type="button" class="btn primary wide" id="sigDlgOpen">Add my signature</button>
            <button type="button" class="btn ghost wide" id="sigDlgSignOut">Sign out</button>
          </div>
          <div id="sigDlgForm" hidden>
            <div id="sigDlgMount"></div>
            <p class="formErr" id="sigDlgMsg" role="alert"></p>
            <button type="button" class="btn primary wide" id="sigDlgSave">Save my signature</button>
            <button type="button" class="btn ghost wide" id="sigDlgBack">Back</button>
          </div>
        </dialog>

        <p class="sheet-foot">&copy; <span id="year"></span> Adebass Base Edge Consult</p>
      </div>
    </main>
  </div>
  <script src="/nav.js"></script>
  <script src="/links.js"></script>
  <script src="/qr.js"></script>
  <script src="/card.js"></script>
  <script src="/download.js"></script>
  <script src="/certificate-art.js"></script>
  <script src="/certificate.js"></script>
  <script src="/handbook.js"></script>
  <script src="/workbook.js"></script>
  <script src="/photo.js"></script>
  <script src="/signature.js"></script>
  <script src="/portal.js"></script>
</body>
</html>
```

#### `public/portal.js`

```js
(function () {
  var KEY = "abecfx_reg";
  var TKEY = "abecfx_token"; // sign-in token from the website; the portal is private without it
  var loading = document.getElementById("loading");
  var signin = document.getElementById("signin");
  var home = document.getElementById("home");
  var form = document.getElementById("signinForm");
  var input = document.getElementById("reg");
  var emailInput = document.getElementById("email");
  var err = document.getElementById("signinErr");
  var btn = document.getElementById("signinBtn");

  document.getElementById("year").textContent = new Date().getFullYear();

  // The quiz opens inside the portal (a frame on the quiz service) so students never leave this page.
  var currentReg = "";
  var quizOrigin = "";
  var quizRun = document.getElementById("quizRun");
  var quizFrame = document.getElementById("quizFrame");
  var quizMsg = document.getElementById("quizRunMsg");
  var quizStartBtn = document.getElementById("quizStartBtn");

  // The quiz is locked until the student enters a one-time quiz code. The website decides (/api/results says
  // `unlocked`); the portal only shows the code box or the quiz buttons to match.
  var quizCfg = null;           // { quizUrl, quizEmbed } from the website, loaded once
  var quizUnlocked = false;     // does this student have a quiz code that is still open?
  var quizAccessKnown = false;  // has the website answered yet? Until it has, neither the code box nor the quiz is shown
  var quizJustFinished = false; // a quiz was saved from the frame on this page
  var quizGate = document.getElementById("quizGate");
  var quizGateNote = document.getElementById("quizGateNote");
  var quizOpenNote = document.getElementById("quizOpenNote");
  var quizLinkEl = document.getElementById("quizLink");
  var GATE_NOTE = "The quiz is locked. Enter the one-time quiz code your mentor or ABEC-FX gave you to open it.";
  var GATE_NOTE_DONE = "You have finished the quiz. To take it again, enter a new one-time quiz code.";

  function remember(reg, token) {
    try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, reg); if (token) localStorage.setItem(TKEY, token); } catch (e) {}
    window.abecNav.refresh(); // signed in: the menu no longer offers "Register"
  }
  function forget() { try { localStorage.removeItem(KEY); localStorage.removeItem(TKEY); } catch (e) {} window.abecNav.refresh(); }
  // After a photo change the browser may still hold the old /api/photo for a few minutes, so the card asks
  // for the photo with a version that changes whenever the student saves a new one.
  function photoUrl(reg) {
    var v = "";
    try { v = localStorage.getItem("abecfx_photo_v") || ""; } catch (e) {}
    return v ? "/api/photo?reg=" + encodeURIComponent(reg) + "&v=" + v : undefined;
  }
  function saved() { try { return localStorage.getItem(KEY) || ""; } catch (e) { return ""; } }
  function token() { try { return localStorage.getItem(TKEY) || ""; } catch (e) { return ""; } }

  // Every private request carries the sign-in token.
  function authFetch(url, opts) {
    var o = opts || {};
    var headers = Object.assign({}, o.headers || {}, { Authorization: "Bearer " + token() });
    return fetch(url, Object.assign({}, o, { headers: headers }));
  }

  // The sign-in has expired or is not valid: back to the sign-in form.
  function signedOut(message) {
    closeQuiz(true);
    window.abecHandbook.reset(); window.abecWorkbook.reset();
    currentReg = "";
    currentView = "";
    forget();
    showSignin(message || "Please sign in again.");
  }

  function login(reg, email) {
    return fetch("/api/portal/login", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ reg: reg, email: email }),
    }).then(function (r) {
      return r.json().then(function (b) { return { ok: r.ok, status: r.status, body: b }; });
    });
  }

  function myCard() {
    return authFetch("/api/portal/me").then(function (r) {
      return r.json().then(function (b) { return { ok: r.ok, status: r.status, body: b }; });
    });
  }

  function showSignin(message) {
    var dd = document.getElementById("dobDlg"); if (dd && dd.open) dd.close();
    var sd = document.getElementById("sigDlg"); if (sd && sd.open) sd.close();
    var pd = document.getElementById("photoDlg"); if (pd && pd.open) pd.close();
    loading.hidden = true;
    home.hidden = true;
    signin.hidden = false;
    err.textContent = message || "";
  }

  function showHome(d) {
    loading.hidden = true;
    signin.hidden = true;
    var first = (d.fullName || "").split(" ")[0];
    document.getElementById("welcome").textContent = first ? "Welcome back, " + first : "Welcome back";
    mySig = "";
    sigNote("");
    pendingSigNotice = !!d.signatureMissing;
    pendingPhotoNotice = !!d.photoRedo;
    showDobNotice(d.dobNotice);
    if (!d.dobNotice) nextNotice();
    document.getElementById("cardMount").replaceChildren(window.renderStudentCard(d, { photoSrc: photoUrl(d.regNumber) }));
    loadSignature(d);
    loadCertificate();
    var t = new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" });
    var note = window.expiryNotice(d);
    var live = document.getElementById("liveLine");
    live.textContent = note ? note.text : "Live record, checked at " + t;
    live.className = "live" + (note ? " live-" + note.level : "");
    setPhotoEditable(!d.photoChanged);
    photoNote("");
    home.hidden = false;
    currentReg = d.regNumber;
    closeQuiz(true);
    window.abecHandbook.reset(); window.abecWorkbook.reset();
    currentView = "";
    route();
  }

  // ---- My portal submenu: ID card, Quiz, Handbook (addresses #/card, #/quiz, #/handbook/03) ----
  var VIEWS = ["card", "quiz", "handbook", "workbook", "community"];
  var currentView = "";

  function parseRoute() {
    var m = /^#\/(card|quiz|handbook|workbook|community)(?:\/(\d{2}))?$/.exec(location.hash || "");
    return { view: m ? m[1] : "card", chapter: m && m[2] ? m[2] : "" };
  }

  function route() {
    if (home.hidden || !currentReg) return; // not signed in: the sign-in form stays
    var r = parseRoute();
    VIEWS.forEach(function (v) {
      document.getElementById("view" + v.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + v.slice(1)).hidden = v !== r.view;
    });
    var links = document.querySelectorAll("#subnav a");
    for (var i = 0; i < links.length; i++) {
      if (links[i].getAttribute("data-view") === r.view) links[i].setAttribute("aria-current", "page");
      else links[i].removeAttribute("aria-current");
    }
    document.querySelector(".sheet-inner").classList.toggle("is-wide", r.view === "handbook" || r.view === "workbook");
    if (r.view === "card" && currentView !== "card") window.abecHandbook.summary();
    if (r.view === "quiz" && currentView !== "quiz") showQuiz(currentReg);
    if (r.view === "community" && currentView !== "community") showCommunity();
    if (r.view === "handbook") window.abecHandbook.show(r.chapter);
    if (r.view === "workbook") window.abecWorkbook.show();
    currentView = r.view;
    if (r.view !== "handbook" || !r.chapter) window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  }
  window.addEventListener("hashchange", route);
  window.abecHandbook.setup({ authFetch: function (u, o) { return authFetch(u, o); }, signedOut: function (m) { signedOut(m); } });
  window.abecWorkbook.setup({ authFetch: function (u, o) { return authFetch(u, o); }, signedOut: function (m) { signedOut(m); } });

  // ---- Community: the WhatsApp and Telegram groups set on the dashboard, opened in a new tab ----
  function safeHttps(u) {
    try { var x = new URL(String(u || "")); return x.protocol === "https:" ? x.href : ""; } catch (e) { return ""; }
  }
  function showCommunity() {
    var msg = document.getElementById("communityMsg");
    var wa = document.getElementById("communityWhatsapp");
    var tg = document.getElementById("communityTelegram");
    msg.textContent = "Loading...";
    msg.hidden = false;
    wa.hidden = true;
    tg.hidden = true;
    authFetch("/api/community")
      .then(function (r) { return r.json().then(function (b) { if (r.status === 401) { signedOut(); throw new Error("signed out"); } if (!r.ok) throw new Error(b.error); return b; }); })
      .then(function (c) {
        var w = safeHttps(c.whatsapp), t = safeHttps(c.telegram);
        if (w) { wa.href = w; wa.hidden = false; }
        if (t) { tg.href = t; tg.hidden = false; }
        // With only one choice, that one is the main button.
        if (!w && t) tg.className = "btn primary wide";
        else tg.className = "btn ghost wide";
        msg.hidden = !!(w || t);
        if (!w && !t) msg.textContent = "The community link is coming soon. Please check back.";
      })
      .catch(function (e) {
        if (e && e.message === "signed out") return;
        msg.textContent = "We could not load the community links just now. Refresh to try again.";
      });
  }

  // ---- Quiz results (taken in the ABEC-FX Telegram quiz bot) ----
  function el(tag, cls, text) {
    var e = document.createElement(tag);
    if (cls) e.className = cls;
    if (text != null) e.textContent = text;
    return e;
  }

  // Locked: only the code box. Unlocked: the ways to take the quiz. Nothing is offered until we know which.
  function hideQuizActions() {
    quizGate.hidden = true;
    quizOpenNote.hidden = true;
    quizStartBtn.hidden = true;
    quizLinkEl.hidden = true;
  }
  function applyQuizAccess() {
    if (!quizAccessKnown) return;
    var cfg = quizCfg || {};
    var hasTelegram = !!(cfg.quizUrl && /^https:\/\//.test(cfg.quizUrl));
    if (hasTelegram) {
      quizLinkEl.href = cfg.quizUrl;
      // When the quiz can run inside the portal, Telegram becomes the second choice.
      quizLinkEl.textContent = cfg.quizEmbed ? "Take it on Telegram instead" : "Take the quiz on Telegram";
    }
    quizGate.hidden = quizUnlocked;
    quizGateNote.textContent = quizJustFinished ? GATE_NOTE_DONE : GATE_NOTE;
    quizOpenNote.hidden = !quizUnlocked;
    quizStartBtn.hidden = !(quizUnlocked && cfg.quizEmbed && quizRun.hidden);
    quizLinkEl.hidden = !(quizUnlocked && hasTelegram);
  }

  function showQuiz(reg) {
    var body = document.getElementById("quizBody");
    body.replaceChildren(el("p", "live", "Loading your results..."));
    quizAccessKnown = false;
    hideQuizActions();
    if (!quizCfg) {
      fetch("/api/config").then(function (r) { return r.json(); }).then(function (c) { quizCfg = c; applyQuizAccess(); }).catch(function () {});
    }
    authFetch("/api/results")
      .then(function (r) { return r.json().then(function (b) { if (r.status === 401) { signedOut(); throw new Error("signed out"); } if (!r.ok) throw new Error(b.error); return b; }); })
      .then(function (d) {
        quizUnlocked = !!d.unlocked;
        quizAccessKnown = true;
        applyQuizAccess();
        body.replaceChildren();
        if (!d.latest) {
          body.appendChild(el("p", "quiz-empty", "You have not taken the quiz yet. Take it from this page and your result will appear here."));
          return;
        }
        var l = d.latest;
        var top = el("div", "quiz-top");
        top.appendChild(el("span", "grade-big g-" + l.grade, l.grade));
        var txt = el("div", "quiz-txt");
        txt.appendChild(el("strong", null, l.pct + "%  " + l.label));
        txt.appendChild(el("span", null, l.score + " of " + l.total + " correct"));
        txt.appendChild(el("span", "quiz-date", "Taken " + l.date + (d.attempts.length > 1 ? "  -  attempt " + l.attempt : "")));
        top.appendChild(txt);
        body.appendChild(top);
        if (d.best && d.best.attempt !== l.attempt) {
          body.appendChild(el("p", "quiz-best", "Best so far: " + d.best.pct + "% (grade " + d.best.grade + ")"));
        }
        if (d.attempts.length > 1) {
          var hist = el("ul", "quiz-hist");
          d.attempts.slice(0, 6).forEach(function (a) {
            var li = el("li");
            li.appendChild(el("span", null, a.date));
            li.appendChild(el("span", null, a.score + "/" + a.total + "  (" + a.pct + "%)"));
            li.appendChild(el("span", "grade g-" + a.grade, a.grade));
            hist.appendChild(li);
          });
          body.appendChild(hist);
        }
        var sc = el("p", "quiz-scale", "Grading: " + d.scale.map(function (s, i) {
          var hi = i === 0 ? 100 : d.scale[i - 1].min - 1;
          return s.grade + " " + s.min + "-" + hi;
        }).join("  |  "));
        body.appendChild(sc);
      })
      .catch(function (e) {
        if (e && e.message === "signed out") return;
        quizUnlocked = false;
        quizAccessKnown = true;
        applyQuizAccess(); // the code box still works: entering a code again is always safe
        body.replaceChildren(el("p", "quiz-empty", "We could not load your quiz results just now. Refresh to try again."));
      });
  }

  // ---- Take the quiz inside the portal ----
  function closeQuiz(silent) {
    if (!silent && !window.confirm("Close the quiz? A quiz you have not finished is not saved.")) return;
    quizFrame.src = "about:blank";
    quizRun.hidden = true;
    quizOrigin = "";
    quizJustFinished = false;
    quizUnlocked = false; // showQuiz asks the website again; nothing is offered until it answers
    quizAccessKnown = false;
    hideQuizActions();
    quizCodeInput.value = ""; // nothing typed here is left behind for the next person on this device
    quizCodeErr.textContent = "";
    if (!silent && currentReg) showQuiz(currentReg);
  }

  function openQuiz() {
    if (!currentReg) return;
    quizStartBtn.disabled = true;
    authFetch("/api/quiz/session")
      .then(function (r) { return r.json().then(function (b) { if (r.status === 401) { signedOut(); throw new Error(""); } return { ok: r.ok, body: b }; }); })
      .then(function (res) {
        if (!res.ok || !res.body.url) {
          var er = new Error(res.body.error || "We could not open the quiz just now.");
          er.locked = !!res.body.locked; // the quiz is locked (never unlocked, finished, or cancelled by an admin)
          throw er;
        }
        quizOrigin = new URL(res.body.url).origin;
        quizMsg.textContent = "Opening the quiz. The first load can take up to a minute.";
        quizMsg.hidden = false;
        document.getElementById("quizTab").href = res.body.url;
        quizFrame.src = res.body.url + "&o=" + encodeURIComponent(window.location.origin);
        quizRun.hidden = false;
        quizStartBtn.hidden = true;
        quizRun.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
      })
      .catch(function (e) {
        if (!e.message) return; // signed out: the sign-in form is already showing
        if (e.locked) { showQuiz(currentReg); } // back to the code box
        else quizStartBtn.hidden = false;
        window.alert(e.message || "We could not open the quiz just now.");
      })
      .then(function () { quizStartBtn.disabled = false; });
  }

  quizStartBtn.addEventListener("click", openQuiz);
  document.getElementById("quizCloseBtn").addEventListener("click", function () { closeQuiz(false); });
  quizFrame.addEventListener("load", function () {
    if (quizFrame.getAttribute("src") && quizFrame.getAttribute("src") !== "about:blank") quizMsg.hidden = true;
  });
  // The quiz page tells the portal when a result has been saved, so the results card refreshes by itself.
  window.addEventListener("message", function (e) {
    if (!quizOrigin || e.origin !== quizOrigin || e.source !== quizFrame.contentWindow) return;
    if (e.data && e.data.type === "abecfx_quiz_saved" && currentReg) {
      // The finished quiz is saved and its code is spent, so the quiz locks again. The frame stays open
      // so the student can read their result there until they close it.
      quizJustFinished = true;
      showQuiz(currentReg);
    }
  });

  // ---- Enter a one-time quiz code ----
  var quizUnlockForm = document.getElementById("quizUnlockForm");
  var quizCodeInput = document.getElementById("quizCode");
  var quizCodeErr = document.getElementById("quizCodeErr");
  var quizUnlockBtn = document.getElementById("quizUnlockBtn");
  quizUnlockForm.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    quizCodeErr.textContent = "";
    var code = quizCodeInput.value.trim();
    if (!code) { quizCodeErr.textContent = "Enter your quiz code."; quizCodeInput.focus(); return; }
    quizUnlockBtn.disabled = true;
    quizUnlockBtn.textContent = "Checking...";
    authFetch("/api/quiz/unlock", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ code: code }),
    })
      .then(function (r) { return r.json().then(function (b) { return { ok: r.ok, status: r.status, body: b }; }); })
      .then(function (res) {
        if (res.status === 401) { signedOut(); return; }
        // 409 means the quiz is already open (another tab or device): show it as open.
        if (!res.ok && res.status !== 409) { quizCodeErr.textContent = res.body.error || "Something went wrong. Please try again."; return; }
        quizCodeInput.value = "";
        quizJustFinished = false;
        showQuiz(currentReg);
      })
      .catch(function () { quizCodeErr.textContent = "Network problem. Check your connection and try again."; })
      .then(function () { quizUnlockBtn.disabled = false; quizUnlockBtn.textContent = "Unlock quiz"; });
  });

  form.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    err.textContent = "";
    var reg = input.value.trim();
    var email = emailInput.value.trim();
    if (!reg) { err.textContent = "Enter your registration number."; input.focus(); return; }
    if (!email) { err.textContent = "Enter the email address you registered with."; emailInput.focus(); return; }
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.textContent = "Signing in...";
    login(reg, email)
      .then(function (res) {
        if (!res.ok) { err.textContent = res.body.error || "Something went wrong. Please try again."; return; }
        remember(res.body.card.regNumber, res.body.token);
        emailInput.value = "";
        showHome(Object.assign({}, res.body.card, { photoChanged: res.body.photoChanged, dobNotice: res.body.dobNotice, signatureMissing: res.body.signatureMissing, photoRedo: res.body.photoRedo }));
      })
      .catch(function () { err.textContent = "Network problem. Check your connection and try again."; })
      .then(function () { btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = "Sign in"; });
  });

  var dlBtn = document.getElementById("downloadBtn");
  dlBtn.addEventListener("click", function () {
    var card = document.querySelector("#cardMount .idc");
    if (!card || dlBtn.disabled) return;
    var label = dlBtn.textContent;
    dlBtn.disabled = true;
    dlBtn.textContent = "Preparing...";
    window.downloadIdCard(card, currentReg).then(function () {
      dlBtn.textContent = label;
    }, function () {
      dlBtn.textContent = "Could not download. Try again";
      setTimeout(function () { dlBtn.textContent = label; }, 3500);
    }).then(function () { dlBtn.disabled = false; });
  });
  // ---- Certificate: shown under the ID card once the student is Certified, with PNG and PDF downloads ----
  var certBlock = document.getElementById("certBlock");
  var certMsg = document.getElementById("certMsg");
  var certNo = "";
  function loadCertificate() {
    certBlock.hidden = true;
    certMsg.textContent = "";
    authFetch("/api/portal/certificate").then(function (r) {
      return r.json().catch(function () { return {}; }).then(function (b) { return { ok: r.ok, body: b }; });
    }).then(function (res) {
      if (!res.ok || !res.body.eligible) return;
      certNo = res.body.certificate.certNo;
      document.getElementById("certMount").replaceChildren(
        window.renderCertificate(res.body.certificate, { signature: res.body.signature, origin: location.origin })
      );
      certBlock.hidden = false;
    }).catch(function () { /* no certificate shown; the ID card is unaffected */ });
  }
  [["certPng", "png"], ["certPdf", "pdf"]].forEach(function (x) {
    var b = document.getElementById(x[0]);
    b.addEventListener("click", function () {
      var frame = document.querySelector("#certMount .cert-frame");
      if (!frame || b.disabled) return;
      var label = b.textContent;
      b.disabled = true;
      b.textContent = "Preparing...";
      window.downloadCertificate(frame, certNo, x[1]).then(function () {
        b.textContent = label;
      }, function () {
        b.textContent = "Could not download. Try again";
        setTimeout(function () { b.textContent = label; }, 3500);
      }).then(function () { b.disabled = false; });
    });
  });

  var pendingSigNotice = false;
  var pendingPhotoNotice = false;
  // ---- Age requirement notice: a pop-up for a student whose date of birth on file is too recent ----
  var dobDlg = document.getElementById("dobDlg");
  var dobForm = document.getElementById("dobForm");
  var dobStart = document.getElementById("dobStart");
  var dobMsg = document.getElementById("dobMsg");
  var dobInput = document.getElementById("dobInput");
  function niceDate(iso) {
    var m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(iso || "");
    if (!m) return iso || "";
    var months = ["January", "February", "March", "April", "May", "June", "July", "August", "September", "October", "November", "December"];
    return Number(m[3]) + " " + months[Number(m[2]) - 1] + " " + m[1];
  }
  var lastDob = "";
  function showDobNotice(n) {
    if (n && n.dob) lastDob = n.dob; else if (n) n.dob = lastDob;
    if (!n) { if (dobDlg.open) dobDlg.close(); return; }
    if (sigNoticeDlg.open) sigNoticeDlg.close();
    if (photoNoticeDlg.open) photoNoticeDlg.close();
    var text = "The system has noticed that you do not meet our age requirements for registration. Your current date of birth is set as: " + niceDate(n.dob) + ".";
    document.getElementById("dobText").textContent = n.canUpdate
      ? text + " Please update it now to continue enjoying our services."
      : text + " You have already used your one update, so please contact your mentor to correct it.";
    document.getElementById("dobOpenForm").hidden = !n.canUpdate;
    dobStart.hidden = false;
    dobForm.hidden = true;
    dobMsg.textContent = "";
    dobInput.value = "";
    if (!dobDlg.open) { if (dobDlg.showModal) dobDlg.showModal(); else dobDlg.setAttribute("open", ""); }
  }
  // The notice cannot be dismissed with Escape: the student updates their date of birth (or signs out).
  dobDlg.addEventListener("cancel", function (e) { e.preventDefault(); });
  document.getElementById("dobOpenForm").addEventListener("click", function () {
    dobStart.hidden = true; dobForm.hidden = false; dobMsg.textContent = "";
    dobInput.focus();
  });
  document.getElementById("dobBack").addEventListener("click", function () {
    dobForm.hidden = true; dobStart.hidden = false; dobMsg.textContent = "";
  });
  document.getElementById("dobSignOut").addEventListener("click", function () {
    if (dobDlg.open) dobDlg.close();
    document.getElementById("switchBtn").click();
  });
  dobForm.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!dobInput.value) { dobMsg.textContent = "Please enter your date of birth."; return; }
    var save = document.getElementById("dobSave");
    save.disabled = true; dobMsg.textContent = "Saving...";
    authFetch("/api/portal/dob", {
      method: "PUT",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ dob: dobInput.value }),
    }).then(function (r) {
      return r.json().catch(function () { return {}; }).then(function (b) {
        if (r.status === 401) { if (dobDlg.open) dobDlg.close(); signedOut(b.error); return; }
        if (!r.ok) {
          dobMsg.textContent = b.error || "We could not save your date of birth. Please try again.";
          if (b.canUpdate === false) showDobNotice({ dob: "", canUpdate: false });
          return;
        }
        dobMsg.textContent = "";
        dobDlg.close();
        liveNote("Your date of birth has been updated.");
        nextNotice();
      });
    }).catch(function () { dobMsg.textContent = "Network problem. Check your connection and try again."; })
      .then(function () { save.disabled = false; });
  });
  function liveNote(t) { var l = document.getElementById("liveLine"); if (l) { l.textContent = t; l.className = "live"; } }

  // ---- Photo notice: a pop-up for a student a mentor has asked to send a new passport photograph ----
  var photoNoticeDlg = document.getElementById("photoDlg");
  var photoNoticeData = "";
  // The notices come one after another: date of birth, then photograph, then signature.
  function nextNotice() {
    if (dobDlg.open || photoNoticeDlg.open) return;
    if (pendingPhotoNotice) { showPhotoNotice(); return; }
    showSigNotice();
  }
  function showPhotoNotice() {
    document.getElementById("photoDlgStart").hidden = false;
    document.getElementById("photoDlgForm").hidden = true;
    document.getElementById("photoDlgMsg").textContent = "";
    photoNoticeData = "";
    if (!photoNoticeDlg.open) { if (photoNoticeDlg.showModal) photoNoticeDlg.showModal(); else photoNoticeDlg.setAttribute("open", ""); }
  }
  photoNoticeDlg.addEventListener("cancel", function (e) { e.preventDefault(); });
  document.getElementById("photoDlgSignOut").addEventListener("click", function () {
    if (photoNoticeDlg.open) photoNoticeDlg.close();
    document.getElementById("switchBtn").click();
  });
  var photoDlgFile = document.getElementById("photoDlgFile");
  photoDlgFile.addEventListener("change", function () {
    var f = photoDlgFile.files && photoDlgFile.files[0];
    if (!f) return;
    var msg = document.getElementById("photoDlgMsg");
    msg.textContent = "";
    window.abecPhoto.process(f).then(function (dataUrl) {
      photoNoticeData = dataUrl;
      document.getElementById("photoDlgPreview").src = dataUrl;
      document.getElementById("photoDlgStart").hidden = true;
      document.getElementById("photoDlgForm").hidden = false;
    }, function (e) {
      document.getElementById("photoDlgStart").hidden = true;
      document.getElementById("photoDlgForm").hidden = false;
      document.getElementById("photoDlgPreview").removeAttribute("src");
      photoNoticeData = "";
      msg.textContent = e && e.message ? e.message : "We could not use that photo. Try a different one.";
    }).then(function () { photoDlgFile.value = ""; });
  });
  document.getElementById("photoDlgSave").addEventListener("click", function () {
    var btn = document.getElementById("photoDlgSave"), msg = document.getElementById("photoDlgMsg");
    if (!photoNoticeData || !currentReg) { msg.textContent = "Choose a photo first."; return; }
    btn.disabled = true; msg.textContent = "Saving...";
    var dataUrl = photoNoticeData;
    authFetch("/api/portal/photo", {
      method: "PUT",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ photo: dataUrl.slice(dataUrl.indexOf(",") + 1) }),
    }).then(function (r) {
      return r.json().catch(function () { return {}; }).then(function (b) {
        if (r.status === 401) { if (photoNoticeDlg.open) photoNoticeDlg.close(); signedOut(b.error); return; }
        if (!r.ok) { msg.textContent = b.error || "We could not save your photo. Please try again."; return; }
        try { localStorage.setItem("abecfx_photo_v", String(Date.now())); } catch (e) {}
        pendingPhotoNotice = false;
        return myCard().then(function (res) {
          if (res.ok) document.getElementById("cardMount").replaceChildren(window.renderStudentCard(res.body, { photoSrc: dataUrl, signatureSrc: mySig }));
          setPhotoEditable(false);
          msg.textContent = "";
          photoNoticeDlg.close();
          liveNote("Your new passport photograph has been saved.");
          nextNotice();
        });
      });
    }).catch(function () { msg.textContent = "Network problem. Check your connection and try again."; })
      .then(function () { btn.disabled = false; });
  });

  // ---- Signature notice: a pop-up for a student who has no signature on file yet ----
  var sigNoticeDlg = document.getElementById("sigDlg");
  var sigNoticeWidget = null;
  function showSigNotice() {
    if (!pendingSigNotice || dobDlg.open || photoNoticeDlg.open) return;
    document.getElementById("sigDlgStart").hidden = false;
    document.getElementById("sigDlgForm").hidden = true;
    document.getElementById("sigDlgMsg").textContent = "";
    if (!sigNoticeDlg.open) { if (sigNoticeDlg.showModal) sigNoticeDlg.showModal(); else sigNoticeDlg.setAttribute("open", ""); }
  }
  sigNoticeDlg.addEventListener("cancel", function (e) { e.preventDefault(); });
  document.getElementById("sigDlgOpen").addEventListener("click", function () {
    if (!sigNoticeWidget) sigNoticeWidget = window.abecSignature.create(document.getElementById("sigDlgMount"));
    document.getElementById("sigDlgStart").hidden = true;
    document.getElementById("sigDlgForm").hidden = false;
  });
  document.getElementById("sigDlgBack").addEventListener("click", function () {
    document.getElementById("sigDlgForm").hidden = true;
    document.getElementById("sigDlgStart").hidden = false;
    document.getElementById("sigDlgMsg").textContent = "";
  });
  document.getElementById("sigDlgSignOut").addEventListener("click", function () {
    if (sigNoticeDlg.open) sigNoticeDlg.close();
    document.getElementById("switchBtn").click();
  });
  document.getElementById("sigDlgSave").addEventListener("click", function () {
    var btn = document.getElementById("sigDlgSave"), msg = document.getElementById("sigDlgMsg");
    if (!sigNoticeWidget || !currentReg) return;
    btn.disabled = true; msg.textContent = "Saving...";
    sigNoticeWidget.value().then(function (url) {
      if (!url) { msg.textContent = ""; return; }
      return authFetch("/api/portal/signature", {
        method: "PUT",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ signature: url.slice(url.indexOf(",") + 1) }),
      }).then(function (r) {
        return r.json().catch(function () { return {}; }).then(function (b) {
          if (r.status === 401) { if (sigNoticeDlg.open) sigNoticeDlg.close(); signedOut(b.error); return; }
          if (!r.ok) throw new Error(b.error || "We could not save your signature. Please try again.");
          mySig = url;
          pendingSigNotice = false;
          sigToggle.textContent = "Change my signature";
          return myCard().then(function (res) {
            if (res.ok) redrawCard(res.body);
            msg.textContent = "";
            sigNoticeDlg.close();
            sigNoticeWidget.clear();
            sigNote("Your signature is saved and now shows on your ID card.", true);
          });
        });
      });
    }).catch(function (e) { msg.textContent = e && e.message ? e.message : "Network problem. Check your connection and try again."; })
      .then(function () { btn.disabled = false; });
  });

  // ---- My signature: shown on the student's own card, drawn or uploaded, saved on the website ----
  var mySig = "";
  var sigToggle = document.getElementById("sigToggle");
  var sigPanel = document.getElementById("sigPanel");
  var sigSave = document.getElementById("sigSave");
  var sigMsg = document.getElementById("sigMsg");
  var sigWidget = null;
  function sigNote(text, ok) {
    sigMsg.textContent = text || "";
    sigMsg.style.color = ok ? "var(--violet, #5a1060)" : "";
  }
  function redrawCard(d) {
    document.getElementById("cardMount").replaceChildren(window.renderStudentCard(d, { photoSrc: photoUrl(d.regNumber), signatureSrc: mySig }));
  }
  function loadSignature(d) {
    sigPanel.hidden = true;
    sigToggle.textContent = "Add my signature";
    authFetch("/api/portal/signature").then(function (r) {
      return r.json().catch(function () { return {}; }).then(function (b) { return { ok: r.ok, body: b }; });
    }).then(function (res) {
      if (!res.ok || !res.body.signature || currentReg !== d.regNumber) return;
      mySig = "data:image/png;base64," + res.body.signature;
      sigToggle.textContent = "Change my signature";
      redrawCard(d);
    }).catch(function () { /* the card simply shows without a signature */ });
  }
  sigToggle.addEventListener("click", function () {
    if (sigPanel.hidden) {
      if (!sigWidget) sigWidget = window.abecSignature.create(document.getElementById("sigPortalMount"));
      sigPanel.hidden = false;
      sigToggle.textContent = "Cancel";
    } else {
      sigPanel.hidden = true;
      sigToggle.textContent = mySig ? "Change my signature" : "Add my signature";
      if (sigWidget) sigWidget.clear();
    }
    sigNote("");
  });
  sigSave.addEventListener("click", function () {
    if (!sigWidget || !currentReg) return;
    sigSave.disabled = true;
    sigNote("Saving...");
    sigWidget.value().then(function (url) {
      if (!url) { sigNote(""); return; }
      return authFetch("/api/portal/signature", {
        method: "PUT",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ signature: url.slice(url.indexOf(",") + 1) }),
      }).then(function (r) {
        return r.json().catch(function () { return {}; }).then(function (b) {
          if (r.status === 401) { signedOut(b.error); return; }
          if (!r.ok) throw new Error(b.error || "We could not save your signature. Please try again.");
          mySig = url;
          return myCard().then(function (res) {
            if (res.ok) redrawCard(res.body);
            sigPanel.hidden = true;
            sigToggle.textContent = "Change my signature";
            sigWidget.clear();
            sigNote("Your signature is saved and now shows on your ID card.", true);
          });
        });
      });
    }).catch(function (e) { sigNote(e && e.message ? e.message : "Network problem. Check your connection and try again."); })
      .then(function () { sigSave.disabled = false; });
  });

  // ---- Change my photo: shrink it in the browser, save it, then redraw the card with the new photo ----
  var newPhoto = document.getElementById("newPhoto");
  var newPhotoBtn = document.getElementById("newPhotoBtn");
  var photoMsg = document.getElementById("photoMsg");
  var photoBusy = false;
  // Each student can change their photo once. After that the button goes away.
  function setPhotoEditable(can) { newPhotoBtn.hidden = !can; newPhoto.disabled = !can; }
  function photoNote(text, ok) {
    photoMsg.textContent = text || "";
    photoMsg.style.color = ok ? "var(--violet, #5a1060)" : "";
  }
  newPhoto.addEventListener("change", function () {
    var file = newPhoto.files && newPhoto.files[0];
    if (!file || photoBusy || !currentReg) return;
    photoBusy = true;
    var label = newPhotoBtn.textContent;
    newPhotoBtn.textContent = "Saving...";
    photoNote("");
    window.abecPhoto.process(file)
      .then(function (dataUrl) {
        return authFetch("/api/portal/photo", {
          method: "PUT",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ photo: dataUrl.slice(dataUrl.indexOf(",") + 1) }),
        }).then(function (r) {
          return r.json().catch(function () { return {}; }).then(function (b) {
            if (r.status === 401) { signedOut(b.error); return; }
            if (r.status === 403 && b.photoChanged) { setPhotoEditable(false); throw new Error(b.error); }
            if (!r.ok) throw new Error(b.error || "We could not save your photo. Please try again.");
            try { localStorage.setItem("abecfx_photo_v", String(Date.now())); } catch (e) {}
            // Show the photo just chosen (the saved copy can be cached by the browser for a few minutes)
            return myCard().then(function (res) {
              if (res.ok) document.getElementById("cardMount").replaceChildren(window.renderStudentCard(res.body, { photoSrc: dataUrl, signatureSrc: mySig }));
              setPhotoEditable(false);
              photoNote("Your photo has been updated. A photo can only be changed once, so this is final.", true);
            });
          });
        });
      })
      .catch(function (e) { photoNote(e && e.message ? e.message : "Network problem. Check your connection and try again."); })
      .then(function () { photoBusy = false; newPhotoBtn.textContent = label; newPhoto.value = ""; });
  });

  document.getElementById("switchBtn").addEventListener("click", function () {
    closeQuiz(true);
    window.abecHandbook.reset(); window.abecWorkbook.reset();
    currentReg = "";
    mySig = "";
    currentView = "";
    if (location.hash) history.replaceState(null, "", location.pathname + location.search);
    forget();
    input.value = "";
    emailInput.value = "";
    showSignin("");
    input.focus();
  });

  // Returning student on this device: go straight to their portal if their sign-in is still valid.
  var reg = saved();
  if (token()) {
    myCard()
      .then(function (res) {
        if (res.ok) { showHome(res.body); return; }
        if (res.status === 401) { forget(); showSignin("Please sign in again."); input.value = reg; return; }
        showSignin("We could not open your portal just now. Please try again.");
        input.value = reg;
      })
      .catch(function () {
        showSignin("Network problem. Please check your connection and try again.");
        input.value = reg;
      });
  } else {
    showSignin("");
    if (reg) input.value = reg; // an earlier visit without a sign-in: ask for the email now
  }
})();
```

#### `public/handbook.js`

```js
// Handbook inside the student portal: chapter list, reader and learning-progress bar.
// Progress = chapters marked as read / total chapters, saved on the website (Google Sheet) for the
// signed-in student. portal.js calls setup() once with its sign-in helpers.
(function () {
  var api = null; // { authFetch(url, opts), signedOut(msg) } from portal.js
  var data = null; // { chapters, final, progress }
  var openId = null; // chapter being read, or null for the chapter list
  var mount = null;
  var busy = false;

  function el(tag, cls, text) {
    var e = document.createElement(tag);
    if (cls) e.className = cls;
    if (text != null) e.textContent = text;
    return e;
  }

  function readJson(r) {
    return r.json().then(function (b) {
      if (r.status === 401) { api.signedOut(); throw new Error("signed out"); }
      if (!r.ok) throw new Error(b.error || "Something went wrong. Please try again.");
      return b;
    });
  }

  // ---- Progress bar (also used by the small summary on the ID card page) ----
  function bar(percent) {
    var track = el("div", "pbar");
    track.setAttribute("role", "progressbar");
    track.setAttribute("aria-valuemin", "0");
    track.setAttribute("aria-valuemax", "100");
    track.setAttribute("aria-valuenow", String(percent));
    track.setAttribute("aria-label", "Learning progress");
    var fill = el("span", "pbar-fill");
    fill.style.width = percent + "%";
    track.appendChild(fill);
    return track;
  }

  function sub(p) {
    if (p.count === 0) return "You have not started yet. Open the handbook and read your first chapter.";
    if (p.count === p.total) return "You have read the whole handbook. Well done.";
    return p.count + " of " + p.total + " chapters read";
  }

  // Small summary on the ID card page.
  function renderJourney(p) {
    document.getElementById("journeyPct").textContent = p.percent + "%";
    document.getElementById("journeyFill").style.width = p.percent + "%";
    document.getElementById("journeySub").textContent = sub(p);
  }

  function loadSummary() {
    document.getElementById("journeySub").textContent = "Loading your progress...";
    return api.authFetch("/api/handbook/progress").then(readJson).then(renderJourney).catch(function (e) {
      if (e.message === "signed out") return;
      document.getElementById("journeyPct").textContent = "";
      document.getElementById("journeyFill").style.width = "0%";
      document.getElementById("journeySub").textContent = "Open the Handbook to see your progress.";
    });
  }

  // ---- Chapter content ----
  function renderBlock(b) {
    var node, i;
    switch (b.t) {
      case "p": return el("p", "hb-p", b.x);
      case "h": return el("h4", "hb-h", b.x);
      case "quote": return el("blockquote", "hb-quote", b.x);
      case "note": return el("p", "hb-note", b.x);
      case "ul":
      case "ol":
        node = el(b.t, "hb-list");
        for (i = 0; i < b.items.length; i++) node.appendChild(el("li", null, b.items[i]));
        return node;
      case "cards":
        node = el("div", "hb-cards");
        for (i = 0; i < b.items.length; i++) {
          var c = el("div", "hb-card");
          c.appendChild(el("strong", null, b.items[i].h));
          c.appendChild(el("span", null, b.items[i].x));
          node.appendChild(c);
        }
        return node;
      case "table":
        var wrap = el("div", "hb-tablewrap");
        var t = el("table", "hb-table");
        var thead = el("thead"), hr = el("tr");
        b.head.forEach(function (h) { hr.appendChild(el("th", null, h)); });
        thead.appendChild(hr);
        t.appendChild(thead);
        var tb = el("tbody");
        b.rows.forEach(function (r) {
          var tr = el("tr");
          r.forEach(function (cell) { tr.appendChild(el("td", null, cell)); });
          tb.appendChild(tr);
        });
        t.appendChild(tb);
        wrap.appendChild(t);
        return wrap;
    }
    return el("span");
  }

  function isRead(id) { return data.progress.read.indexOf(id) !== -1; }

  function openPdf(btn) {
    btn.disabled = true;
    api.authFetch("/api/handbook/pdf-link").then(readJson).then(function (b) {
      window.open(b.url, "_blank", "noopener");
    }).catch(function (e) {
      if (e.message !== "signed out") window.alert(e.message);
    }).then(function () { btn.disabled = false; });
  }

  function pdfButton(label) {
    var b = el("button", "btn ghost wide", label);
    b.type = "button";
    b.addEventListener("click", function () { openPdf(b); });
    return b;
  }

  // ---- Screens ----
  function header(p) {
    var box = el("section", "hb-progress");
    var top = el("div", "hb-progress-top");
    top.appendChild(el("strong", null, "Your learning journey"));
    top.appendChild(el("span", "hb-pct", p.percent + "%"));
    box.appendChild(top);
    box.appendChild(bar(p.percent));
    box.appendChild(el("p", "hb-progress-sub", sub(p)));
    return box;
  }

  function hasVideo(id) { return !!(data.videos && data.videos[id]); }

  // The video tutorial for a chapter (chapters 01-12). Until the editor adds one it says "coming soon".
  function videoBlock(id) {
    if (id === "00") return null;
    var box = el("section", "hb-video");
    box.appendChild(el("h3", null, "Video tutorial"));
    var vid = data.videos && data.videos[id];
    if (!vid || !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{11}$/.test(vid)) {
      box.appendChild(el("p", "hb-video-soon", "The video for this chapter is coming soon. Check back later."));
      return box;
    }
    var frame = el("div", "hb-video-frame");
    var f = document.createElement("iframe");
    f.src = "https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/" + vid + "?rel=0";
    f.title = "Video tutorial";
    f.loading = "lazy";
    f.allow = "accelerometer; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; fullscreen";
    f.setAttribute("allowfullscreen", "");
    f.setAttribute("referrerpolicy", "strict-origin-when-cross-origin");
    frame.appendChild(f);
    box.appendChild(frame);
    var a = el("a", "hb-video-link", "Not playing? Watch on YouTube");
    a.href = "https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=" + vid;
    a.target = "_blank";
    a.rel = "noopener noreferrer";
    box.appendChild(a);
    return box;
  }

  function renderList() {
    var p = data.progress;
    mount.replaceChildren();
    mount.appendChild(el("h2", null, "Handbook"));
    mount.appendChild(el("p", "lead", "The Edge Handbook your quiz comes from. Read each chapter, then mark it as read to move your progress bar."));
    mount.appendChild(header(p));

    var list = el("ol", "hb-chapters");
    data.chapters.forEach(function (c) {
      var li = el("li");
      var a = el("a", "hb-chapter" + (isRead(c.id) ? " is-read" : ""));
      a.href = "#/handbook/" + c.id;
      a.appendChild(el("span", "hb-num", isRead(c.id) ? "✓" : c.id));
      var txt = el("span", "hb-chapter-txt");
      txt.appendChild(el("strong", null, c.title));
      txt.appendChild(el("span", null, (isRead(c.id) ? "Read" : "Not read yet") + (hasVideo(c.id) ? " · Video tutorial" : "")));
      a.appendChild(txt);
      li.appendChild(a);
      list.appendChild(li);
    });
    mount.appendChild(list);

    if (p.count === p.total) {
      var fin = el("section", "hb-final");
      fin.appendChild(el("h3", null, data.final.title));
      data.final.paragraphs.forEach(function (t) { fin.appendChild(el("p", null, t)); });
      var q = el("a", "btn primary wide", "Take the quiz");
      q.href = "#/quiz";
      fin.appendChild(q);
      mount.appendChild(fin);
    }
    var foot = el("div", "hb-foot");
    foot.appendChild(el("p", "hb-foot-note", "The PDF also has the charts and the practice workbook pages."));
    foot.appendChild(pdfButton("Open the full handbook PDF"));
    mount.appendChild(foot);
  }

  function renderChapter(id, keepScroll) {
    var idx = -1;
    data.chapters.forEach(function (c, i) { if (c.id === id) idx = i; });
    if (idx < 0) { location.hash = "#/handbook"; return; }
    var c = data.chapters[idx];
    var p = data.progress;
    mount.replaceChildren();

    var back = el("a", "hb-back", "← All chapters");
    back.href = "#/handbook";
    mount.appendChild(back);

    var mini = el("div", "hb-mini");
    mini.appendChild(el("span", null, "Progress " + p.percent + "%"));
    mini.appendChild(bar(p.percent));
    mount.appendChild(mini);

    var art = el("article", "hb-article");
    art.appendChild(el("p", "hb-kicker", c.id === "00" ? "Introduction" : "Chapter " + c.id));
    art.appendChild(el("h2", null, c.title));
    art.appendChild(el("p", "hb-tagline", c.tagline));
    var vb = videoBlock(c.id);
    if (vb) art.appendChild(vb);
    c.blocks.forEach(function (b) { art.appendChild(renderBlock(b)); });
    mount.appendChild(art);

    var done = isRead(c.id);
    var markBtn = el("button", (done ? "btn ghost wide" : "btn primary wide") + " hb-mark", done ? "✓ Read. Mark as not read" : "Mark this chapter as read");
    markBtn.type = "button";
    var msg = el("p", "hb-msg");
    msg.setAttribute("role", "status");
    markBtn.addEventListener("click", function () { mark(c.id, !isRead(c.id), markBtn, msg); });
    mount.appendChild(markBtn);
    mount.appendChild(msg);

    var nav = el("div", "hb-pager");
    if (idx > 0) {
      var prev = el("a", "btn ghost", "← Previous");
      prev.href = "#/handbook/" + data.chapters[idx - 1].id;
      nav.appendChild(prev);
    } else nav.appendChild(el("span"));
    if (idx < data.chapters.length - 1) {
      var next = el("a", "btn ghost", "Next →");
      next.href = "#/handbook/" + data.chapters[idx + 1].id;
      nav.appendChild(next);
    }
    mount.appendChild(nav);
    mount.appendChild(pdfButton("Open the full handbook PDF"));
    if (!keepScroll) window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  }

  function render() {
    if (!data) return;
    if (openId) renderChapter(openId); else renderList();
  }

  // Save one chapter. The bar moves right away and goes back if the save fails.
  function mark(id, read, btn, msg) {
    if (busy) return;
    busy = true;
    btn.disabled = true;
    msg.textContent = "Saving...";
    api.authFetch("/api/handbook/progress", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ chapter: id, read: read }),
    }).then(readJson).then(function (p) {
      data.progress = p;
      renderJourney(p);
      busy = false;
      renderChapter(id, true);
      var again = document.querySelector("#hbMount .hb-mark");
      if (again) again.focus({ preventScroll: true });
      var m = document.querySelector("#hbMount .hb-msg");
      if (m) {
        m.textContent = read
          ? (p.count === p.total ? "Saved. You have read every chapter: 100% complete." : "Saved. Your progress is now " + p.percent + "%.")
          : "Saved. Your progress is now " + p.percent + "%.";
      }
    }).catch(function (e) {
      busy = false;
      if (e.message === "signed out") return;
      btn.disabled = false;
      msg.textContent = e.message || "We could not save that. Please try again.";
    });
  }

  function load() {
    mount.replaceChildren(el("p", "live", "Opening the handbook..."));
    return api.authFetch("/api/handbook").then(readJson).then(function (d) {
      data = d;
      renderJourney(d.progress);
      render();
    }).catch(function (e) {
      if (e.message === "signed out") return;
      var box = el("div");
      box.appendChild(el("p", "quiz-empty", e.message || "We could not open the handbook just now."));
      var retry = el("button", "btn ghost", "Try again");
      retry.type = "button";
      retry.addEventListener("click", load);
      box.appendChild(retry);
      mount.replaceChildren(box);
    });
  }

  window.abecHandbook = {
    setup: function (a) { api = a; mount = document.getElementById("hbMount"); },
    // Called by the portal each time the Handbook view is shown. id is a chapter id or "".
    show: function (id) {
      openId = id || null;
      if (data) render(); else load();
    },
    summary: function () { return loadSummary(); },
    reset: function () { data = null; openId = null; busy = false; if (mount) mount.replaceChildren(); },
  };
})();
```

<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8" />
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
  <title>ID Card | ABEC-FX</title>
  <meta name="description" content="Find an ABEC-FX ID card and check a member's live status using their registration number." />
  <meta name="theme-color" content="#5a1060" />
  <link rel="icon" type="image/png" href="/favicon.png" />
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="/styles.css" />
</head>
<body>
  <div class="stage">
    <aside class="panel">
      <a class="brandbar" href="/" aria-label="ABEC-FX home">
        <img src="/logo-white.png" alt="" />
        <span><span class="name">ABEC-FX</span><span class="tag">Adebass Base Edge Consult</span></span>
      </a>

      <div class="pitch">
        <h1>Find an ID card.</h1>
        <p>Enter a registration number, or scan the QR code on an ID card, to see a live status.</p>
      </div>

      <nav class="nav" aria-label="Main">
        <a href="/"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="3" y="5" width="18" height="14" rx="2.5"/><circle cx="9" cy="11" r="2"/><path d="M6.5 16c.6-1.4 1.6-2 2.5-2s1.9.6 2.5 2M14 10h4M14 13h3"/></svg><span>My portal</span></a>
        <a href="/register"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="10" cy="8" r="3.5"/><path d="M3.5 20c0-3.6 2.9-6 6.5-6M17 11v6M14 14h6"/></svg><span>Register</span></a>
        <a href="/status" aria-current="page"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="6.5"/><path d="M16 16l4.5 4.5"/></svg><span>Find a student</span></a>
      </nav>

      <div class="social" id="siteLinks" hidden></div>
    </aside>

    <main class="sheet">
      <div class="sheet-inner">
        <div class="field kind-pick">
          <label for="lookupKind">What do you want to check?</label>
          <select id="lookupKind">
            <option value="/status" selected>ID card</option>
            <option value="/certificate">Certificate</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <h2>ID card</h2>
        <p class="lead">Enter the registration number, for example ABECFX/26/0001.</p>

        <form id="lookupForm" novalidate>
          <div class="search">
            <div class="field">
              <label for="reg" class="sr-only">Registration number</label>
              <input id="reg" type="text" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="characters" spellcheck="false" placeholder="ABECFX/26/0001" required />
            </div>
            <button type="submit" id="lookupBtn" class="primary" style="width:auto">Find ID card</button>
          </div>

          <p class="live" id="liveLine" role="status"></p>

</body>
</html>
