#### `public/status.js`

```js
(function () {
  var form = document.getElementById("lookupForm");
  var input = document.getElementById("reg");
  var err = document.getElementById("lookupErr");
  var btn = document.getElementById("lookupBtn");
  var result = document.getElementById("result");
  var mount = document.getElementById("cardMount");
  var liveLine = document.getElementById("liveLine");
  var currentReg = "";

  document.getElementById("year").textContent = new Date().getFullYear();

  function show(d) {
    currentReg = d.regNumber;
    mount.replaceChildren(window.renderStudentCard(d));
    var t = new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" });
    var note = window.expiryNotice(d);
    liveLine.textContent = note ? note.text : "Live record, checked at " + t;
    liveLine.className = "live" + (note ? " live-" + note.level : "");
    var certLink = document.getElementById("certLink");
    certLink.hidden = d.status !== "Certified";
    certLink.href = "/certificate?reg=" + encodeURIComponent(d.regNumber);
    result.hidden = false;
    try { history.replaceState(null, "", "/status?reg=" + d.regNumber); } catch (e) {}
    if (window.matchMedia("(max-width: 900px)").matches) {
      result.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
    }
  }

  function search() {
    err.textContent = "";
    result.hidden = true;
    var reg = input.value.trim();
    if (!reg) {
      err.textContent = "Enter a registration number to search.";
      input.focus();
      return;
    }
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.textContent = "Searching...";
    fetch("/api/lookup?reg=" + encodeURIComponent(reg))
      .then(function (r) {
        return r.json().then(function (b) { return { ok: r.ok, body: b }; });
      })
      .then(function (res) {
        if (!res.ok) {
          err.textContent = res.body.error || "Something went wrong. Please try again.";
          return;
        }
        show(res.body);
      })
      .catch(function () {
        err.textContent = "Network problem. Check your connection and try again.";
      })
      .then(function () {
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.textContent = "Find ID card";
      });
  }

  form.addEventListener("submit", function (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    search();
  });

  document.getElementById("linkBtn").addEventListener("click", function () {
    var b = this;
    var link = location.origin + "/status?reg=" + currentReg;
    function done() {
      b.textContent = "Link copied";
      setTimeout(function () { b.textContent = "Copy link"; }, 1800);
    }
    if (navigator.clipboard) navigator.clipboard.writeText(link).then(done, done);
    else done();
  });

  // Opened from a QR code (or shared link): look the student up straight away.
  var pre = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get("reg");
  if (pre) {
    input.value = pre;
    search();
  }
})();
```

#### `public/card.js`

```js
/*
 * Student ID card renderer (shared by the registration success screen and the status page).
 * Needs qr.js loaded first. All text is set with textContent, never innerHTML.
 */
(function () {
  "use strict";

  var SVG_NS = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
  var stampCounter = 0;

  var STATUS_CLASS = {
    "Uncertified": "s-uncertified",
    "Certified": "s-certified",
    "Team Member": "s-team",
    "Alumni Member": "s-alumni"
  };

  function el(tag, cls, text) {
    var e = document.createElement(tag);
    if (cls) e.className = cls;
    if (text != null) e.textContent = text;
    return e;
  }
  function svgEl(tag, attrs) {
    var e = document.createElementNS(SVG_NS, tag);
    Object.keys(attrs || {}).forEach(function (k) { e.setAttribute(k, attrs[k]); });
    return e;
  }
  function initials(name) {
    var parts = String(name || "").trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    if (!parts.length) return "?";
    var a = parts[0].charAt(0), b = parts.length > 1 ? parts[parts.length - 1].charAt(0) : "";
    return (a + b).toUpperCase();
  }
  function emojiFlag(code) {
    if (!/^[a-z]{2}$/i.test(code || "")) return "";
    return String.fromCodePoint.apply(null, code.toUpperCase().split("").map(function (c) {
      return 0x1f1e6 + c.charCodeAt(0) - 65;
    }));
  }

  // The kind of card: only Uncertified and Certified people are students. Team members and alumni are not called students.
  function cardKind(status) {
    return status === "Team Member" ? "Team ID card" : status === "Alumni Member" ? "Alumni ID card" : "Student ID card";
  }

  // What the card calls a status. A Certified student shows as "Certified".
  function statusLabel(status) { return status; }

  // Round stamp: "Certified", "Team member" or "Alumni member".
  var STAMP_TOP = { "Certified": "CERTIFIED", "Team Member": "TEAM MEMBER", "Alumni Member": "ALUMNI MEMBER" };
  var STAMP_LABEL = { "Certified": "Certified student of ABEC-FX", "Team Member": "Team member of ABEC-FX", "Alumni Member": "Alumni member of ABEC-FX" };
  function buildStamp(status, dateText) {
    var top = STAMP_TOP[status] || "CERTIFIED";
    var label = STAMP_LABEL[status] || STAMP_LABEL.Certified;
    var id = "stamp" + ++stampCounter;

    var svg = svgEl("svg", { viewBox: "0 0 200 200", "class": "idc-stamp-svg", role: "img", "aria-label": label });

    var defs = svgEl("defs");
    defs.appendChild(svgEl("path", { id: id + "t", d: "M 100,100 m -67,0 a 67,67 0 1,1 134,0" }));
    defs.appendChild(svgEl("path", { id: id + "b", d: "M 100,100 m -78,0 a 78,78 0 0,0 156,0" }));
    svg.appendChild(defs);

    svg.appendChild(svgEl("circle", { cx: 100, cy: 100, r: 95, "class": "st-ring st-ring-thick" }));
    svg.appendChild(svgEl("circle", { cx: 100, cy: 100, r: 89, "class": "st-ring st-ring-thin" }));
    svg.appendChild(svgEl("circle", { cx: 100, cy: 100, r: 53, "class": "st-ring st-ring-thin" }));

    function arcText(pathId, str, size) {
      var t = svgEl("text", { "class": "st-arc", "font-size": size });
      var tp = svgEl("textPath", { href: "#" + pathId, startOffset: "50%", "text-anchor": "middle" });
      tp.setAttributeNS("http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink", "xlink:href", "#" + pathId);
      tp.textContent = str;
      t.appendChild(tp);
      return t;
    }
    svg.appendChild(arcText(id + "t", top, top.length > 20 ? 10.5 : top.length > 14 ? 12.5 : 13.5)); // long titles get a smaller size so they fit the ring
    svg.appendChild(arcText(id + "b", "ADEBASS BASE EDGE CONSULT", 10.5));

    var stars = svgEl("text", { x: 100, y: 83, "text-anchor": "middle", "class": "st-stars", "font-size": 10 });
    stars.textContent = "★ ★ ★";
    svg.appendChild(stars);

    var name = svgEl("text", { x: 100, y: 107, "text-anchor": "middle", "class": "st-name", "font-size": 20 });
    name.textContent = "ABEC-FX";
    svg.appendChild(name);

    svg.appendChild(svgEl("line", { x1: 72, y1: 115, x2: 128, y2: 115, "class": "st-line" }));

    var date = svgEl("text", { x: 100, y: 129, "text-anchor": "middle", "class": "st-date", "font-size": 9.5 });
    date.textContent = dateText ? String(dateText).toUpperCase() : "OFFICIAL";
    svg.appendChild(date);
    return svg;
  }

  // Text for the "expires" line on the card. Only an Uncertified ID has one: any other status shows no line at all.
  function expiryLine(d) {
    if (d.expiryKind === "none") return null;
    if (d.expiryKind === "date") {
      if (d.expired) return { text: "Expired " + d.expires, cls: "is-expired" };
      if (d.daysLeft === 0) return { text: "Expires today", cls: "is-soon" };
      return { text: "Expires " + d.expires, cls: d.daysLeft <= 14 ? "is-soon" : "" };
    }
    if (d.expiryKind === "unknown") return { text: "Expiry date not set", cls: "" };
    return null;
  }

  // Message shown under the card when an ID has expired or is about to.
  window.expiryNotice = function (d) {
    if (d.expiryKind !== "date") return null;
    if (d.expired) return { level: "bad", text: "This ID expired on " + d.expires + ". Speak to ABEC-FX to renew it." };
    if (d.daysLeft <= 14) {
      var when = d.daysLeft === 0 ? "today" : d.daysLeft === 1 ? "tomorrow" : "in " + d.daysLeft + " days";
      return { level: "soon", text: "This ID expires " + when + " (" + d.expires + ")." };
    }
    return null;
  };

  // The signature is stored as dark ink on see-through. On the card it is drawn in white, so recolour it here.
  function whiteInk(src, done) {
    var img = new Image();
    img.onload = function () {
      try {
        var c = document.createElement("canvas");
        c.width = img.naturalWidth; c.height = img.naturalHeight;
        var ctx = c.getContext("2d");
        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "source-in";
        ctx.fillStyle = "#fff";
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
        done(c.toDataURL("image/png"));
      } catch (e) { done(src); }
    };
    img.onerror = function () { done(src); };
    img.src = src;
  }

  /**
   * d: card data from /api/lookup or /api/register
   * opts.photoSrc: use this image instead of /api/photo (e.g. the just-taken photo)
   * opts.signatureSrc: the holder's signature picture (shown only on their own card)
   */
  window.renderStudentCard = function (d, opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    var status = d.status || "Uncertified";
    var card = el("article", "idc");
    card.setAttribute("data-status", status.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, "-"));
    card.setAttribute("aria-label", cardKind(status) + " for " + d.fullName);

    // ---- header
    var top = el("div", "idc-top");
    var brand = el("div", "idc-brand");
    var logo = el("img");
    logo.src = "/logo-white.png"; logo.alt = ""; logo.width = 30; logo.height = 23;
    brand.appendChild(logo);
    brand.appendChild(el("span", null, "ABEC-FX"));
    top.appendChild(brand);
    card.appendChild(top);
    card.appendChild(el("div", "idc-kind", cardKind(status))); // big banner: Student / Team / Alumni ID card

    // ---- passport photo + national flag + facts
    var hero = el("div", "idc-hero");
    var photoBox = el("div", "idc-photo");
    var fallback = el("span", "idc-initials", initials(d.fullName));
    fallback.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
    photoBox.appendChild(fallback);
    var photo = el("img", "idc-photo-img");
    photo.alt = "Passport photograph of " + d.fullName;
    photo.addEventListener("load", function () { photoBox.classList.add("has-photo"); });
    photo.addEventListener("error", function () { photo.remove(); });
    photo.src = opts.photoSrc || "/api/photo?reg=" + encodeURIComponent(d.regNumber);
    photoBox.appendChild(photo);

    if (d.countryCode) {
      var flagWrap = el("div", "idc-flag");
      var flag = el("img");
      flag.alt = (d.country || "National") + " flag";
      flag.src = "https://flagcdn.com/w80/" + d.countryCode + ".png";
      flag.srcset = "https://flagcdn.com/w80/" + d.countryCode + ".png 1x, https://flagcdn.com/w160/" + d.countryCode + ".png 2x";
      flag.addEventListener("error", function () {
        flagWrap.textContent = emojiFlag(d.countryCode);
        flagWrap.classList.add("idc-flag-emoji");
      });
      flagWrap.appendChild(flag);
      photoBox.appendChild(flagWrap);
    }
    hero.appendChild(photoBox);

    var facts = el("dl", "idc-facts");
    function fact(label, value) {
      var row = el("div");
      row.appendChild(el("dt", null, label));
      row.appendChild(el("dd", null, value || "Not recorded"));
      facts.appendChild(row);
    }
    fact("Country", d.country);
    if (d.state) fact("State", /^abuja federal capital territory$/i.test(d.state.trim()) ? "FCT Abuja" : d.state);
    fact("Gender", d.gender);
    if (status !== "Team Member") { // programme and admission year are student details
      fact("Programme", d.programme);
      fact("Admitted", d.admitted ? String(d.admitted) : "");
    } else {
      fact("Joined", d.admitted ? String(d.admitted) : "");
    }
    hero.appendChild(facts);
    card.appendChild(hero);

    // ---- name + registration number
    if (d.expired) {
      card.classList.add("idc-is-expired");
      var mark = el("div", "idc-expired-mark", "EXPIRED");
      mark.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
      card.appendChild(mark);
    }
    card.appendChild(el("h3", "idc-name", String(d.fullName || "").toUpperCase()));
    if (status === "Team Member" && d.teamRole) card.appendChild(el("p", "idc-roleline", d.teamRole));
    card.appendChild(el("p", "idc-reg", d.regNumber));

    // ---- status (+ stamp for certified graduates, alumni and the team)
    var row = el("div", "idc-statusrow");
    // A team member's card shows no status chip or "Since ..." line (the round team stamp says it); only students have a status to show.
    if (status !== "Team Member") {
      var col = el("div", "idc-statuscol");
      var chip = el("span", "status " + (STATUS_CLASS[status] || "s-uncertified"));
      chip.appendChild(el("span", null, statusLabel(status)));
      col.appendChild(chip);
      col.appendChild(el("span", "idc-since",
        status === "Uncertified" ? "Awaiting certification"
          : "Since " + (d.statusDate || "date not recorded")));
      row.appendChild(col);
    } else {
      row.classList.add("idc-statusrow-solo");
    }
    if (status === "Certified" || status === "Alumni Member" || status === "Team Member") {
      var stampWrap = el("div", "idc-stamp");
      stampWrap.appendChild(buildStamp(status, d.statusDate));
      row.appendChild(stampWrap);
    }
    card.appendChild(row);

    // ---- the student's own signature, white ink straight under "Since ..." (only on their own card,
    // never on the public find-a-student card)
    if (opts.signatureSrc) {
      var sigBox = el("div", "idc-sig");
      var sigImg = el("img");
      sigImg.alt = "Signature of " + d.fullName;
      sigImg.hidden = true;
      whiteInk(opts.signatureSrc, function (url) { sigImg.src = url; sigImg.hidden = false; });
      sigBox.appendChild(sigImg);
      sigBox.appendChild(el("span", null, "Holder's signature"));
      card.appendChild(sigBox);
    }

    // ---- QR code: opens this student's live status
    var url = (opts.origin || location.origin) + "/status?reg=" + d.regNumber;
    var verify = el("div", "idc-verify");
    var qr = el("div", "idc-qr");
    try { qr.appendChild(window.QR.toSvg(url, { border: 1 })); } catch (e) { qr.textContent = ""; }
    verify.appendChild(qr);
    var vt = el("div", "idc-vtext");
    vt.appendChild(el("strong", null, "Scan to check status"));
    vt.appendChild(el("span", null, "Opens this ID card's live record."));
    if (d.issued) vt.appendChild(el("span", "idc-issued", "Issued " + d.issued));
    var exp = expiryLine(d);
    if (exp) vt.appendChild(el("span", "idc-expiry " + exp.cls, exp.text));
    verify.appendChild(vt);
    card.appendChild(verify);

    var foot = el("div", "idc-foot");
    foot.appendChild(el("span", null, "Adebass Base Edge Consult"));
    card.appendChild(foot);
    return card;
  };
})();
```

#### `public/qr.js`

```js
/*
 * Minimal QR Code generator (byte mode, error correction level M, versions 1-10).
 * Written for the ABEC-FX ID card so the site needs no outside QR service.
 * Exposes: QR.encode(text) -> { size, modules[y][x] }  and  QR.toSvg(text, { border })
 */
(function () {
  "use strict";

  var EC_PER_BLOCK = [0, 10, 16, 26, 18, 24, 16, 18, 22, 22, 26]; // level M
  var NUM_BLOCKS = [0, 1, 1, 1, 2, 2, 4, 4, 4, 5, 5];             // level M
  var MAX_VERSION = 10;

  function rawModules(ver) {
    var r = (16 * ver + 128) * ver + 64;
    if (ver >= 2) {
      var n = Math.floor(ver / 7) + 2;
      r -= (25 * n - 10) * n - 55;
      if (ver >= 7) r -= 36;
    }
    return r;
  }
  function dataCodewords(ver) {
    return Math.floor(rawModules(ver) / 8) - EC_PER_BLOCK[ver] * NUM_BLOCKS[ver];
  }

  // ---- Reed-Solomon over GF(256), polynomial 0x11D ----
  function gfMul(x, y) {
    var z = 0;
    for (var i = 7; i >= 0; i--) {
      z = (z << 1) ^ ((z >>> 7) * 0x11d);
      z ^= ((y >>> i) & 1) * x;
    }
    return z;
  }
  function rsDivisor(degree) {
    var result = [], i, j;
    for (i = 0; i < degree - 1; i++) result.push(0);
    result.push(1);
    var root = 1;
    for (i = 0; i < degree; i++) {
      for (j = 0; j < result.length; j++) {
        result[j] = gfMul(result[j], root);
        if (j + 1 < result.length) result[j] ^= result[j + 1];
      }
      root = gfMul(root, 2);
    }
    return result;
  }
  function rsRemainder(data, divisor) {
    var result = divisor.map(function () { return 0; });
    data.forEach(function (b) {
      var factor = b ^ result.shift();
      result.push(0);
      divisor.forEach(function (coef, i) { result[i] ^= gfMul(coef, factor); });
    });
    return result;
  }

  function addEccAndInterleave(data, ver) {
    var numBlocks = NUM_BLOCKS[ver];
    var eccLen = EC_PER_BLOCK[ver];
    var raw = Math.floor(rawModules(ver) / 8);
    var numShort = numBlocks - (raw % numBlocks);
    var shortLen = Math.floor(raw / numBlocks);
    var blocks = [];
    var div = rsDivisor(eccLen);
    for (var i = 0, k = 0; i < numBlocks; i++) {
      var dat = data.slice(k, k + shortLen - eccLen + (i < numShort ? 0 : 1));
      k += dat.length;
      var ecc = rsRemainder(dat, div);
      if (i < numShort) dat.push(0);
      blocks.push(dat.concat(ecc));
    }
    var result = [];
    for (var c = 0; c < blocks[0].length; c++) {
      blocks.forEach(function (block, j) {
        if (c !== shortLen - eccLen || j >= numShort) result.push(block[c]);
      });
    }
    return result;
  }

  function utf8Bytes(str) {
    var s = unescape(encodeURIComponent(str)), out = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < s.length; i++) out.push(s.charCodeAt(i));
    return out;
  }
  function getBit(x, i) { return ((x >>> i) & 1) !== 0; }

  function encode(text) {
    var bytes = utf8Bytes(text);
    var ver, capBits;
    for (ver = 1; ver <= MAX_VERSION; ver++) {
      capBits = dataCodewords(ver) * 8;
      var ccBits = ver < 10 ? 8 : 16;
      if (4 + ccBits + bytes.length * 8 <= capBits) break;
    }
    if (ver > MAX_VERSION) throw new Error("QR text too long");

    var bits = [];
    function push(val, len) { for (var i = len - 1; i >= 0; i--) bits.push((val >>> i) & 1); }
    push(4, 4);
    push(bytes.length, ver < 10 ? 8 : 16);
    bytes.forEach(function (b) { push(b, 8); });
    push(0, Math.min(4, capBits - bits.length));
    push(0, (8 - (bits.length % 8)) % 8);
    for (var pad = 0xec; bits.length < capBits; pad ^= 0xec ^ 0x11) push(pad, 8);

    var data = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < bits.length; i += 8) {
      var v = 0;
      for (var j = 0; j < 8; j++) v = (v << 1) | bits[i + j];
      data.push(v);
    }
    var all = addEccAndInterleave(data, ver);

    var size = ver * 4 + 17;
    var modules = [], isFn = [];
    for (var y = 0; y < size; y++) {
      modules.push(new Array(size).fill(false));
      isFn.push(new Array(size).fill(false));
    }
    function setFn(x, y, dark) { modules[y][x] = dark; isFn[y][x] = true; }

    function drawFinder(cx, cy) {
      for (var dy = -4; dy <= 4; dy++) {
        for (var dx = -4; dx <= 4; dx++) {
          var dist = Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy));
          var xx = cx + dx, yy = cy + dy;
          if (xx >= 0 && xx < size && yy >= 0 && yy < size) setFn(xx, yy, dist !== 2 && dist !== 4);
        }
      }
    }
    function drawAlign(cx, cy) {
      for (var dy = -2; dy <= 2; dy++)
        for (var dx = -2; dx <= 2; dx++)
          setFn(cx + dx, cy + dy, Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy)) !== 1);
    }
    function alignPositions() {
      if (ver === 1) return [];
      var n = Math.floor(ver / 7) + 2;
      var step = Math.ceil((ver * 4 + 4) / (n * 2 - 2)) * 2;
      var res = [6];
      for (var pos = size - 7; res.length < n; pos -= step) res.splice(1, 0, pos);
      return res;
    }
    function drawFormat(mask) {
      var d = (0 << 3) | mask; // level M = 00
      var rem = d, i;
      for (i = 0; i < 10; i++) rem = (rem << 1) ^ ((rem >>> 9) * 0x537);
      var b = ((d << 10) | rem) ^ 0x5412;
      for (i = 0; i <= 5; i++) setFn(8, i, getBit(b, i));
      setFn(8, 7, getBit(b, 6));
      setFn(8, 8, getBit(b, 7));
      setFn(7, 8, getBit(b, 8));
      for (i = 9; i < 15; i++) setFn(14 - i, 8, getBit(b, i));
      for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) setFn(size - 1 - i, 8, getBit(b, i));
      for (i = 8; i < 15; i++) setFn(8, size - 15 + i, getBit(b, i));
      setFn(8, size - 8, true);
    }
    function drawVersion() {
      if (ver < 7) return;
      var rem = ver, i;
      for (i = 0; i < 12; i++) rem = (rem << 1) ^ ((rem >>> 11) * 0x1f25);
      var b = (ver << 12) | rem;
      for (i = 0; i < 18; i++) {
        var bit = getBit(b, i), a = size - 11 + (i % 3), c = Math.floor(i / 3);
        setFn(a, c, bit);
        setFn(c, a, bit);
      }
    }

    for (i = 0; i < size; i++) { setFn(6, i, i % 2 === 0); setFn(i, 6, i % 2 === 0); }
    drawFinder(3, 3); drawFinder(size - 4, 3); drawFinder(3, size - 4);
    var ap = alignPositions(), last = ap.length - 1;
    for (i = 0; i < ap.length; i++) {
      for (j = 0; j < ap.length; j++) {
        if ((i === 0 && j === 0) || (i === 0 && j === last) || (i === last && j === 0)) continue;
        drawAlign(ap[i], ap[j]);
      }
    }
    var j;
    drawFormat(0);
    drawVersion();

    // place codewords in the zig-zag order
    var n = 0;
    for (var right = size - 1; right >= 1; right -= 2) {
      if (right === 6) right = 5;
      for (var vert = 0; vert < size; vert++) {
        for (j = 0; j < 2; j++) {
          var x = right - j;
          var upward = ((right + 1) & 2) === 0;
          var yy = upward ? size - 1 - vert : vert;
          if (!isFn[yy][x] && n < all.length * 8) {
            modules[yy][x] = getBit(all[n >>> 3], 7 - (n & 7));
            n++;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    function applyMask(mask) {
      for (var y = 0; y < size; y++) {
        for (var x = 0; x < size; x++) {
          var inv;
          switch (mask) {
            case 0: inv = (x + y) % 2 === 0; break;
            case 1: inv = y % 2 === 0; break;
            case 2: inv = x % 3 === 0; break;
            case 3: inv = (x + y) % 3 === 0; break;
            case 4: inv = (Math.floor(x / 3) + Math.floor(y / 2)) % 2 === 0; break;
            case 5: inv = ((x * y) % 2) + ((x * y) % 3) === 0; break;
            case 6: inv = (((x * y) % 2) + ((x * y) % 3)) % 2 === 0; break;
            default: inv = (((x + y) % 2) + ((x * y) % 3)) % 2 === 0;
          }
          if (!isFn[y][x] && inv) modules[y][x] = !modules[y][x];
        }
      }
    }

    function penalty() {
      var score = 0, x, y, run, k, line;
      var dark = 0;
      for (y = 0; y < size; y++) for (x = 0; x < size; x++) if (modules[y][x]) dark++;
      function runs(get) {
        for (var a = 0; a < size; a++) {
          run = 1;
          for (var b = 1; b <= size; b++) {
            if (b < size && get(a, b) === get(a, b - 1)) run++;
            else { if (run >= 5) score += 3 + (run - 5); run = 1; }
          }
        }
      }
      runs(function (a, b) { return modules[a][b]; });
      runs(function (a, b) { return modules[b][a]; });
      for (y = 0; y < size - 1; y++)
        for (x = 0; x < size - 1; x++) {
          var c = modules[y][x];
          if (c === modules[y][x + 1] && c === modules[y + 1][x] && c === modules[y + 1][x + 1]) score += 3;
        }
      function finderLike(s) {
        var count = 0, idx = -1, pats = ["00001011101", "10111010000"];
        pats.forEach(function (p) {
          idx = s.indexOf(p);
          while (idx !== -1) { count++; idx = s.indexOf(p, idx + 1); }
        });
        return count;
      }
      for (y = 0; y < size; y++) {
        line = ""; for (x = 0; x < size; x++) line += modules[y][x] ? "1" : "0";
        score += 40 * finderLike(line);
      }
      for (x = 0; x < size; x++) {
        line = ""; for (y = 0; y < size; y++) line += modules[y][x] ? "1" : "0";
        score += 40 * finderLike(line);
      }
      k = Math.ceil(Math.abs(dark * 20 - size * size * 10) / (size * size)) - 1;
      score += Math.max(0, k) * 10;
      return score;
    }

    var best = 0, bestScore = Infinity, m;
    for (m = 0; m < 8; m++) {
      applyMask(m);
      drawFormat(m);
      var p = penalty();
      if (p < bestScore) { bestScore = p; best = m; }
      applyMask(m); // undo
    }
    applyMask(best);
    drawFormat(best);
    return { size: size, version: ver, mask: best, modules: modules };
  }

  function toSvg(text, opts) {
    var border = opts && opts.border != null ? opts.border : 2;
    var qr = encode(text);
    var ns = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";
    var d = "";
    for (var y = 0; y < qr.size; y++)
      for (var x = 0; x < qr.size; x++)
        if (qr.modules[y][x]) d += "M" + (x + border) + "," + (y + border) + "h1v1h-1z";
    var total = qr.size + border * 2;
    var svg = document.createElementNS(ns, "svg");
    svg.setAttribute("viewBox", "0 0 " + total + " " + total);
    svg.setAttribute("shape-rendering", "crispEdges");
    svg.setAttribute("role", "img");
    svg.setAttribute("aria-label", "QR code to check this student's status");
    var bg = document.createElementNS(ns, "rect");
    bg.setAttribute("width", total); bg.setAttribute("height", total); bg.setAttribute("fill", "#fff");
    var path = document.createElementNS(ns, "path");
    path.setAttribute("d", d); path.setAttribute("fill", "#14081a");
    svg.appendChild(bg); svg.appendChild(path);
    return svg;
  }

  var api = { encode: encode, toSvg: toSvg };
  if (typeof module !== "undefined" && module.exports) module.exports = api;
  else window.QR = api;
})();
```

#### `public/nav.js`

```js
// Main menu: once someone is registered / signed in on this device, "Register" is not needed there any more.
// (They signed in or registered if the portal's sign-in token is saved. Signing out brings the link back.)
(function () {
  function signedIn() {
    try { return !!localStorage.getItem("abecfx_token"); } catch (e) { return false; }
  }
  function refresh() {
    var hide = signedIn();
    var links = document.querySelectorAll('.nav a[href="/register"]');
    for (var i = 0; i < links.length; i++) {
      // never hide the page you are on, so the menu does not look empty of a current page
      links[i].hidden = hide && links[i].getAttribute("aria-current") !== "page";
    }
  }
  window.abecNav = { refresh: refresh };
  refresh();
})();
```

#### `public/admin.html`

```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8" />
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
  <meta name="robots" content="noindex, nofollow" />
  <title>Dashboard | ABEC-FX</title>
  <meta name="theme-color" content="#5a1060" />
  <link rel="icon" type="image/png" href="/favicon.png" />
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="/styles.css" />
  <link rel="stylesheet" href="/admin.css" />
</head>
<body class="adm">
  <header class="adm-top">
    <a class="adm-brand" href="/" aria-label="ABEC-FX home">
      <img src="/logo-white.png" alt="" width="34" height="26" />
      <span><b>ABEC-FX</b><i>Dashboard</i></span>
    </a>
    <div class="adm-actions" id="topActions" hidden>
      <span class="adm-stamp" id="stamp"></span>
      <span class="adm-who" id="who"></span>
      <button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="acctBtn" hidden>Change password</button>
      <button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="refreshBtn">Refresh</button>
      <button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="logoutBtn">Sign out</button>
    </div>
  </header>

  <main class="adm-main">
    <section id="login" class="adm-login">
      <h1>Dashboard sign in</h1>
      <p class="lead">Choose your username (Owner, Admin, Moderator...), then enter your password.</p>
      <form id="loginForm" novalidate>
        <div class="field">
          <label for="unSel">Username</label>
          <select id="unSel" aria-label="Choose your role" hidden></select>
          <input id="un" type="text" placeholder="Username" autocomplete="username" autocapitalize="none" spellcheck="false" hidden />
        </div>
        <div class="field">
          <label for="pw">Password</label>
          <input id="pw" type="password" autocomplete="current-password" required />
        </div>
        <p class="formErr" id="loginErr" role="alert"></p>
        <button type="submit" class="primary" id="loginBtn">Sign in</button>
      </form>
    </section>

    <section id="dash" hidden>
      <nav class="adm-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Dashboard sections">
        <button type="button" role="tab" id="tab-overview" aria-selected="true" aria-controls="view-overview">Statistics</button>
        <button type="button" role="tab" id="tab-quiz" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="view-quiz">Quiz results</button>
        <button type="button" role="tab" id="tab-quizcodes" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="view-quizcodes" hidden>Quiz codes</button>
        <button type="button" role="tab" id="tab-links" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="view-links">Site links</button>
        <button type="button" role="tab" id="tab-community" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="view-community" hidden>Community</button>
        <button type="button" role="tab" id="tab-videos" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="view-videos">Chapter videos</button>
        <button type="button" role="tab" id="tab-team" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="view-team" hidden>Team</button>
        <button type="button" role="tab" id="tab-idcards" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="view-idcards" hidden>ID cards</button>
        <button type="button" role="tab" id="tab-certificates" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="view-certificates" hidden>Certificates</button>
        <button type="button" role="tab" id="tab-database" aria-selected="false" aria-controls="view-database">Database</button>
      </nav>

      <p class="adm-hint" id="statsHint">Tap or click any number, bar or chart column to see the students behind it.</p>

      <div id="view-overview" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tab-overview">
        <div class="kpis" id="kpis"></div>

        <div class="grid">
          <article class="adm-card"><h3>Certification status</h3><div id="c-status"></div><p class="adm-note" id="c-status-note"></p></article>
          <article class="adm-card"><h3>Gender</h3><div id="c-gender"></div></article>
          <article class="adm-card"><h3>Programme enrolled</h3><div id="c-programme"></div></article>
          <article class="adm-card"><h3>Year joined</h3><div id="c-year"></div></article>
          <article class="adm-card"><h3>Country of residence</h3><div id="c-country"></div></article>
          <article class="adm-card"><h3>State of residence</h3><div id="c-state"></div></article>
        </div>

        <article class="adm-card wide">
          <div class="adm-card-head">
            <h3>Month joined</h3>
            <label class="adm-select">Year <select id="yearSel"></select></label>
          </div>
          <div id="c-monthyear"></div>
        </article>

        <article class="adm-card wide">
          <h3>Monthly student growth</h3>
          <p class="adm-note">Bars are new students each month; the line is the running total.</p>
          <div id="c-growth"></div>
          <div class="tablewrap"><table class="adm-table" id="growthTable"></table></div>
          <p class="adm-note" id="undatedNote"></p>
        </article>
      </div>

      <div id="view-quiz" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tab-quiz" hidden>
        <div class="kpis" id="quizKpis"></div>
        <div class="grid">
          <article class="adm-card"><h3>Grades (latest attempt per student)</h3><div id="c-grades"></div></article>
          <article class="adm-card"><h3>Grading scale</h3><div class="tablewrap"><table class="adm-table" id="c-scale"></table></div></article>
        </div>

        <article class="adm-card wide">
          <div class="adm-card-head">
            <h3>All results</h3>
            <button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="csvBtn">Download CSV</button>
          </div>
          <div class="filters">
            <input id="q" type="search" placeholder="Search name or reg number" aria-label="Search results" />
            <select id="gradeFilter" aria-label="Filter by grade"><option value="">All grades</option></select>
            <select id="viewMode" aria-label="Which attempts">
              <option value="all">Every attempt</option>
              <option value="latest">Latest per student</option>
              <option value="best">Best per student</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div class="tablewrap"><table class="adm-table" id="resultsTable"></table></div>
          <p class="adm-note" id="resultsNote"></p>
        </article>
      </div>

      <div id="view-quizcodes" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tab-quizcodes" hidden>
        <article class="adm-card wide">
          <h3>Make quiz codes</h3>
          <p class="adm-note">Students cannot open the quiz until they enter a one-time code. Make codes here and give them to your students. A code opens the quiz for one student and stays open until they finish it. After that the quiz locks again, and the next attempt needs a new code.</p>
          <form id="qcForm" class="lk-form" novalidate>
            <div class="field">
              <label for="qc-count">How many codes</label>
              <input id="qc-count" type="number" min="1" max="50" step="1" value="1" inputmode="numeric" />
              <p class="lk-err" id="qc-e-count" role="alert"></p>
            </div>
            <div class="field">
              <label for="qc-for">For one student only (optional)</label>
              <input id="qc-for" type="text" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="characters" spellcheck="false" placeholder="ABECFX/26/0001. Leave empty for a code any student can use" />
              <p class="lk-err" id="qc-e-for" role="alert"></p>
            </div>
            <div class="lk-bar">
              <button type="submit" class="primary" id="qcMake">Make codes</button>
              <span class="lk-msg" id="qcMsg" role="status"></span>
            </div>
          </form>
          <div class="qc-new" id="qcNew" hidden>
            <div class="adm-card-head">
              <h3>New codes</h3>
              <button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="qcCopyAll">Copy all</button>
            </div>
            <ul class="qc-list" id="qcNewList"></ul>
          </div>
        </article>

        <article class="adm-card wide">
          <div class="adm-card-head">
            <h3>All quiz codes</h3>
            <button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="qcReload">Reload</button>
          </div>
          <div class="filters">
            <input id="qcSearch" type="search" placeholder="Search code, student name or reg number" aria-label="Search quiz codes" />
            <select id="qcStatus" aria-label="Filter by status">
              <option value="">Every status</option>
              <option value="Unused">Unused</option>
              <option value="Unlocked">In use (quiz open)</option>
              <option value="Completed">Completed</option>
              <option value="Revoked">Cancelled</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div class="tablewrap"><table class="adm-table" id="qcTable"></table></div>
          <p class="adm-note" id="qcNote" role="status"></p>
        </article>
      </div>

      <div id="view-links" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tab-links" hidden>
        <article class="adm-card wide">
          <h3>Site links</h3>
          <p class="adm-note">These show as icons on the website (portal, register and find-a-student pages) and change as soon as you press Save. Leave a box empty to hide that icon. Web addresses must start with https://</p>
          <form id="linksForm" class="lk-form" novalidate></form>
          <div class="lk-bar">
            <button type="button" class="primary" id="linksSave">Save</button>
            <span class="lk-msg" id="linksMsg" role="status"></span>
          </div>
        </article>
      </div>

      <div id="view-community" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tab-community" hidden>
        <article class="adm-card wide">
          <h3>Student community</h3>
          <p class="adm-note">Students see these on the Community page of their portal, as "Open WhatsApp community" and "Open Telegram community". Paste the invite link of your group. A button only appears for a link you set; leave a box empty to hide it. Links must start with https://</p>
          <form id="communityForm" class="lk-form" novalidate></form>
          <div class="lk-bar">
            <button type="button" class="primary" id="communitySave">Save</button>
            <span class="lk-msg" id="communityMsg" role="status"></span>
          </div>
        </article>
      </div>

      <div id="view-videos" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tab-videos" hidden>
        <article class="adm-card wide">
          <h3>Chapter video tutorials</h3>
          <p class="adm-note">Paste the YouTube address of the video for each chapter. Students see it inside that chapter of the Handbook. A chapter with no address shows "coming soon". Unlisted videos work too, but they must allow embedding.</p>
          <form id="videosForm" class="lk-form" novalidate></form>
          <div class="lk-bar">
            <button type="button" class="primary" id="videosSave">Save</button>
            <span class="lk-msg" id="videosMsg" role="status"></span>
          </div>
        </article>
      </div>

      <div id="view-database" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="tab-database" hidden>
        <article class="adm-card wide">

Their name, their role and <b>the signature on their own ID card</b> are printed on every certificate automatically, so there is nothing to upload here. If they change the signature on their ID card, the certificates follow. The organisation name and CAC RC number print on every certificate too.</p>
          <form id="certForm" class="lk-form" novalidate>

            <div class="cert-signer" id="certSigner" aria-live="polite"></div>

            <div class="field" id="cert-w-certRc"><label for="cert-certRc">CAC RC number</label><input id="cert-certRc" type="text" maxlength="20" autocomplete="off" /><span class="hint">Printed as &ldquo;CAC RC: 9420199&rdquo;. Type the number only.</span><p class="lk-err" id="cert-e-certRc"></p></div>
            <div class="lk-bar">

          <div class="field" style="margin-top:12px;max-width:520px">
            <label for="certq">Search</label>

          <div class="tablewrap"><table class="adm-table" id="tmTable"></table></div>
          <p class="adm-note" id="tmNote" role="status"></p>

  <!-- A team member: add, change role / password, disable or remove -->

      <div class="lk-bar">

        <button type="button" class="btn ghost sh-del" id="tmRemove" hidden>Remove</button>

  </dialog>

  <!-- A role: name and access -->
  <dialog id="rlDlg" class="adm-drill sh-dlg" aria-labelledby="rlDlgTitle">

      <div class="drill-head">

      </div>
      <div class="lk-form sh-fields">

        <div class="field" id="rl-w-perms">

  <!-- Your own password -->
  <dialog id="pwDlg" class="adm-drill sh-dlg" aria-labelledby="pwDlgTitle">
    <form id="pwForm" class="drill-box" novalidate>
      <div class="drill-head">

  <!-- One record: add, edit or delete -->

      <div class="drill-head">
        <h2 id="shDlgTitle">Row</h2>

  <!-- The list behind whichever statistic was clicked -->

      <div class="tablewrap"><table class="adm-table" id="drillTable"></table></div>
      <p class="adm-note" id="drillNote"></p>

  </dialog>

  <script src="/qr.js"></script>
  <script src="/card.js"></script>
  <script src="/download.js"></script>
  <script src="/certificate-art.js"></script>
  <script src="/certificate.js"></script>
  <script src="/admin.js"></script>
</body>
</html>
```

#### `public/admin.js`

```js
(function () {
  "use strict";
  var SS_KEY = "abecfx_admin";
  var MONTH = ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"];
  var NS = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";

  var $ = function (id) { return document.getElementById(id); };
  var token = "";   // sign-in token (it says who you are; your role is checked by the server on every request)
  var me = null;    // { username, name, role, roleLabel, perms, canChangePassword }
  var stats = null;
  var results = [];
  var scale = [];
  // The Database view: meta = what each table allows, data = the table being looked at, row = the record open in the form.
  var sh = { meta: null, key: "students", data: null, page: 0, mode: "edit", row: null, flash: "" };
  var shSeq = 0;
  var SH_PAGE = 50;
  var qc = { codes: [], newCodes: [] }; // the Quiz codes tab: every code, and the ones just made

  function getSaved() { try { return sessionStorage.getItem(SS_KEY) || ""; } catch (e) { return ""; } }
  function save(v) { try { if (v) sessionStorage.setItem(SS_KEY, v); else sessionStorage.removeItem(SS_KEY); } catch (e) {} }

  function el(tag, cls, text) {
    var e = document.createElement(tag);
    if (cls) e.className = cls;
    if (text != null) e.textContent = text;
    return e;
  }
  function svg(tag, attrs) {
    var e = document.createElementNS(NS, tag);
    Object.keys(attrs || {}).forEach(function (k) { e.setAttribute(k, attrs[k]); });
    return e;
  }
  function pct(n, total) { return total ? Math.round((n / total) * 100) : 0; }

  // ---------- API ----------
  function api(path, method, body) {
    var opts = { method: method || "GET", headers: { Authorization: "Bearer " + token } };
    if (body) { opts.headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json"; opts.body = JSON.stringify(body); }
    return fetch(path, opts).then(function (r) {
      return r.json().then(function (b) {
        if (r.status === 401) { var e = new Error(b.error || "Please sign in again."); e.auth = true; throw e; }
        if (!r.ok) { var e2 = new Error(b.error || "Something went wrong."); e2.fields = b.errors; e2.status = r.status; throw e2; }
        return b;
      });
    });
  }

  function showLogin(msg) {
    // Leave no student details on the screen once signed out.
    shSeq++;
    sh.meta = null; sh.data = null; sh.row = null; sh.flash = "";
    if ($("shDlg").open) $("shDlg").close();
    $("shTable").replaceChildren();
    $("shSearch").value = "";
    me = null;
    setDashTitle();
    teamData = null;
    if ($("tmDlg").open) $("tmDlg").close();
    if ($("pwDlg").open) $("pwDlg").close();
    $("tmTable").replaceChildren();
    $("certMount").replaceChildren(); $("certList").replaceChildren(); $("certActions").hidden = true; certCurrent = null;
    if ($("rlDlg").open) $("rlDlg").close();
    $("rlTable").replaceChildren();
    $("qcTable").replaceChildren();
    $("qcNew").hidden = true;
    $("qcNewList").replaceChildren();
    qc.codes = [];
    $("dash").hidden = true;
    $("topActions").hidden = true;
    $("login").hidden = false;
    $("loginErr").textContent = msg || "";
  }

  function load() {
    return Promise.all([api("/api/admin/stats"), api("/api/admin/results"), api("/api/admin/me")]).then(function (res) {
      stats = res[0];
      results = res[1].results;
      scale = res[1].scale;
      me = res[2];
      save(token);
      applyRole();
      $("login").hidden = true;
      $("dash").hidden = false;
      $("topActions").hidden = false;
      $("stamp").textContent = "Updated " + stats.generatedAt;
      renderOverview();
      renderQuiz();
    });
  }

  // Show only what this role may use. (The server enforces it too; hiding is just so nobody meets a dead end.)
  var TAB_NEEDS = { overview: "view", quiz: "view", quizcodes: "quizcodes", links: "links", community: "community", videos: "videos", database: "database", team: "team", idcards: "team", certificates: "certificates" };
  function allowed(perm) { return !!(me && me.perms.indexOf(perm) >= 0); }
  // The header and tab title name the signed-in role: "Admin dashboard", "Moderator dashboard", ...
  function setDashTitle() {
    var name = me && me.roleLabel ? me.roleLabel + " dashboard" : "Dashboard";
    var tag = document.querySelector(".adm-brand i");
    if (tag) tag.textContent = name;
    document.title = name + " | ABEC-FX";
  }
  function applyRole() {
    setDashTitle();
    Object.keys(TAB_NEEDS).forEach(function (n) { $("tab-" + n).hidden = !allowed(TAB_NEEDS[n]); });
    $("who").textContent = me.name + " · " + me.roleLabel;
    $("acctBtn").hidden = !me.canChangePassword;
    // if the open tab is no longer allowed (a role was changed), go back to the overview
    var open = Object.keys(TAB_NEEDS).filter(function (n) { return !$("view-" + n).hidden; })[0];
    if (open && !allowed(TAB_NEEDS[open])) setTab("overview");
  }

  // ---------- Building blocks ----------
  function bars(container, items, opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    var total = opts.total != null ? opts.total : items.reduce(function (n, i) { return n + i.count; }, 0);
    var limit = opts.limit || 0;
    container.replaceChildren();
    if (!items.length) { container.appendChild(el("p", "empty", "No data yet.")); return; }
    var max = Math.max.apply(null, items.map(function (i) { return i.count; })) || 1;
    var list = el("div", "bars");
    container.appendChild(list);
    function draw(all) {
      list.replaceChildren();
      (all || !limit ? items : items.slice(0, limit)).forEach(function (i) {
        var row = el(opts.drill ? "button" : "div", "bar");
        if (opts.drill) {
          row.type = "button";
          row.setAttribute("aria-label", i.label + ": " + i.count + ". Show the list");
          row.addEventListener("click", function () { var d = opts.drill(i); openDrill(d.kind, d.value, row); });
        }
        row.appendChild(el("span", "lbl", i.label));
        row.title = i.label;
        var track = el("span", "track");
        var fill = el("span", "fill");
        fill.style.width = (i.count ? Math.max(2, (i.count / max) * 100) : 0) + "%";
        track.appendChild(fill);
        row.appendChild(track);
        var num = el("span", "num", String(i.count));
        num.appendChild(el("small", null, pct(i.count, total) + "%"));
        row.appendChild(num);
        list.appendChild(row);
      });
    }
    draw(false);
    if (limit && items.length > limit) {
      var more = el("button", "btn ghost more", "Show all " + items.length);
      more.type = "button";
      var open = false;
      more.addEventListener("click", function () {
        open = !open;
        draw(open);
        more.textContent = open ? "Show fewer" : "Show all " + items.length;
      });
      container.appendChild(more);
    }
  }

  // A headline number. With a drill target it is a button that opens the list behind it.
  function kpi(label, value, target) {
    var d = el(target ? "button" : "div", "kpi");
    if (target) {
      d.type = "button";
      d.setAttribute("aria-label", label + ": " + value + ". Show the list");
      d.addEventListener("click", function () { openDrill(target.kind, target.value, d); });
    }
    d.appendChild(el("b", null, String(value)));
    d.appendChild(el("span", null, label));
    if (target) d.appendChild(el("em", "kpi-cue", "View list \u203a"));
    return d;
  }

  // Make an SVG shape behave like a button (mouse, touch and keyboard).
  function svgButton(node, labelText, target) {
    node.setAttribute("tabindex", "0");
    node.setAttribute("role", "button");
    node.setAttribute("aria-label", labelText + ". Show the list");
    var go = function () { openDrill(target.kind, target.value, node); };
    node.addEventListener("click", go);
    node.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) { if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") { e.preventDefault(); go(); } });
    return node;
  }

  // Column chart; points = [{label, value}]
  function columns(container, points, color) {
    container.replaceChildren();
    var W = 720, H = 220, L = 30, B = 26, T = 14, R = 8;
    var max = Math.max.apply(null, points.map(function (p) { return p.value; }).concat([1]));
    var s = svg("svg", { viewBox: "0 0 " + W + " " + H, role: "img", "aria-label": "Column chart" });
    [0, 0.5, 1].forEach(function (f) {
      var y = T + (H - T - B) * (1 - f);
      s.appendChild(svg("line", { x1: L, x2: W - R, y1: y, y2: y, "class": "grid-line" }));
      var t = svg("text", { x: L - 6, y: y + 4, "text-anchor": "end" });
      t.textContent = Math.round(max * f);
      s.appendChild(t);
    });
    var slot = (W - L - R) / points.length;
    points.forEach(function (p, i) {
      var h = (H - T - B) * (p.value / max);
      var x = L + slot * i + slot * 0.18;
      var r = svg("rect", { x: x, y: H - B - h, width: slot * 0.64, height: h, rx: 4, "class": "bar-rect" });
      s.appendChild(r);
      if (p.drill) {
        var hit = svg("rect", { x: L + slot * i, y: T, width: slot, height: H - T - B, "class": "hit" });
        var tt = svg("title");
        tt.textContent = p.label + ": " + p.value + " (click for the list)";
        hit.appendChild(tt);
        s.appendChild(svgButton(hit, p.label + ": " + p.value, p.drill));
      }
      if (p.value) {
        var v = svg("text", { x: x + slot * 0.32, y: H - B - h - 4, "text-anchor": "middle" });
        v.textContent = p.value;
        s.appendChild(v);
      }
      var lab = svg("text", { x: x + slot * 0.32, y: H - 8, "text-anchor": "middle" });
      lab.textContent = p.label;
      s.appendChild(lab);
    });
    var wrap = el("div", "chart");
    wrap.appendChild(s);
    container.appendChild(wrap);
  }

  // Bars = new per month, line = running total
  function growthChart(container, months) {
    container.replaceChildren();
    if (!months.length) { container.appendChild(el("p", "empty", "No joining dates yet.")); return; }
    var W = 720, H = 260, L = 34, B = 30, T = 16, R = 34;
    var maxNew = Math.max.apply(null, months.map(function (m) { return m.new; }).concat([1]));
    var maxTot = Math.max.apply(null, months.map(function (m) { return m.total; }).concat([1]));
    var s = svg("svg", { viewBox: "0 0 " + W + " " + H, role: "img", "aria-label": "Monthly growth chart" });
    [0, 0.5, 1].forEach(function (f) {
      var y = T + (H - T - B) * (1 - f);
      s.appendChild(svg("line", { x1: L, x2: W - R, y1: y, y2: y, "class": "grid-line" }));
      var a = svg("text", { x: L - 6, y: y + 4, "text-anchor": "end" });
      a.textContent = Math.round(maxNew * f);
      s.appendChild(a);
      var b = svg("text", { x: W - R + 6, y: y + 4 });
      b.textContent = Math.round(maxTot * f);
      s.appendChild(b);
    });
    var slot = (W - L - R) / months.length;
    var step = Math.ceil(months.length / 12);
    var pts = [];
    months.forEach(function (m, i) {
      var h = (H - T - B) * (m.new / maxNew);
      var x = L + slot * i + slot * 0.15;
      var r = svg("rect", { x: x, y: H - B - h, width: slot * 0.7, height: h, rx: 3, "class": "bar-rect" });
      s.appendChild(r);
      var hit = svg("rect", { x: L + slot * i, y: T, width: slot, height: H - T - B, "class": "hit" });
      var tt = svg("title");
      tt.textContent = label(m.ym) + ": " + m.new + " new, " + m.total + " total (click for the students who joined)";
      hit.appendChild(tt);
      s.appendChild(svgButton(hit, label(m.ym) + ": " + m.new + " new, " + m.total + " total", { kind: "month", value: m.ym }));
      pts.push([x + slot * 0.35, T + (H - T - B) * (1 - m.total / maxTot)]);
      if (i % step === 0) {
        var t = svg("text", { x: x + slot * 0.35, y: H - 10, "text-anchor": "middle" });
        t.textContent = label(m.ym);
        s.appendChild(t);
      }
    });
    s.appendChild(svg("polyline", { points: pts.map(function (p) { return p.join(","); }).join(" "), "class": "line" }));
    pts.forEach(function (p, i) {
      var dot = svg("circle", { cx: p[0], cy: p[1], r: 6, "class": "dot" });
      var dt = svg("title");
      dt.textContent = label(months[i].ym) + ": " + months[i].total + " students in total (click for the list)";
      dot.appendChild(dt);
      s.appendChild(svgButton(dot, label(months[i].ym) + ": " + months[i].total + " students in total", { kind: "upto", value: months[i].ym }));
    });
    var wrap = el("div", "chart");
    wrap.appendChild(s);
    container.appendChild(wrap);
  }

  function label(ym) {
    var p = ym.split("-");
    return MONTH[Number(p[1]) - 1] + " " + p[0].slice(2);
  }

  function table(tbl, head, rows) {
    tbl.replaceChildren();
    var thead = el("thead"), tr = el("tr");
    head.forEach(function (h) { tr.appendChild(el("th", h.n ? "n" : "", h.t)); });
    thead.appendChild(tr);
    tbl.appendChild(thead);
    var tb = el("tbody");
    rows.forEach(function (r) {
      var row = el("tr");
      r.forEach(function (c, i) {
        var td = el("td", head[i].n ? "n" : "");
        if (c && c.node) td.appendChild(c.node); else td.textContent = c == null ? "" : String(c);
        row.appendChild(td);
      });
      tb.appendChild(row);
    });
    tbl.appendChild(tb);
  }

  // A table cell that opens a list.
  function linkCell(text, kind, value) {
    var b = el("button", "linkbtn", String(text));
    b.type = "button";
    b.addEventListener("click", function () { openDrill(kind, value, b); });
    return { node: b };
  }

  function gradeBadge(g) { return { node: el("span", "grade g-" + g, g) }; }

  // ---------- Overview ----------
  function renderOverview() {
    var s = stats;
    var k = $("kpis");
    k.replaceChildren(
      kpi("Total students", s.total, { kind: "all" }),
      kpi("Certified graduates", s.graduates, { kind: "graduates" }),
      kpi("Uncertified", s.uncertified, { kind: "uncertified" }),
      kpi("Team members", s.team, { kind: "team" }),
      kpi("Took the quiz", s.quiz.students, { kind: "quiz-students" }),
      kpi("Average quiz score", s.quiz.avgPct == null ? "-" : s.quiz.avgPct + "%", { kind: "quiz-avg" })
    );
    var by = function (kind) { return function (i) { return { kind: kind, value: i.label }; }; };
    bars($("c-status"), s.status.filter(function (x) { return x.count > 0; }), { total: s.total, drill: by("status") });
    $("c-status-note").textContent = "Certified graduates = Certified and Alumni Member.";
    bars($("c-gender"), s.gender, { drill: by("gender") });
    bars($("c-programme"), s.programme, { drill: by("programme") });
    bars($("c-year"), s.byYear, { drill: by("year") });
    bars($("c-country"), s.country, { limit: 10, drill: by("country") });
    bars($("c-state"), s.state, { limit: 10, drill: by("state") });

    var years = Object.keys(s.monthsByYear).sort();
    var sel = $("yearSel");
    var keep = sel.value;
    sel.replaceChildren();
    years.forEach(function (y) { var o = el("option", null, y); o.value = y; sel.appendChild(o); });
    if (years.length) sel.value = years.indexOf(keep) >= 0 ? keep : years[years.length - 1];
    drawMonthYear();

    growthChart($("c-growth"), s.months);
    var rows = s.months.slice().reverse().map(function (m) {
      var g = m.growthPct;
      var cell = g == null ? "-" : { node: el("span", g >= 0 ? "up" : "down", (g >= 0 ? "+" : "") + g + "%") };
      return [linkCell(label(m.ym), "month", m.ym), linkCell(m.new, "month", m.ym), linkCell(m.total, "upto", m.ym), cell];
    });
    table($("growthTable"), [{ t: "Month" }, { t: "New students", n: 1 }, { t: "Total", n: 1 }, { t: "Change vs last month", n: 1 }], rows);
    $("undatedNote").textContent = s.undated
      ? s.undated + " student(s) have no joining date and are left out of the month charts."
      : "";
    $("undatedNote").replaceChildren();
    if (s.undated) {
      $("undatedNote").appendChild(document.createTextNode(s.undated + " student(s) have no joining date and are left out of the month charts. "));
      $("undatedNote").appendChild(linkCell("Show them", "undated", "").node);
    }
  }

  function drawMonthYear() {
    var y = $("yearSel").value;
    var data = (stats.monthsByYear[y] || Array(12).fill(0));
    if (!y) { $("c-monthyear").replaceChildren(el("p", "empty", "No joining dates yet.")); return; }
    columns($("c-monthyear"), MONTH.map(function (m, i) {
      return { label: m, value: data[i], drill: { kind: "month", value: y + "-" + String(i + 1).padStart(2, "0") } };
    }));
  }

  // ---------- Quiz ----------
  function renderQuiz() {
    var q = stats.quiz;
    $("quizKpis").replaceChildren(
      kpi("Students who took the quiz", q.students, { kind: "quiz-students" }),
      kpi("Not yet taken", q.notTaken, { kind: "quiz-not-taken" }),
      kpi("Total attempts", q.attempts, { kind: "quiz-attempts" }),
      kpi("Average score", q.avgPct == null ? "-" : q.avgPct + "%", { kind: "quiz-avg" })
    );
    bars($("c-grades"), q.gradeDist.map(function (g) { return { label: g.grade + " - " + g.label, count: g.count, grade: g.grade }; }),
      { total: q.students, drill: function (i) { return { kind: "grade", value: i.grade }; } });
    table($("c-scale"), [{ t: "Grade" }, { t: "Score" }, { t: "Meaning" }],
      scale.map(function (s, i) {
        var hi = i === 0 ? 100 : scale[i - 1].min - 1;
        return [gradeBadge(s.grade), s.min + " - " + hi + "%", s.label];
      }));
    var gf = $("gradeFilter");
    if (gf.options.length <= 1) scale.forEach(function (s) { var o = el("option", null, "Grade " + s.grade); o.value = s.grade; gf.appendChild(o); });
    renderResults();
  }

  function filtered() {
    var term = $("q").value.trim().toLowerCase();
    var grade = $("gradeFilter").value;
    var mode = $("viewMode").value;
    var list = results;
    if (mode !== "all") {
      var map = new Map();
      // results is newest first
      list.forEach(function (r) {
        var cur = map.get(r.regNumber);
        if (mode === "latest") { if (!cur) map.set(r.regNumber, r); }
        else if (!cur || r.pct > cur.pct) map.set(r.regNumber, r);
      });
      list = Array.from(map.values());
    }
    return list.filter(function (r) {
      if (grade && r.grade !== grade) return false;
      if (term && (r.fullName + " " + r.regNumber).toLowerCase().indexOf(term) < 0) return false;
      return true;
    });
  }

  function renderResults() {
    var list = filtered();
    table($("resultsTable"),
      [{ t: "Date" }, { t: "Reg number" }, { t: "Name" }, { t: "Score", n: 1 }, { t: "Percent", n: 1 }, { t: "Grade" }, { t: "Attempt", n: 1 }],
      list.slice(0, 500).map(function (r) {
        return [r.date, r.regNumber, r.fullName, r.score + " / " + r.total, r.pct + "%", gradeBadge(r.grade), r.attempt];
      }));
    $("resultsNote").textContent = list.length
      ? "Showing " + Math.min(list.length, 500) + " of " + list.length + " result(s). Download the CSV for the full list."
      : "No quiz results match.";
  }

  function csvCell(v) {
    var s = String(v == null ? "" : v);
    if (/^[=+\-@\t\r]/.test(s)) s = "'" + s; // stop spreadsheets running names as formulas
    return /[",\n]/.test(s) ? '"' + s.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"' : s;
  }

  function downloadCsv() {
    var head = ["Date", "Reg Number", "Name", "Score", "Total", "Percent", "Grade", "Attempt"];
    var lines = [head.join(",")].concat(filtered().map(function (r) {
      return [r.date, r.regNumber, r.fullName, r.score, r.total, r.pct, r.grade, r.attempt].map(csvCell).join(",");
    }));
    var blob = new Blob(["﻿" + lines.join("\r\n")], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8" });
    var a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    a.download = "abecfx-quiz-results.csv";
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    a.remove();
    setTimeout(function () { URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href); }, 2000);
  }

  // ---------- The list behind a statistic ----------
  var dlg = $("drill");
  var drillData = null; // { title, type, rows, count, truncated }
  var drillSeq = 0;
  var STUDENT_COLS = [{ t: "Reg number" }, { t: "Name" }, { t: "Gender" }, { t: "Status" }, { t: "Country" }, { t: "State" }, { t: "Programme" }, { t: "Joined" }];
  var QUIZ_COLS = [{ t: "Date" }, { t: "Reg number" }, { t: "Name" }, { t: "Score", n: 1 }, { t: "Percent", n: 1 }, { t: "Grade" }, { t: "Attempt", n: 1 }];

  function openDrill(kind, value, trigger) {
    var seq = ++drillSeq;
    drillData = null;
    $("drillTitle").textContent = "Loading...";
    $("drillSummary").textContent = "";
    $("drillNote").textContent = "";
    $("drillSearch").value = "";
    $("drillTable").replaceChildren();
    $("drillCsv").disabled = true;
    if (!dlg.open) dlg.showModal();
    api("/api/admin/drill?kind=" + encodeURIComponent(kind) + "&value=" + encodeURIComponent(value || ""))
      .then(function (d) {
        if (seq !== drillSeq) return; // a newer click replaced this one
        drillData = d;
        $("drillTitle").textContent = d.title;
        $("drillCsv").disabled = !d.rows.length;
        renderDrill();
      })
      .catch(function (err) {
        if (seq !== drillSeq) return;
        if (err.auth) { dlg.close(); showLogin(err.message); return; }
        $("drillTitle").textContent = "Could not load this list";
        $("drillSummary").textContent = err.message;
      });
  }

  function rowText(r) { return Object.keys(r).map(function (k) { return r[k]; }).join(" ").toLowerCase(); }

  function drillRows() {
    if (!drillData) return [];
    var term = $("drillSearch").value.trim().toLowerCase();
    return term ? drillData.rows.filter(function (r) { return rowText(r).indexOf(term) >= 0; }) : drillData.rows;
  }

  function renderDrill() {
    if (!drillData) return;
    var d = drillData, list = drillRows(), isQuiz = d.type === "quiz";
    var unit = isQuiz ? "result" : "student";
    var summary = d.count + " " + unit + (d.count === 1 ? "" : "s");
    if (!isQuiz && stats && stats.total) summary += " (" + pct(d.count, stats.total) + "% of " + stats.total + " students)";
    if (isQuiz && d.count && /Average quiz score/.test(d.title)) {
      summary += ". Average: " + Math.round(d.rows.reduce(function (n, r) { return n + r.pct; }, 0) / d.rows.length) + "%";
    }
    $("drillSummary").textContent = summary + ".";
    if (isQuiz) {
      table($("drillTable"), QUIZ_COLS, list.slice(0, 500).map(function (r) {
        return [r.date, r.regNumber, r.fullName, r.score + " / " + r.total, r.pct + "%", gradeBadge(r.grade), r.attempt];
      }));
    } else {
      table($("drillTable"), STUDENT_COLS, list.slice(0, 500).map(function (r) {
        return [r.regNumber, r.fullName, r.gender, r.status, r.country, r.state, r.programme, r.joined];
      }));
    }
    var note = "";
    if (!d.rows.length) note = "Nothing to show here yet.";
    else if (!list.length) note = "No one in this list matches your search.";
    else if (list.length > 500) note = "Showing the first 500 of " + list.length + ". Download the CSV for the full list.";
    if (d.truncated) note += (note ? " " : "") + "The list is limited to " + d.rows.length + " rows.";
    $("drillNote").textContent = note;
  }

  function downloadDrillCsv() {
    if (!drillData) return;
    var isQuiz = drillData.type === "quiz";
    var head = isQuiz ? ["Date", "Reg Number", "Name", "Score", "Total", "Percent", "Grade", "Attempt"]
      : ["Reg Number", "Name", "Gender", "Status", "Country", "State", "Programme", "Joined"];
    var lines = [head.join(",")].concat(drillRows().map(function (r) {
      var cells = isQuiz ? [r.date, r.regNumber, r.fullName, r.score, r.total, r.pct, r.grade, r.attempt]
        : [r.regNumber, r.fullName, r.gender, r.status, r.country, r.state, r.programme, r.joined];
      return cells.map(csvCell).join(",");
    }));
    var blob = new Blob(["\ufeff" + lines.join("\r\n")], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8" });
    var a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    a.download = "abecfx-" + drillData.title.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "").slice(0, 50) + ".csv";
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    a.remove();
    setTimeout(function () { URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href); }, 2000);
  }

  $("drillClose").addEventListener("click", function () { dlg.close(); });
  $("drillSearch").addEventListener("input", renderDrill);
  $("drillCsv").addEventListener("click", downloadDrillCsv);
  // a click on the dimmed area (the dialog itself, not its content) closes it
  dlg.addEventListener("click", function (e) { if (e.target === dlg) dlg.close(); });
  dlg.addEventListener("close", function () { drillSeq++; drillData = null; });

  // ---------- Site links ----------
  var linkFields = [];
  // ---------- Quiz codes: make, list and cancel the one-time codes that unlock the quiz ----------
  var QC_LABEL = { Unused: "Unused", Unlocked: "In use", Completed: "Completed", Revoked: "Cancelled" };

  function copyText(text, done) {
    var ok = function () { if (done) done(true); };
    var fallback = function () {
      var t = el("textarea");
      t.value = text;
      t.setAttribute("readonly", "");
      t.style.position = "fixed";
      t.style.opacity = "0";
      document.body.appendChild(t);
      t.select();
      var good = false;
      try { good = document.execCommand("copy"); } catch (e) {}
      document.body.removeChild(t);
      if (done) done(good);
    };
    if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(ok, fallback);
    else fallback();
  }

  function showQcErrors(errs) {
    $("qc-e-count").textContent = (errs && errs.count) || "";
    $("qc-e-for").textContent = (errs && errs.forReg) || "";
  }

  function qcWho(reg, name) { return reg ? (name ? name + " (" + reg + ")" : reg) : ""; }

  function renderQcNew() {
    var list = $("qcNewList");
    list.replaceChildren();
    $("qcNew").hidden = !qc.newCodes.length;
    qc.newCodes.forEach(function (c) {
      var li = el("li");
      var left = el("div");
      left.appendChild(el("span", "qc-code", c.code));
      if (c.forReg) { left.appendChild(document.createElement("br")); left.appendChild(el("span", "qc-for", "Only for " + qcWho(c.forReg, c.forName))); }
      li.appendChild(left);
      var b = el("button", "btn ghost", "Copy");
      b.type = "button";
      b.addEventListener("click", function () {
        copyText(c.code, function (ok) { b.textContent = ok ? "Copied" : "Copy failed"; setTimeout(function () { b.textContent = "Copy"; }, 1500); });
      });
      li.appendChild(b);
      list.appendChild(li);
    });
  }

  function qcVisible() {
    var term = $("qcSearch").value.trim().toLowerCase();
    var st = $("qcStatus").value;
    return qc.codes.filter(function (c) {
      if (st && c.status !== st) return false;
      if (!term) return true;
      return [c.code, c.usedBy, c.usedByName, c.forReg, c.forName, c.createdBy].join(" ").toLowerCase().indexOf(term) >= 0;
    });
  }

  function renderQcTable() {
    var rows = qcVisible();
    var open = qc.codes.filter(function (c) { return c.status === "Unlocked"; }).length;
    var unused = qc.codes.filter(function (c) { return c.status === "Unused"; }).length;
    $("qcNote").textContent = qc.codes.length
      ? "Showing " + Math.min(rows.length, 500) + " of " + qc.codes.length + " codes. " + unused + " unused, " + open + " in use right now."
      : "No quiz codes yet. Make some above.";
    table($("qcTable"), [
      { t: "Code" }, { t: "Status" }, { t: "For" }, { t: "Used by" }, { t: "Unlocked at" }, { t: "Completed at" }, { t: "Made by" }, { t: "" },
    ], rows.slice(0, 500).map(function (c) {
      var badge = el("span", "qc-status qc-" + c.status, QC_LABEL[c.status] || c.status);
      var action = "";
      if (c.status === "Unused" || c.status === "Unlocked") {
        var b = el("button", "btn ghost sh-rowbtn sh-del", "Cancel");
        b.type = "button";
        b.addEventListener("click", function () { revokeQc(c, b); });
        action = { node: b };
      }
      var code = el("span", "qc-code", c.code);
      return [{ node: code }, { node: badge }, qcWho(c.forReg, c.forName) || "Any student", qcWho(c.usedBy, c.usedByName), c.unlockedAt, c.completedAt || (c.revoked ? "Cancelled: " + c.revoked : ""), c.createdBy, action];
    }));
  }

  function loadQuizCodes() {
    return api("/api/admin/quiz-codes").then(function (r) { qc.codes = r.codes; renderQcTable(); });
  }

  function revokeQc(c, btn) {
    var msg = c.status === "Unlocked"
      ? "This code is in use: " + qcWho(c.usedBy, c.usedByName) + " has the quiz open right now. Cancelling locks their quiz straight away, and a quiz they have not finished will not be saved. Cancel it?"
      : "Cancel code " + c.code + "? Nobody will be able to use it.";
    if (!window.confirm(msg)) return;
    btn.disabled = true;
    api("/api/admin/quiz-codes/revoke", "POST", { code: c.code })
      .then(function () { $("qcMsg").textContent = "Code " + c.code + " cancelled."; return loadQuizCodes(); })
      .catch(function (err) {
        if (err.auth) return showLogin(err.message);
        btn.disabled = false;
        $("qcMsg").textContent = err.message;
        loadQuizCodes().catch(function () {});
      });
  }

  function openQuizCodes() {
    $("qcNote").textContent = "Loading...";
    loadQuizCodes().catch(function (err) { if (err.auth) showLogin(err.message); else $("qcNote").textContent = err.message; });
  }

  $("qcForm").addEventListener("submit", function (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    showQcErrors(null);
    $("qcMsg").textContent = "";
    var b = $("qcMake");
    b.disabled = true;
    api("/api/admin/quiz-codes", "POST", { count: Number($("qc-count").value), forReg: $("qc-for").value })
      .then(function (r) {
        qc.newCodes = r.codes;
        renderQcNew();
        $("qcMsg").textContent = r.codes.length === 1 ? "1 code made." : r.codes.length + " codes made.";
        return loadQuizCodes();
      })
      .catch(function (err) {
        if (err.auth) return showLogin(err.message);
        showQcErrors(err.fields);
        $("qcMsg").textContent = err.fields ? "" : err.message;
      })
      .then(function () { b.disabled = false; });
  });
  $("qcCopyAll").addEventListener("click", function () {
    var b = $("qcCopyAll");
    copyText(qc.newCodes.map(function (c) { return c.code; }).join("\n"), function (ok) { b.textContent = ok ? "Copied" : "Copy failed"; setTimeout(function () { b.textContent = "Copy all"; }, 1500); });
  });
  $("qcReload").addEventListener("click", openQuizCodes);
  $("qcSearch").addEventListener("input", renderQcTable);
  $("qcStatus").addEventListener("change", renderQcTable);

  function renderLinks(fields, values) {
    linkFields = fields;
    renderFields($("linksForm"), fields, values);
  }
  // Builds the labelled boxes for a list of link fields (used by Site links and Community).
  function renderFields(form, fields, values) {
    form.replaceChildren();
    fields.forEach(function (f) {
      var wrap = el("div", "field");
      wrap.id = "lk-wrap-" + f.key;
      var label = el("label", null, f.label);
      label.setAttribute("for", "lk-" + f.key);
      var input;
      if (f.kind === "text") { input = el("textarea"); input.maxLength = f.max || 700; }
      else { input = el("input"); input.type = f.kind === "phone" ? "tel" : "url"; input.autocomplete = "off"; input.spellcheck = false; }
      input.id = "lk-" + f.key;
      input.placeholder = f.hint || "";
      input.value = values[f.key] || "";
      var err = el("p", "lk-err");
      err.id = "lk-err-" + f.key;
      err.setAttribute("role", "alert");
      wrap.appendChild(label); wrap.appendChild(input); wrap.appendChild(err);
      form.appendChild(wrap);
    });
  }
  function showLinkErrors(errs) {
    linkFields.forEach(function (f) {
      var msg = (errs && errs[f.key]) || "";
      $("lk-err-" + f.key).textContent = msg;
      $("lk-wrap-" + f.key).classList.toggle("invalid", !!msg);
    });
  }
  // ---------- Student community (WhatsApp and Telegram groups) ----------
  var communityFields = [];
  function showCommunityErrors(errs) {
    communityFields.forEach(function (f) {
      var msg = (errs && errs[f.key]) || "";
      $("lk-err-" + f.key).textContent = msg;
      $("lk-wrap-" + f.key).classList.toggle("invalid", !!msg);
    });
  }
  function loadCommunity() {
    return api("/api/admin/community").then(function (r) {
      communityFields = r.fields;
      renderFields($("communityForm"), r.fields, r.values);
      $("communityMsg").textContent = "";
    });
  }
  $("communitySave").addEventListener("click", function () {
    var b = this, body = {};
    communityFields.forEach(function (f) { body[f.key] = $("lk-" + f.key).value; });
    b.disabled = true;
    $("communityMsg").textContent = "Saving...";
    showCommunityErrors(null);
    api("/api/admin/community", "PUT", body)
      .then(function (r) { renderFields($("communityForm"), communityFields, r.values); $("communityMsg").textContent = "Saved. Students now see these groups on their Community page."; })
      .catch(function (err) {
        if (err.auth) { showLogin(err.message); return; }
        showCommunityErrors(err.fields);
        $("communityMsg").textContent = err.message;
      })
      .then(function () { b.disabled = false; });
  });

  function loadLinks() {
    return api("/api/admin/links").then(function (r) { renderLinks(r.fields, r.values); $("linksMsg").textContent = ""; });
  }
  $("linksSave").addEventListener("click", function () {
    var b = this, body = {};
    linkFields.forEach(function (f) { body[f.key] = $("lk-" + f.key).value; });
    b.disabled = true;
    $("linksMsg").textContent = "Saving...";
    showLinkErrors(null);
    api("/api/admin/links", "PUT", body)
      .then(function (r) { renderLinks(linkFields, r.values); $("linksMsg").textContent = "Saved. The website now shows these links."; })
      .catch(function (err) {
        if (err.auth) { showLogin(err.message); return; }
        showLinkErrors(err.fields);
        $("linksMsg").textContent = err.message;
      })
      .then(function () { b.disabled = false; });
  });

  // ---------- Chapter videos ----------
  var videoChapters = [];
  function renderVideos(chapters, values) {
    videoChapters = chapters;
    var form = $("videosForm");
    form.replaceChildren();
    chapters.forEach(function (c) {
      var wrap = el("div", "field");
      wrap.id = "vd-wrap-" + c.id;
      var label = el("label", null, "Chapter " + c.id + ": " + c.title);
      label.setAttribute("for", "vd-" + c.id);
      var input = el("input");
      input.type = "url"; input.autocomplete = "off"; input.spellcheck = false;
      input.id = "vd-" + c.id;
      input.placeholder = "https://youtu.be/...";
      input.value = values[c.id] || "";
      var err = el("p", "lk-err");
      err.id = "vd-err-" + c.id;
      err.setAttribute("role", "alert");
      wrap.appendChild(label); wrap.appendChild(input); wrap.appendChild(err);
      form.appendChild(wrap);
    });
  }
  function showVideoErrors(errs) {
    videoChapters.forEach(function (c) {
      var msg = (errs && errs[c.id]) || "";
      $("vd-err-" + c.id).textContent = msg;
      $("vd-wrap-" + c.id).classList.toggle("invalid", !!msg);
    });
  }
  function loadVideos() {
    return api("/api/admin/videos").then(function (r) { renderVideos(r.chapters, r.values); $("videosMsg").textContent = ""; });
  }
  $("videosSave").addEventListener("click", function () {
    var b = this, body = {};
    videoChapters.forEach(function (c) { body[c.id] = $("vd-" + c.id).value; });
    b.disabled = true;
    $("videosMsg").textContent = "Saving...";
    showVideoErrors(null);
    api("/api/admin/videos", "PUT", body)
      .then(function (r) { renderVideos(videoChapters, r.values); $("videosMsg").textContent = "Saved. Students see these videos in the handbook."; })
      .catch(function (err) {
        if (err.auth) { showLogin(err.message); return; }
        showVideoErrors(err.fields);
        $("videosMsg").textContent = err.message;
      })
      .then(function () { b.disabled = false; });
  });

  // ---------- Database: browse, add, edit and delete the records ----------
  // The server decides what each sheet allows (sh.data.editable, canDelete, add) and checks every change again.
  var shDlg = $("shDlg");

  function loadSheetMeta() {
    return api("/api/admin/database").then(function (m) {
      sh.meta = m;
      var open = $("shOpen");
      var ok = typeof m.sheetUrl === "string" && m.sheetUrl.indexOf("https://docs.google.com/") === 0;
      open.hidden = !ok;
      if (ok) open.href = m.sheetUrl;
      renderPicker();
    });
  }

  function renderPicker() {
    var box = $("shPicker");
    box.replaceChildren();
    ((sh.meta && sh.meta.sheets) || []).forEach(function (s) {
      var b = el("button", "sh-pick", s.label);
      b.type = "button";
      b.setAttribute("aria-pressed", s.key === sh.key ? "true" : "false");
      b.addEventListener("click", function () { $("shSearch").value = ""; loadSheet(s.key); });
      box.appendChild(b);
    });
  }

  function loadSheet(key) {
    sh.key = key;
    renderPicker();
    $("shNote").textContent = "Loading...";
    var seq = ++shSeq;
    return api("/api/admin/database/" + encodeURIComponent(key)).then(function (d) {
      if (seq !== shSeq) return; // a newer click replaced this one
      sh.data = d;
      sh.page = 0;
      renderSheet();
    }).catch(function (err) {
      if (seq !== shSeq) return;
      if (err.auth) { showLogin(err.message); return; }
      sh.data = null;
      $("shTable").replaceChildren();
      $("shNote").textContent = err.message;
    });
  }

  function shVisibleRows() {
    var d = sh.data;
    if (!d) return [];
    var rows = d.rows.slice();
    if (d.newestFirst) rows.reverse();
    var term = $("shSearch").value.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (term) rows = rows.filter(function (r) { return r.cells.join(" ").toLowerCase().indexOf(term) >= 0; });
    return rows;
  }

  function renderSheet() {
    var d = sh.data;
    if (!d) return;
    $("shHelp").textContent = d.help;
    $("shAdd").hidden = !d.add;
    if (d.add) $("shAdd").textContent = d.add.label;
    var rows = shVisibleRows();
    var pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(rows.length / SH_PAGE));
    if (sh.page >= pages) sh.page = pages - 1;
    var slice = rows.slice(sh.page * SH_PAGE, (sh.page + 1) * SH_PAGE);
    var canOpen = d.editable.length > 0 || d.canDelete;
    var head = [{ t: "Row", n: 1 }].concat(canOpen ? [{ t: "Actions" }] : []).concat(d.headers.map(function (h) { return { t: h }; }));
    table($("shTable"), head, slice.map(function (r) {
      var first = r.n;
      var lead = [];
      if (canOpen) {
        var b = el("button", "linkbtn", String(r.n));
        b.type = "button";
        b.setAttribute("aria-label", (d.editable.length ? "Edit" : "Open") + " row " + r.n);
        b.addEventListener("click", function () { openRow(r); });
        first = { node: b };
        // visible buttons, right after the row number so they never scroll out of sight
        var box = el("span", "sh-actions");
        var eb = el("button", "btn ghost sh-rowbtn", d.editable.length ? "Edit" : "Open");
        eb.type = "button";
        eb.setAttribute("aria-label", (d.editable.length ? "Edit" : "Open") + " record " + r.n);
        eb.addEventListener("click", function () { openRow(r); });
        box.appendChild(eb);
        if (d.canDelete) {
          var db = el("button", "btn ghost sh-rowbtn sh-del", "Delete");
          db.type = "button";
          db.setAttribute("aria-label", "Delete record " + r.n);
          db.addEventListener("click", function () { deleteRow(r, db); });
          box.appendChild(db);
        }
        lead = [{ node: box }];
      }
      return [first].concat(lead).concat(r.cells.map(function (c) {
```
